# Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot

ברטנורא על משנה כתובות

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Nashim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 383)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:1)


**בְּתוּלָה נִשֵּׂאת לַיּוֹם רְבִיעִי.** וְלֹא בְלֵיל חֲמִישִׁי, דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא יִהְיֶה טָרוּד בַּנִּשּׂוּאִים וְלֹא מָצֵי בָּעֵיל:


בתולה נשאת ליום רביעי – but not on Thursday night, for one would suspect that he would be excited/troubled with the marriage [ceremony] and he would not be able to engage in intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:2)


**שֶׁאִם יֵשׁ לוֹ טַעֲנַת בְּתוּלִים.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁיֹּאמַר בָּעַלְתִּי וְלֹא מָצָאתִי דָם, לֹא שְׁנָא אִם הִיא קְטַנָּה אוֹ נַעֲרָה אוֹ בּוֹגֶרֶת. אוֹ פֶּתַח פָּתוּחַ מָצָאתִי, אִם הִיא קְטַנָּה אוֹ נַעֲרָה, אֲבָל בּוֹגֶרֶת אֵין לָהּ טַעֲנַת פֶּתַח פָּתוּחַ:


שמא יש לו טענת בתולים – such that he [i.e., the husband] would say, I engaged in sexual intercourse but I did not find blood; it made no difference whether she was a minor or a young maiden or an adult, or whether I found an absence of virginity [in her]. If she was a minor or a young maiden , but if she was an adult, there can be no claim of the absence of virginity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:1:3)


**יַשְׁכִּים וְיָבֹא לְבֵית דִּין.** בְּעוֹד כַּעֲסוֹ עָלָיו, שֶׁמָּא יִתְפַּיֵּס וְתִתְקָרֵר דַּעְתּוֹ וִיקַיְּמֶנָּה, וְשֶׁמָּא זִנְּתָה תַּחְתָּיו וַהֲרֵי הִיא אֲסוּרָה עָלָיו. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ וְתִנָּשֵׂא בְּאֶחָד בְּשַׁבָּת וְאִם יֶשׁ לוֹ טַעֲנַת בְּתוּלִים יַשְׁכִּים בְּיוֹם שֵׁנִי לְבֵית דִּין. וּמְשָׁנֵי, שָׁקְדוּ חֲכָמִים עַל תַּקָּנַת בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיְּהֵא טוֹרֵחַ בַּסְּעֻדָּה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים, אֶחָד בְּשַׁבָּת וְשֵׁנִי בְשַׁבָּת וּשְׁלִישִׁי בְשַׁבָּת, וּבִרְבִיעִי כּוֹנְסָהּ. וּבִזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בָּתֵּי דִינִין יוֹשְׁבִים בְּשֵׁנִי וּבַחֲמִישִׁי, אִשָּׁה נִשֵּׂאת בְּכָל יוֹם, וְהוּא שֶׁטָּרַח בַּסְּעֻדָּה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים. וּמִפְּנֵי תַּקָּנַת עֲנִיִּים נוֹהֲגִים בִּקְצָת מְקוֹמוֹת לִשָּׂא בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת. וְטַעֲמָא דְּאַלְמָנָה לְיוֹם חֲמִישִׁי, שָׁקְדוּ חֲכָמִים עַל תַּקָּנַת בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׂמֵחַ עִמָּהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים, חֲמִישִׁי בְשַׁבָּת וְשִׁשִּׁי בְשַׁבָּת וְשַׁבָּת. וְהַנּוֹשֵׂא אֶת הָאַלְמָנָה אָסוּר בַּעֲשִׂיַּת מְלָאכָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים. וְהַנּוֹשֵׂא אֶת הַבְּתוּלָה כָּל שִׁבְעָה. וּלְעִנְיַן בִּרְכַּת חֲתָנִים, בֵּין בָּחוּר שֶׁנָּשָׂא אַלְמָנָה בֵּין אַלְמָן שֶׁנָּשָׂא בְּתוּלָה מְבָרְכִים בִּרְכַּת חֲתָנִים כָּל שִׁבְעָה, וְאַלְמָן שֶׁנָּשָׂא אַלְמָנָה, יוֹם אֶחָד:


ישכים ויבא לב"ד – all the while his anger is upon him, lest he become appeased and be pacified and sustain her and lest she invite unfaithfulness against him and she became forbidden to him. , and the Gemara raises the question , if she should get married on Sunday and if he has a claim [against her] concerning her virginity , he should get up early on Monday morning to go to the Jewish court. And they responded that the Sages were considerate towards the daughters of Israel (in ordaining weddings to be held on the fourth day of the week) that he should be engaged in preparations for the [wedding] meal for three days, Sunday Monday and Tuesday and on Wednesday, he would consummate the marriage. And at a time when the Jewish courts would not be in session on Mondays and Thursdays, a woman may be married on any day, but that he should be engaged in the preparations for the [wedding] meal for three days. And because of the ordinance for the poor, we have the practice in some places to marry on Fridays. And the reason that a widow [would marry] on Thursdays, is because the Sages were considerate towards the daughters of Israel that he should be happy with her for three days: Thurday, Friday and Shabbat. And a person who marries a widow it is prohibited to engage in work for three days, but one who marries a virgin [is prohibited to engage in work] all seven days. And regarding the wedding blessings [in the Birkat HaMazon/the blessing following the meal], it makes no difference whether a young man married a widow or whether a widower married a virgin, we recite the wedding blessings all seven days. But a widower who married a widow, [recites them] one day.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:2:1)


**בְּתוּלָה אַלְמָנָה גְּרוּשָׁה.** כְּלוֹמַר, בְּתוּלָה שֶׁהִיא אַלְמָנָה אוֹ גְרוּשָׁה אוֹ חֲלוּצָה מִן הָאֵרוּסִין, וְחָזְרָה וְנִשֵּׂאת, כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִן הַשֵּׁנִי מָאתַיִם:


בתולה אלמה גרושה – that is to say, a virgin who is a widow or divorcee or a woman who was released from leviratical marriage [by a halitzah] by the brother of now-deceased betrothed, and she went back and married [another], her Ketubah/marriage contract from the second husband is two hundred [zuz].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:2:2)


**וְיֵשׁ לָהּ טַעֲנַת בְּתוּלִים.** אִם לֹא מָצָא לָהּ הַשֵּׁנִי בְּתוּלִים, אִבְּדָה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, דְּמֶקַּח טָעוּת הוּא שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּחֶזְקַת בְּתוּלָה נְשָׂאָהּ:


ויש לה טענת בתולים – if the second [husband] did not find her to be a virgin, she has lost her Ketubah, for it is a mistaken purchase as there was a presumption of her being a virgin when she married.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:1)


**שֶׁבָּא עַל הַקְּטַנָּה.** פְּחוּתָה מִבַּת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים וְיוֹם אֶחָד, שֶׁאֵין בִּיאָתָהּ בִּיאָה:


שבא על הקטנה – [a minor girl who is] less than three years and one day [old], for her coition is not coition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:2)


**וְקָטָן.** פָּחוֹת מִבֶּן תֵּשַׁע, שֶׁאֵין בִּיאָתוֹ בִּיאָה, וּבָא עַל הַגְּדוֹלָה:


וקטן – [a minor boy who is] less than nine [years of age] as his coition is not coition if he engages in sexual relations with an adult women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:3)


**וּמֻכַּת עֵץ.** שֶׁנִּתְקַע לָהּ עֵץ בְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם:


ומוכת עץ – if [piece of wood] had been inserted in her at that same place


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:4)


**כְּתֻבָּתָן מָאתַיִם.** אִם נִשְּׂאוּ סְתָם:


כתובתן מאתים – it they simply married in an undefined manner


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:3:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים מֻכַּת עֵץ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מָנֶה.** וְהִלְכָתָא כְּוָתַיְהוּ. וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא הִכִּיר בָּהּ, כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מָנֶה, וְלֹא הֲוֵי מֶקַּח טָעוּת:


וחכמים אומרים: מוכת עץ כתובתה מנה – And the Halakha is according to the Sages, and even if he did not know her, her Ketubah is a Maneh, and it is not considered an errant purchase.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:4:1)


**בְּתוּלָה שֶׁהִיא אַלְמָנָה אוֹ גְרוּשָׁה אוֹ חֲלוּצָה מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִים.** אִם חָזְרָה וְנִשֵּׂאת סְתָם, כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מָנֶה, דִּבְחֶזְקַת בְּעוּלָה קָיְמָא מִשֶּׁנִּכְנְסָה לַחֻפָּה:


בתולה שהיא אלמנה או גרושה או חלוצה מן הנשואים – if she went back and got married in an undefined manner, her Ketubah is a Maneh, for she is under the presumption [of being] no longer a virgin, we confirm , when she enters the marriage canopy.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:5:1)


**הָאוֹכֵל אֵצֶל חָמִיו בִּיהוּדָה.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂין סְעֻדַּת אֵרוּסִין בְּבֵית אֲבִי הַכַּלָּה בִּיהוּדָה, הָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים שֶׁהָאָרוּס מִתְיַחֵד עִם אֲרוּסָתוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא לִבּוֹ גַּס בָּהּ, לְפִיכָךְ כְּשֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת אַחַר כָּךְ אֵין לוֹ טַעֲנַת בְּתוּלִים:


האוכל אצל חמיו ביהודה – When they were making the festive meal [celebrating her] betrothal in the house of the bride’s father in Judea, it was the practice that the betrothing [male] would be alone with his betrothed [female] in order that he may become bold towards her (i.e., become intimate). Therefore, when she gets married after that, there is no claim of [her not being] a virgin.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:1)


**וְנִסְתַּחֲפָה שָׂדֶךָ.** מָטָר סוֹחֵף בָּא עַל שָׂדְךָ וְהִפְסִידָהּ, כְּלוֹמַר מַזָּלְךָ גָּרַם וְתֶן לִי כְּתֻבָּתִי:


ונסתחפה שדך – an inundating rain has come upon your field ( which is a euphemism) and ruined it, that is to say, your luck has caused this- and you [must] give me my Ketubah-value.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:2)


**וְהָיָה מִקָּחִי מֶקַּח טָעוּת.** וְאֵין לָךְ כְּתֻבָּה מִמֶּנִּי:


והיה מקחי מקח טעות – and you don’t have a Ketubah [settlement] from me.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:6:3)


**נֶאֱמֶנֶת.** וּמַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ שְׁבוּעַת הֶסֵּת שֶׁכֵּן הוּא כִדְבָרֶיהָ, וְתִטֹּל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה. וְאִי אָמְרָה בְּתוּלָה מְצָאָנִי, אֵינָהּ נֶאֱמֶנֶת, דְּהֵימְנוּהוּ רַבָּנָן לַבַּעַל וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּלֹא שְׁבוּעָה, דַּחֲזָקָה אֵין אָדָם טוֹרֵחַ בִּסְעֻדָּה וּמַפְסִידָהּ:


נאמנת – and we make her take an equitable oath (i.e., one who is sued for a debt denies the latter entirely) that such is according to her words and she takes her Ketubah-value. And such is the Halakha. And if she said: “You found me to be a virgin,” she is not believed, but the Rabbis found the husband to be trustworthy and even without an oath, for it is presumption that a husband will not busy himself with the [wedding] meal and cause her a loss.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:7:1)


**דְּרוּסַת אִישׁ.** בְּעוּלַת אִישׁ:


דרוסת איש – she had sexual relations with a man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:7:2)


**נֶאֱמֶנֶת.** וְלֹא הִפְסִידָה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם אִי אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן הִיא וּלְאַחַר זְמַן הָאֵרוּסִין כְּשֶׁנִּשְּׂאָה טָעַן דְּרוּסַת אִישׁ אַתְּ, מַפְּקִינַן לָהּ מִנֵּיהּ, דְּשֶׁמָּא לְאַחַר שֶׁאֵרַס אוֹתָהּ נִבְעֲלָה, וּמִכִּי אָמַר דְּרוּסַת אִישׁ אַתְּ שַׁוְיַהּ אַנַּפְשֵׁיהּ חֲתִיכָה דְאִסּוּרָא. וַאֲפִלּוּ נִבְעֲלָה בְּאֹנֶס, הָא קַיְמָא לָן אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן שֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה אֲסוּרָה לְבַעֲלָהּ. וּמְגָרֵשׁ לָהּ וְיָהֵיב לָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


נאמנת – and she does not lose her Ketubah-payment. But nevertheless, if she was the wife of a Kohen and following the time of her betrothal, when she married, he claimed that “you had engaged in sexual relations with a man” we remove her from him, lest after he had betrothed her, she had had sexual relations with [another] man, since he said to her, “you had sexual relations with a man,” she is considered to him as a piece [of meat] that is forbidden, and even if by uncontrollable accident had engaged in sexual relations, we establish for ourselves that if she is the wife of a Kohen who had by uncontrollable accident [had sexual relations with another man], she is forbidden to her husband, and he must divorce her and provide her Ketubah-settlement.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:1)


**מְדַבֶּרֶת.** מִתְיַחֶדֶת:


מדברת – engaging in sexual relations with


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:2)


**וְכֹהֵן הוּא.** מְיֻחָס:


וכהן הוא – of traceable genealogy, distinguished birth/well-connected


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:8:3)


**נֶאֱמֶנֶת.** כְּשֵׁרָה לַכְּהֻנָּה:


נאמנת – and she is fit to marry into the priesthood.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:9:1)


**מַה טִּיבוֹ שֶׁל עֻבָּר זֶה וְכוּ'.** רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר נֶאֱמֶנֶת, וּכְשֵׁרִים לַכְּהֻנָּה הִיא וּבִתָּהּ. אֲבָל לְעִנְיַן יְרוּשָׁה לֹא אָמַר רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל שֶׁזֶּה הָעֻבָּר יִירָשֶׁנּוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל בְּכָל הָנֵי בָּבֵי דְמַתְנִיתִין. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָכִי לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא תִנָּשֵׂא לַכְּהֻנָּה לֹא הַמְדַבֶּרֶת עִם אֶחָד וְלֹא הַמְעֻבֶּרֶת, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיוּ רֹב אַנְשֵׁי הָעִיר מְיֻחָסִים וְנִבְעֲלָה זֹאת הָאִשָּׁה מֵאֶחָד מֵהֶם שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ חוּץ לָעִיר וּבָעַל, דְּאָמְרִינַן כָּל דְּפָרֵישׁ מֵרֻבָּא פָּרֵישׁ, וְאָז תִּנָּשֵׂא לְכַתְּחִלָּה לַכְּהֻנָּה בֵּין הִיא בֵּין בִּתָּהּ:


מה טיבו של עובר זה וכו' – Rabban Gamaliel states that she is to be believed and she and are daughter are fit to [be married into] the priesthood. But [concerning] the matter of inheritance, Rabban Gamaliel did not state that this fetus should inherit him, and the Halakha is according to Rabban Gamaliel in all the sections of our Mishnah. And even so, ab initio, she should not marry into the priesthood – neither one who speaks (i.e., one who has had sexual relationships) with one person and not the pregnant, other than if most of the people of the city are of traceable genealogy and this particular woman was impregnated from one of them who had separated himself outside of the city and had sexual relations [with her], for we say: “whatever comes out of a mixed multitude is presumed to have come from the majority “(i.e., has the legal status of the majority -see Talmud Ketubot 15a and Zevahim 73a), and so, she may marry into the priesthood whether she or her daughter.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/1:10:1)


**אִם רֹב הָעִיר מַשִּׂיאִין לַכְּהֻנָּה.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּרֻבָּא חַד סַגִּי בְּעַלְמָא, מַעֲלָה עָשׂוּ בְּיוֹחֲסִין, וְהָכָא אַצְרְכוּהָ רַבָּנָן תְּרֵי רֻבֵּי, רֹב סִיעָה הָעוֹבֶרֶת שָׁם וְרֹב הָעִיר. וְאֵין מַשִּׂיאִין לְכַתְּחִלָּה לַכְּהֻנָּה אֶלָּא בִּתְרֵי רֻבֵּי, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


אם רוב העיר משיאין לכהונה – Even though that a majority of one in a general way is sufficient, regarding priestly descent is concerned, they put up a higher standard (i.e., made the law more stringent – Talmud Ketubot 13a). And here, the rabbis required a double majority: most of the party [of priests] that pass through the place and most of the people of the city. But, ab initio, we do not marry into the priesthood other than with the double majority – and such is the Halakha.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁנִּתְאַרְמְלָה אוֹ נִתְגָּרְשָׁה.** וְתוֹבַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


האשה שנתארמלה או נתגרשה – and she claims her Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:2)


**הוּא אוֹמֵר.** קָאֵי אַנִּתְגָּרְשָׁה. וְאִי אַנִּתְאַרְמְלָה, הַיּוֹרְשִׁים אוֹמְרִים אַלְמָנָה נְשָׂאָךְ אָבִינוּ וְאֵין לָךְ אֶלָּא מָנֶה:


הוא אומר – it refers to [her stating] “when I was divorced,” for if [it was] “when I was a widow,” the heirs would say, ‘our father married you when you were a widow,” and you have only a Maneh [as the payment for the Ketubah for a non-virgin].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:3)


**הִנּוּמָא.** אִיכָּא מַאן דְּאָמַר חֻפָּה שֶׁל הֲדַס שֶׁעוֹשִׂים לַבְּתוּלוֹת. וְאִיכָּא מַאן דְּאָמַר צָעִיף שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים עַל הָעֵינַיִם וּמִתְנַמְנֶמֶת בּוֹ:


הינומא – there are those who say that it is a canopy of myrtles that they make for virgins and there are those who say that it is a veil that they place on the eyes and she is drowsy in it (see Talmud Ketubot 17b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:4)


**וְרֹאשָׁהּ פָּרוּעַ.** שַׂעֲרָהּ עַל כְּתֵפָהּ. כָּךְ הָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים לְהוֹצִיא הַבְּתוּלוֹת מִבֵּית אֲבִיהֶן לְבֵית הַחֲתוּנָה:


וראשה פרוע – her hair is on her shoulder; such was how they would practice to remove the virgins from the home of their fathers to the wedding chamber.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:1:5)


**אַף חִלּוּק קְלָיוֹת.** בִּמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָא הָיוּ נוֹהֲגִים לְחַלֵּק קְלָיוֹת בְּנִשּׂוּאֵי הַבְּתוּלוֹת. וְאִי לֵיכָּא סַהֲדֵי שֶׁעָשׂוּ בְנִשּׂוּאֶיהָ כְּמִנְהַג נִשּׂוּאֵי הַבְּתוּלוֹת, וּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה אָבַד, בַּעַל מְהֵימָן וְאֵינָהּ גוֹבָה אֶלָּא מָנֶה:


אף חלוק קליות – in the place of Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka they had the practice to distribute parched corn at the nuptials of virgins and if there weren’t witnesses that they did so at her wedding according to the custom of the nuptials of virgins and the Ketubah/marriage contract was lost, the husband is believed and she doesn’t collect anything other than a Maneh.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:2:1)


**וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּלְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא גַּבֵּי הִיא אוֹמֶרֶת מִשֶּׁאֵרַסְתַּנִי נֶאֱנַסְתִּי פָּלֵיג רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אַרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל וְאָמַר דְּלֹא מְהֵמְנִינַן לָאִשָּׁה בְּמַאי דְאָמְרָה מִשֶּׁאֵרַסְתַּנִי נֶאֱנַסְתִּי וּפָסְלָה עַצְמָהּ מִן הַכְּהֻנָּה בְּמִגּוֹ דְּאִי בַּעְיָא אָמְרָה מֻכַּת עֵץ אֲנִי וְהָיְתָה כְּשֵׁרָה לַכְּהֻנָּה, הָנֵי מִילֵי הֵיכָא דְאִיכָּא אִסּוּר וְהֶתֵּר לִפְסוֹל לַכְּהֻנָּה אוֹ לְהַכְשִׁיר, בְּהָא פָּלֵיג רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אַרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל וְאָמַר דְּלֹא מְהֵימְנִינַן לָהּ בְּמִגּוֹ, אֲבָל הֵיכָא דְּלֵיכָּא אִסּוּר וְהֶתֵּר אֶלָּא דְּרָרָא דְמָמוֹנָא, כְּגוֹן הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ שָׂדֶה זוֹ שֶׁל אָבִיךָ הָיְתָה וּלְקַחְתִּיהָ מִמֶּנּוּ, מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לְרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל דְּבִכְהַאי גַוְנָא מְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ בְּמִגּוֹ דְּאִי בָּעֵי אָמַר שֶׁלִּי הִיא וְאָמַר שֶׁל אָבִיךָ הָיְתָה וּלְקַחְתִּיהָ מִמֶּנּוּ נֶאֱמָן:


ומודה רבי יהושע – even though that above in the first chapter (Mishnah 6), regarding what she says: “after you betrothed me, I was raped,” Rabbi Yehoshua disputes Rabban Gamaliel and states, “we do not believe the woman.” In what she said: “after you betrothed me, I was raped,” and she disqualifies herself from [being able to marry into] the priesthood through “Miggo”-the legal rule according to which the deponent’s statement is accepted as true on the ground that, if he (or she) had intended to tell a lie, he/she might have invented one more advantageous to his/her case (see Talmud Ketubot 16a), for if she had wanted, she could have stated, “I was one who lost my hymen through an accidental lesion and was fit [to being able to marry into] the priesthood. These words [apply] where there is [a case] of prohibition or permission to prohibit [her to marry into] the priesthood or to validate it – in this Rabbi Yehoshua disputes Rabban Gamaliel and states that we don’t believe her through “Miggo,” but where there isn’t a case of prohibition and permission other than money at stake/eventual loss (see Talmud Ketubot 23b) such as, for example, where a person says to his fellow: “this field was your father’s, and I purchased it from him,” Rabbi Yehoshua agrees with Rabban Gamaliel that in a case like this, we believe him through “Miggo,” for if he had wanted, he could have said, “it is mine,” and when he said, “this field was your father’s, and I purchased it from him,” he Is believed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:2:2)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ עֵדִים שֶׁהִיא שֶׁל אָבִיו אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּהָא מַתְנִיתִין אָתָא לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן כְּגוֹן שֶׁלֹּא אֲכָלָהּ אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים בִּפְנֵי הָאָב וְשָׁנָה אַחַת בִּפְנֵי הַבֵּן, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא שָׁלְמוּ לוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ שְׁנֵי חֲזָקָה בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב, הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁאֲכָלָהּ בִּפְנֵי הַבֵּן אֵינוֹ עוֹלֶה לְמִנְיַן שְׁנֵי חֲזָקָה:


ואם יש עדים שהיא של אביו אינו נאמן – In the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 17b) it explains that this Mishnah comes to inform us as for example, that he did not consume other than two years in the presence of the father and one year in the presence of the son, since he did not pay him three years of legal claim of undisturbed possession during a legally fixed period in the lifetime of the father, the year that he ate in the presence of the son does not count for him for the count of the years of presumptive ownership.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:3:1)


**אֲנוּסִים הָיִינוּ.** אֹנֶס נְפָשׁוֹת שֶׁמָּא יַהַרְגֶנּוּ. אֲבָל אֹנֶס מָמוֹן שֶׁמָּא יִקַּח מָמוֹנוֹ [מָמוֹנֵנוּ], וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם אָמְרוּ בִּשְׁבִיל רֹב מָמוֹן שֶׁנָּתַן לָנוּ, אֵינָן נֶאֱמָנִין, דְּאֵין אָדָם מֵשִׂים עַצְמוֹ רָשָׁע, דְּאָדָם קָרוֹב אֵצֶל עַצְמוֹ וְאֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן עַל עַצְמוֹ לֹא לִזְכוּת וְלֹא לְחוֹבָה. וְכֵן פְּסוּלֵי עֵדוּת הָיִינוּ, מֵחֲמַת קֻרְבָה. אֲבָל מֵחֲמַת עֲבֵרָה אֵינָן נֶאֱמָנִים:


אנוסים היינו – compulsion at the pain of death lest he kill him, but compulsion of money, lest he take his money [from him] and all the more so, if he said to him: “for most of the money that he gave us,” they are not believed, for no one incriminates himself (i.e., his testimony against himself has no legal effect), for a person is close to himself, and is not believed about himself, neither for acquittal nor for conviction. And similarly, those who are ineligible for testimony, which is on account of nearness but on account of transgression, they are not believed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:3:2)


**יוֹצֵא מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר.** חֲתוּמִים בִּשְׁטָר אַחֵר שֶׁהֻחְזַק בְּבֵית דִּין, וּבָא אוֹתוֹ שְׁטָר לְפָנֵינוּ עִם זֶה, וּכְתַב חוֹתְמָן דּוֹמִין זֶה לְשֶׁל זֶה:


יוצא ממקום אחר – inscribed in another document that is in the possession of the Jewish court , and this same document came before us with this one and the writing of the signatories are similar one to the other.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:1)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ נֶאֱמָנִים.** דְּהָא אִיכָּא תְּרֵי סַהֲדֵי עַל כָּל כְּתָב וּכְתָב:


הרי אלו נאמנים – for surely there are two witnesses on all of the handwritings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:2)


**צְרִיכִים לְצָרֵף עִמָּהֶם אַחֵר.** שֶׁעַל כְּתַב יָדָן הֵן מְעִידִין לֹא עַל מָנֶה שֶׁבַּשְּׁטָר. וְצָרִיךְ תְּרֵי סַהֲדֵי עַל כָּל כְּתָב וּכְתָב:


צריכים לצרף עמהם אחר – that on their own signatures they testify, not on the Maneh/monetary portion (the weight of gold or silver equal to one hundred common or fifty sacred shekels) in the document , and they require two witnesses for each handwriting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:4:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵינָם צְרִיכִין לְצָרֵף עִמָּהֶם אַחֵר.** שֶׁעַל מָנֶה שֶׁבַּשְּׁטָר הֵם מְעִידִים, וּכְשֶׁזֶּה אוֹמֵר זֶה כְתַב יָדִי וְזֶה אוֹמֵר זֶה כְתַב יָדִי נִמְצְאוּ שְׁנֵי עֵדִים עַל מָנֶה שֶׁבַּשְּׁטָר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א אומרים אינם צריכין לצרף עמהם חר – that on the Maneh/monetary portion in the document they are testifying and when this one says, “this is my signature,” and the other [witness] says, “this is my signature,” it is found that there are two witnesses for the monetary amount in the document and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:5:1)


**אִם מִשֶּׁנִּשֵּׂאת.** לֹא נִשֵּׂאת מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא אִם מִשֶּׁהִתִּירוּהָ לִנָּשֵׂא בָּאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁנִּשְׁבֵּית:


אם שנשאת – she did not actually get married, but rather, when they permitted her to get married, witnesses came that she had been taken captive,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:5:2)


**לֹא תֵצֵא.** מֵהֶתֵּרָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְתִנָּשֵׂא לְכַתְּחִלָּה. אֲבָל אִם בָּאוּ עֵדִים שֶׁנִּטְמֵאת, אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ לָהּ כַּמָּה בָנִים, תֵּצֵא:


she should not go forth (i.e., leave through divorce) from her first permission [to marry] and she should marry ab initio, but if witnesses came that she had been defiled, even if she had several children, she should go forth (i.e., leave the marriage).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:1)


**שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים שֶׁנִּשְׁבּוּ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ עֵדִים שֶׁנִּשְׁבּוּ:


שתי נשים שנשבו – that there are witnesses that they were taken captive.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:2)


**שֶׁמְּעִידוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ.** כָּל אַחַת אוֹמֶרֶת חֲבֶרְתִּי טְהוֹרָה:


שמעידות זו את זו – each one of them says, “my companion is pure.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:6:3)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ נֶאֱמָנוֹת.** דְּבִשְׁבוּיָה הֵקֵלּוּ לְהַכְשִׁיר עֵד אֶחָד וַאֲפִלּוּ עֶבֶד וַאֲפִלּוּ אִשָּׁה וַאֲפִלּוּ קָטָן מֵסִיחַ לְפִי תֻמּוֹ. וּבִלְבָד שֶׁיָּעִיד הָעֵד שֶׁלֹּא פֵרַשׁ מִמֶּנָּה מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁנִּשְׁבֵּית עַד שֶׁיָּצְאָה מֵרְשׁוּת הַגּוֹיִם:


הרי אלו נאמנות – for with a captive woman, they were lenient to declare fit one witness, and even a slave and even a woman and even a minor who makes a statement in ignorance of its legal bearing, and as long as the witness testifies that he did not leave from her from the time that she was taken captive until the time that she left from the domain of the heathens (see also Tractate Yevamot, Chapter 16, Mishnah 7 and more crucially, the earliest of Mishnah texts, Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 6, Mishnah 1, especially the words of Rabbi Yehuda ben Baba).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:7:1)


**אֵינָן נֶאֱמָנִים.** לִתֵּן לָהֶם תְּרוּמָה:


אינן נאמנים – to give to them Terumah/priest’s due.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:7:2)


**וּבִזְמַן שֶׁהֵן מְעִידִין זֶה עַל זֶה.** שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד אוֹמֵר אֲנִי וַחֲבֵרִי כֹּהֵן:


ובזמן שהן מעידין זה על זה – for each one says, “I and my colleague is a Kohen.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֵין מַעֲלִין לַכְּהֻנָּה עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד.** אֲפִלּוּ הֵיכָא דְלֵיכָּא גּוֹמְלִין, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הָכָא דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ לְגוֹמְלִין, הָעֵד אַתָּה עָלַי וַאֲנִי עָלֶיךָ:


ר' יהודה אומר אין מעלין לכהונה ע"פ עד אחד – even where there are not [witnesses] repaying each other (with mutual recommendations), and all the more so here, where one can be suspicious of repaying each other: “you testify about me and I [will testify] about you.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:2)


**עוֹרְרִין.** שֶׁקּוֹרִין עָלָיו שֵׁם פְּסוּל. וְאֵין עִרְעוּר פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁנַיִם:


עוררין – that they call upon him a name of disqualification, but there is no contesting the legitimacy of a person with less than two [people].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:3)


**מַעֲלִין.** הֵיכָא דְלֵיכָּא גּוֹמְלִים. וְהַיְנוּ דְּאִיכָּא בֵּין רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְתַנָּא קַמָּא דְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מעלין – where there aren’t [witnesses] repaying each other (with mutual recommendations), which is exactly what is between Rabbi Eliezer and the first Tanna/teacher of Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:8:4)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר מַעֲלִין לַכְּהֻנָּה.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל הַיְנוּ רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר. וּמַסִּיק, דִּבְמִצְטָרְפִין לְעֵדוּת קָמִפַּלְגֵּי, כְּגוֹן דְּמֻחְזָק לָן בַּאֲבוּהָ דְּהַאי דְּכֹהֵן הוּא, וְנָפַק עֲלֵיהּ קָלָא דְּבֶן גְּרוּשָׁה אוֹ בֶן חֲלוּצָה הוּא וְאַחְתִּינֵיהּ, וְאָתָא עֵד אֶחָד וְאָמַר יָדַעְנָא בֵיהּ דְּכֹהֵן הוּא וְאַסְקִינֵיהּ, וְאָתוּ בֵי תְּרֵי וְאָמְרֵי בֶּן גְּרוּשָׁה אוֹ בֶן חֲלוּצָה הוּא וְאַחְתִּינֵיהּ, וְאָתָא עֵד אֶחָד וְאָמַר יָדַעְנָא בֵיהּ דְּכֹהֵן הוּא, רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר מַעֲלִין לַכְּהֻנָּה עַל פִּי זֶה הָעֵד הָאַחֲרוֹן מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמְּצָרְפִין אוֹתוֹ עִם הָעֵד הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבִּטֵּל הַקּוֹל וְאָמַר יָדַעְנָא בֵיהּ דְּכֹהֵן הוּא, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הֵעִידוּ עֵדוּתָן בְּבַת אַחַת עֵדוּתָן מִצְטָרֶפֶת, וְאָמְרִינַן אוֹקֵי הָנֵי תְּרֵי דְּאָמְרֵי כֹּהֵן הוּא לַהֲדֵי תְּרֵי דְּאָמְרֵי דְּבֶן גְּרוּשָׁה הוּא, וְאוֹקֵי גַּבְרָא אַחֶזְקָתֵּיהּ. וּלְרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר לֹא מַסְּקִינַן לֵיהּ עַד שֶׁיָּעִידוּ שְׁנֵי עֵדִים בִּזְמַן אֶחָד דְּכֹהֵן הוּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל שֶׁהָעֵדִים מִצְטָרְפִין אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הֵעִידוּ עֵדוּתָן כְּאֶחָד:


רשב"ג אומר מעלין לכהונה – In the Gemara (Tractate Ketubot 26a-26b) an objection is raised: Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel is identical with Rabbi Eliezer and brings up that they disagree on whether they combine for testimony, such as the example that we treat here a case where the father of this [person] is a Kohen, and a rumor came out concerning him that he is the son of a divorced woman or a the son of a halutzah (where she removed the shoe of her dead husband’s brother who refused to leviratical marriage, and they put him down (from the status of priesthood) and one witness came and testified, “I know that he is a Kohen,” and they raised him [again] and [then] came two [other witnesses] and said [that] “he is the son of a divorced woman or the son of halutzah,” and they put him down [again] and [then] one witness came and said: “I know that he is a Kohen,” Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel says that they raise him to the priesthood on [the testimony] of this last witness because they combine him with the first witness whose “voice” was voided, and who stated: “I know that he is a Kohen,” and even though they did not make their testimonies at the same time, their testimony combines and we state, we establish these two [witnesses] who stated that he is a Kohen with those two [witnesses] who stated that he is a the son of a divorced woman or the son of a halutzah, and we establish a person with his legal presumption, but for Rabbi Eliezer, we don’t raise him up until two witnesses will testify at the same time that he is a Kohen, but the Halakah is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel that the witnesses combine, even though they did not testify as one.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:1)


**עַל יְדֵי מָמוֹן מֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעְלָהּ.** שֶׁמְּפַחֲדִין לְהַפְסִיד מָמוֹנָם וְלֹא מַפְקְרֵי לָהּ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁיַּד יִשְׂרָאֵל תַּקִּיפָה עַל הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים, אָז מֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעְלָהּ אֲפִלּוּ בַּעְלָהּ כֹּהֵן, וְלֹא דָמִי לִשְׁבוּיָה, מִשּׁוּם דְּמִרְתְתֵי לְהַפְסִיד מָמוֹנָן. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁיַּד הָעוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים תַּקִּיפָה, אֲפִלּוּ עַל יְדֵי מָמוֹן אֲסוּרָה לְבַעְלָהּ אִם בַּעְלָהּ כֹּהֵן. וְאִם בַּעְלָהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מֻתֶּרֶת בְּכָל עִנְיָן, דְּאֵשֶׁת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה מֻתֶּרֶת לְבַעְלָהּ:


ע"י ממון מותרת לבעלה – for they are afraid of losing their money and don’t make it ownerless, and especially when the hand of the Israelites is strong over the idolaters, then she is permitted [to return] to her husband, even if her husband is a Kohen and it is not similar to a woman taken captive because they are frightened to lose their money, but when the hand of the idolaters is stronger, even through the case of money, she is prohibited to her husband if her husband is a Kohen, but if her husband is an Israelite, she is permitted in all matters, for the wife of an Israelite who meets with an accident is permitted to her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:2)


**עַל יְדֵי נְפָשׁוֹת.** שֶׁהָיְתָה נִדּוֹנֶת לְמָוֶת, אֲסוּרָה אֲפִלּוּ לְבַעְלָהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא נִבְעֲלָה בְּרָצוֹן לְאֶחָד מֵהֶן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:3)


**כַּרְכּוֹם.** תַּרְגּוּם מָצוֹר, כַּרְכּוֹמָא:


כרכום – Aramaic translation is works of siege.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:4)


**פְּסוּלוֹת.** אֲסוּרוֹת לְכֹהֵן, דְּאֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן אֲסוּרָה כְּשֶׁנֶּאֶנְסָה. וְאִם יֵשׁ בָּעִיר מַחֲבוֹאָה אַחַת שֶׁיְּכוֹלָה אִשָּׁה אַחַת לְהֵחָבֵא שָׁם, כָּל אַחַת וְאַחַת מִנְּשֵׁי הָעִיר נֶאֱמָנוֹת לוֹמַר בַּמַּחֲבוֹאָה נֶחְבֵּאתִי וְלֹא נִטְמֵאתִי, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁיְּכוֹלָה לוֹמַר נֶחְבֵּאתִי, נֶאֱמֶנֶת גַּם כֵּן כְּשֶׁאָמְרָה לֹא נֶחְבֵּאתִי וְלֹא נִטְמֵאתִי:


פסולות – they are prohibited to [marry] a Kohen for the wife of a Kohen is prohibited when she meets with an accident and if there is in the city a hiding place (see Tractate Ketubot 27a) that one woman can hide there, each one of the women of that city are believe to state, “I was hidden and I was not defiled,” and since she is able to say, “I was hidden,” she is believed; also when she stated: “I was not hidden and I was not defiled.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:5)


**הַמָּעוֹן הַזֶּה.** שְׁבוּעָה הִיא:


המעון הזה – it is an oath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:9:6)


**אֵין אָדָם מֵעִיד עַל יְדֵי עַצְמוֹ.** וְאִם הֵעִיד אָדָם עַל שְׁבוּיָה שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה, הֲרֵי זֶה לֹא יִשָּׂאֶנָּה אִם הוּא כֹּהֵן, מִפְּנֵי הַחֲשָׁד. וְאִם פָּדָה אוֹתָהּ מִן הַשֶּׁבִי בְּמָמוֹנוֹ, הֲרֵי זֶה מֵעִיד עָלֶיהָ וְנוֹשְׂאָהּ, דְּלֹא שָׁדֵי אֶינַשׁ זוּזֵי בִּכְדִי, וְאִם לֹא יָדַע שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה לֹא נָתַן מָמוֹנוֹ עָלֶיהָ:


אין אדם מעיד ע"י עצמו – and if a man testified about a woman taken captive that she is ritually pure, he should not marry her if he is a Kohen because of the suspicion and if he redeemed her from prison with his money, he may testify on her behalf and marries her for a person does not throw money at random (unless sure that there is no legal impediment to marrying the woman whom he is about to redeem)and if he did not known that she is pure, he would not give his money for her.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:1)


**זֶה כְתַב יָדוֹ שֶׁל אַבָּא.** וּמְקַיְּמִין הַשְּׁטָר עַל פִּיו, שֶׁקִּיּוּם שְׁטָרוֹת דְּרַבָּנָן וְהֵימְנוּהוּ רַבָּנָן בִּדְרַבָּנָן:


validating a document is all verbal, and the mitzvah of validating a document is from the rabbis and we are more lenient


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:2)


**שֶׁיָּצְתָה בְהִנּוּמָא וְרֹאשָׁהּ פָּרוּעַ.** וְנוֹטֶלֶת כְּתֻבָּה מָאתַיִם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא מַפְקִינַן מָמוֹנָא אֶלָּא בְּעֵדוּת בְּרוּרָה, הָכָא שָׁאנֵי, הוֹאִיל וְרֹב נָשִׁים בְּתוּלוֹת נִשָּׂאוֹת, גִּלּוּי מִלְּתָא בְּעַלְמָא הוּא:


שיצתה בהינומא וראשה פרוע – and she takes her Ketubah of two hundred [zuz] and even though they do not release money other than through clear testimony, it is different here, for most of the women are married as virgins, it is revealed as something general.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:3)


**וְשֶׁהָיָה אִישׁ פְּלוֹנִי יוֹצֵא מִבֵּית הַסֵּפֶר.** כְּשֶׁהָיִינוּ לוֹמְדִין תִּינוֹקוֹת בְּבֵית רַבָּן:


ושהיה איש פלוני יוצא מבית הספר – when we were studying as school children,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:4)


**לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** וְעַל פִּי עֵדוּת זוֹ מַאֲכִילִין לֵיהּ תְּרוּמָה דְּרַבָּנָן, כְּגוֹן תְּרוּמָה שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין מֵעָצִיץ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נָקוּב וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה. אֲבָל תְּרוּמָה דְאוֹרַיְיתָא אֵין מַאֲכִילִין עַל פִּי עֵדוּת כָּזֶה. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא עֶבֶד כֹּהֵן הָיָה, שֶׁאָסוּר לְלַמֵּד אֶת הָעֶבֶד תּוֹרָה:


to eat Terumah and through this testimony, we feed Terumah of the Rabbis to him, such as for example Terumah/priest’s due that we remove from a pot without holes and things similar to it, but Terumah, according to the Torah, we don’t feed him through testimony such as this, and we do not suspect lest he ws the servant of a Kohen, for it is prohibited to teach the slave Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:5)


**וְשֶׁהָיָה חוֹלֵק עִמָּנוּ עַל הַגֹּרֶן.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא עֶבֶד כֹּהֵן הָיָה, שֶׁאֵין חוֹלְקִים תְּרוּמָה לְעֶבֶד אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן רַבּוֹ עִמּוֹ:


ושהיה חולק עמנו על הגורן – and we do not suspect lest he was the servant of Kohen, for we do not distribute Terumah to a slave other than if his master is with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:6)


**וְשֶׁהַמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** הַחוֹרֵשׁ אֶת הַקֶּבֶר הֲרֵי זֶה עוֹשֶׂה בֵּית הַפְּרָס מֵאָה אַמָּה, שֶׁכָּךְ שִׁעֲרוּ שֶׁהַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה מוֹלֶכֶת עַצְמוֹת הַמֵּת. וְטֻמְאַת בֵּית הַפְּרָס דְּרַבָּנָן:


ושהמקום הזה בית הפרס – A person who plows the grave makes a a field of a square P’ras, declared unclean on account of crushed bones carried over it from a ploughed grave which is one hundred cubits for this is what they measured that the plough drags the bones of the dead and the defilement of the field of a square P’ras according to the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:7)


**וְעַד כָּאן הָיִינוּ בָאִין בְּשַׁבָּת.** דִּתְחוּמִים דְּרַבָּנָן:


ועד כאן היינו באין בשבת – the Rabbinic Sabbath limits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 2:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/2:10:8)


**מַעֲמָד וּמִסְפֵּד.** מָקוֹם הָיָה לוֹ כָּאן לְהַסְפִּיד מֵתָיו וְלַעֲשׂוֹת מַעֲמָדוֹת וּמוֹשָׁבוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂין לַמֵּת, בְּהָא לֹא מְהֵימָן, שֶׁהוּא דָּבָר שֶׁבְּמָמוֹן וְצָרִיךְ עֵדוּת גְּמוּרָה:


מעמד ומספד – a place which he had herei. to eulogize his dead and to make the “standing up and sitting down” (i.e., the halting of the funeral escort on returning from burial for lamentation or consolation) which they would do for the dead, in this he Is not believed, which is a monetary matter, and which requires complete testimony.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ נְעָרוֹת.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן פְּסוּלוֹת יֵשׁ לָהֶן קְנָס. אִם אָנַס אָדָם אַחַת מֵהֶן נוֹתֵן לְאָבִיהָ חֲמִשִּׁים כֶּסֶף:


אלו נערות – for even though they are ineligible/invalid [as marriageable parners], they are assessed a fine. If a man perpetrated violence against one of them, he gives to her father fifty silver [coins].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:2)


**הַנְּתִינָה.** מִן הַגִּבְעוֹנִים. וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁנְּתָנָם יְהוֹשֻׁעַ חֹטְבֵי עֵצִים וְשֹׁאֲבֵי מַיִם נִקְרָאִים נְתִינִים. וְהֵם אֲסוּרִים לָבֹא בַקָּהָל:


הנתינה – from the Gibeonites and on account that Joshua made them choppers of wood and water carriers, they are called Netinim, and they forbidden to enter into the community [of Israel].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:3)


**וְעַל הַכּוּתִית.** הַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר כּוּתִים גֵּרֵי אֲרָיוֹת הֵם וּכְגוֹיִם הֵם חֲשׁוּבִים:


על הכותית – This Tanna/teacher holds that Kutim are “lion-proselytes,” (i.e., proselytes from mere fear, with reference to II Kings 17:25) and are considered as heathens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:4)


**פְּחוּתוֹת מִבַּת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים.** דִּבְחֶזְקַת בְּתוּלוֹת הֵן, שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּבְעֲלוּ בַּשִּׁבְיָה אוֹ בְגֵיּוּתָן, בְּתוּלֵיהֶן חוֹזְרִים:


פחותות מבת שלש שנים – that is they have the legal presumption of being virgins, even though they had intercourse during captivity or while in a gentile status, their virginity returns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:5)


**וְעַל אֵשֶׁת אָחִיו וְעַל אֵשֶׁת אֲחִי אָבִיו.** שֶׁנִּתְקַדְּשָׁה לְאֶחָד מֵהֶן וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה מִן הָאֵרוּסִין, וַעֲדַיִן בְּתוּלָה הִיא:


ועל אשת אחיו ועל אשת אחי אביו – that she was betrothed to one of them or divorced from betrothal, and she was still a virgin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:1:6)


**שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן בְּהִכָּרֵת אֵין בָּהֶן מִיתַת בֵּית דִּין.** וְכָרֵת אֵינוֹ פוֹטֵר מִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין. וְהָנֵי מִילֵי, שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה שָׁם הַתְרָאָה. אֲבָל אִם הָיְתָה שָׁם הַתְרָאָה פָּטוּר מִן הַקְּנָס, דְּקַיְמָא לָן כָּל חַיָּבֵי כְּרֵתוֹת שֶׁהִתְרוּ בָּהֶן לוֹקִין, וְאֵין אָדָם לוֹקֶה וּמְשַׁלֵּם:


שאע"פ הן בהכרת אין בהן מיתת ב"ד – and extirpation does not exempt from payment/indemnity, and these words [apply] where there had been no warning but if they ad been warning, one is exempt from the fine, for hold that those who are liable for extirpation who had been warned are flogged, for no one is flogged and pays a fine.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:1)


**יְתֵרוֹת עַל בְּנוֹת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּרְאוּיוֹת לְבִיאָה בְּחֶזְקַת הֶפְקֵר הֵן, וְנִבְעֲלוֹת בְּגֵיּוּתָן, וְהַשְּׁבוּיָה בְּשִׁבְיָתָהּ:


יתריות על בנות שלש שנים – for since they are appropriate for coition with the legal presumption that they are ownerless, and they had intercourse in their gentile status, and the captive woman while she is in her captivity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:2)


**הֲרֵי הִיא בִקְדֻשָּׁתָהּ.** וְאֵינָהּ בְּחֶזְקַת בְּעוּלָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּשְׁבֵּית כְּשֶׁהִיא גְּדוֹלָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


הרי היא בקדושתה – and she is not in the legal status of one no longer a virgin even though she had been taken captive while she was an adult, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:2:3)


**וְלֹא יִהְיֶה אָסוֹן עָנוֹשׁ יֵעָנֵשׁ.** הָא אִם יִהְיֶה אָסוֹן בְּאֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי אֵלּוּ הָאֲנָשִׁים הַמְּרִיבִין, שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג אֶחָד מֵהֶן, לֹא יֵעָנֵשׁ הָהוֹרֵג בְּמָמוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא חַיָּב מִיתַת בֵּית דִּין כֵּיוָן שֶׁהֲרָגוֹ לָזֶה. וְאֵין הֶפְרֵשׁ בַּעֲבֵרָה שֶׁחַיָּבִין עָלֶיהָ מִיתַת בֵּית דִּין בֵּין שׁוֹגֵג לְמֵזִיד, לְעוֹלָם פָּטוּר מִתַּשְׁלוּמִין. אֲבָל בַּעֲבֵרָה שֶׁחַיָּבִין עָלֶיהָ כָּרֵת אוֹ מַלְקוֹת, אֵינוֹ פָּטוּר מִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שָׁם עֵדִים וְהַתְרָאָה וְיִלְקֶה, וְאָז אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם:


ולא יהיה און ענוש יענש – surely if there will be an accident with one of these two people who are arguing that one of them was killed , the person who killed is not punished monetarily for he is liable death penalty by the Jewish court because they killed this one, there is no difference regarding the sin which makes one liable for death by the Jewish court whether inadvertently or willfully, is always exempt from payment/indemnity, but concerning a transgression where one is liable for extirpation through it, or stripes/flogging, he is not exempt from payment/indemnity until there will be witnesses and warning and he will be flogged and then does not pay.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:3:1)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי אוֹמֵר אֵין לָהּ קְנָס.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (דברים כב) אֲשֶׁר לֹא אֹרָשָׂה, הָא אֹרָשָׂה פַּעַם אַחַת שׁוּב אֵין לָהּ קְנָס:


רבי יוסי הגלילי אומר אין לה קנס – as the Biblical verse states (Deuteronomy 22:28): “[If a man comes upon a virgin] who is not engaged [and seizes her and lies with her, and they are discovered],” but if she is engaged one time, furthermore, there is no indemnity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:3:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר יֶשׁ לָהּ קְנָס וּקְנָסָהּ לְעַצְמָהּ.** דְּדָרְשִׁינַן אֲשֶׁר לֹא אֹרָשָׂה, לְאָבִיהָ, הָא אֹרָשָׂה, לְעַצְמָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


רבי עקיבא אומר יש לה קנס וקנסה לעצמה – since we expound upon “who is not engaged” (Deuteronomy 22:28) to her father, but she is engaged to herself, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:1)


**הָאוֹנֵס נוֹתֵן אֶת הַצַּעַר.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ דוֹמֶה נִבְעֶלֶת בְּאֹנֶס לְנִבְעֶלֶת בְּרָצוֹן, וְהַמְפֻתָּה אֵין לָהּ צַעַר, שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר עִנּוּי בַּתּוֹרָה אֵצֶל אֹנֶס וְלֹא נֶאֱמַר עִנּוּי אֵצֶל מְפֻתָּה:


האונס נותן את הצער – someone who had intercourse by compulsion is not the same as someone who had intercourse willingly, and the seduced woman does not have pain, for regarding ‘’suffering,” in the Torah concerning compulsion, but “suffering” is not stated regarding a seduced woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:2)


**הָאוֹנֵס נוֹתֵן מִיָּד.** לְאָבִיהָ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכּוֹנְסָהּ:


והאונס נותן מיד – to her father, even though he married her/consummated a marriage by conducting a woman to his house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:3)


**לִכְשֶׁיּוֹצִיא.** כְּלוֹמַר לִכְשֶׁלֹּא יִכְנֹס. שֶׁאִם יִכְנֹס אֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן קְנָס, כְּדִכְתִיב (שמות כב) מָהֹר יִמְהָרֶנָּה וְאִם מָאֵן יְמָאֵן כֶּסֶף יִשְׁקֹל. אֲבָל גַּבֵּי אוֹנֵס כְּתִיב (דברים כב) וְנָתַן חֲמִשִּׁים כֶּסֶף וְלוֹ תִהְיֶה לְאִשָּׁה:


לכיוציא – meaning to say that if he will not marry her/consummate a marriage by conducting a woman to his house, for if he does not marry [her], he doesn’t pay the fine, as it is written (Exodus 22:15): “[If a man seduces a virgin for whom the bride-price has not been paid, and lies with her] he must make her his wife by payment of the bride-price,” and “if her father refuses [to give her to him], he must weigh out the silver [in accordance with the bride-price for virgins]” (Exodus 22:16); but concerning the rape, it is written (Deuteronomy 22:29): “[the man who lay with her] shall pay the girl’s father fifty [shekels of] silver and she shall be his wife.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:4:4)


**שׁוֹתֶה בַעֲצִיצוֹ.** בִּכְלִי מָאוּס שֶׁבָּחַר בּוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר עַל כָּרְחוֹ יִשָּׂאֶנָּה:


שותה בעציצו – in a despised utensil that he chose for It, meaning to say, he is forced to marry her.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:5:1)


**מֻכַּת שְׁחִין.** מְצֹרַעַת:


מוכת שחין – a leprous woman.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:6:1)


**יְתוֹמָה שֶׁנִּתְאָרְסָה וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה הָאוֹנֵס חַיָּב וְהַמְפַתֶּה פָטוּר.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּיתוֹמָה הִיא וּקְנָסָהּ שֶׁלָּהּ הוּא, הָא אַחִילְתֵּיהּ גַּבֵּיהּ, דְּמִדַּעְתָּהּ נִתְפַּתְּתָה לוֹ, וּפָטוּר. וְלֹא יְתוֹמָה בִּלְבָד, אֶלָּא כָּל נַעֲרָה שֶׁנִּתְאָרְסָה וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה אוֹנֵס חַיָּב וּמְפַתֶּה פָּטוּר כִּיתוֹמָה, דְּהָא קְנָסָהּ לְעַצְמָהּ כִּדְאָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא לְעֵיל, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


יתומה שנתארסה ונתגרשה האונס חייב והמפתה פטור – for since she is an orphan and her fine is hers, the logical support is towards him, for of her own free will she was seduced to him and he is exempt. But not only an orphan, but rather, any young woman who was betrothed and divorced, the rapist is liable and the seducer is exempt like the orphan-girl, for the fine is for herself, as is stated by Rabbi Akiva above (in Mishnah 3 of this chapter) and such is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:1)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי הַמְבַיֵּשׁ.** אָדָם בֵּינוֹנִי הַמְבַיֵּשׁ, בָּשְׁתּוֹ קָשָׁה מֵאָדָם בָּזוּי וּמֵאָדָם חָשׁוּב. כָּךְ מָצָאתִי בְּפֵרוּשֵׁי רַבּוֹתַי. וּבְפֶרֶק הַחוֹבֵל מָצָאתִי דְּאָדָם קַל שֶׁבִּיֵּשׁ, בָּשְׁתּוֹ מְרֻבָּה. וְכֵן עִקָּר:


הכל לפי המבייש – an average person who shames, his shame is harder than a despised person and/or from an important person. This is what I found in the explanations of my teachers. But in the [eight chapter of Bava Kamma], the Chapter of החובל /One who causes injury, I found that a simple person who is shamed, his shame is greater, and this is the essence (see the statement in Tractate Bava Kamma, Chapter 8, Mishnah 6: זה הכלל: הכל לפי כבודו – This is the general principle: Everything is according to one’s station/status).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:2)


**וְהַמִּתְבַּיֵּשׁ.** לְפִי חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ בָּשְׁתּוֹ:


והמתבייש – according to his importance is his shame.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:7:3)


**כְּאִלּוּ הִיא שִׁפְחָה.** אוֹמְדִין כַּמָּה אָדָם רוֹצֶה לִתֵּן בֵּין שִׁפְחָה בְּתוּלָה לְשִׁפְחָה בְּעוּלָה לְהַשִּׂיאָהּ לְעַבְדּוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְרַבּוֹ קוֹרַת רוּחַ הֵימֶנּוּ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ מְנָלָן דְּנוֹתֵן בֹּשֶׁת וּפְגָם וָצַעַר, אֵימָא חֲמִשִּׁים כֶּסֶף אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא לְכָל מִילֵי. וּמְתָרֵץ, אָמַר קְרָא (דברים כב) וְנָתַן הָאִישׁ הַשּׁוֹכֵב עִמָּהּ לַאֲבִי הַנַּעֲרָה חֲמִשִּׁים כֶּסֶף, הֲנָאַת שְׁכִיבָה לְבַד הִיא חֲמִשִּׁים כֶּסֶף, חוּץ מֵהַבֹּשֶׁת וְהַפְּגָם וְהַצַּעַר:


כאילו היא שפחה – we appraise how much a man wants to give whether for a virgin maidservant or for a maidservant who has had intercourse to marry her to his servant whose master has satisfaction from him. And in the Gemara (Tractate Ketubot 40b) raises a question: from where [in the Torah] do we learn that he pays for shame, and damage and pain? I would say that the All-Merciful said (Deuteronomy 22:29): “fifty [shekels of] silver,” to all the words and he answers, Scripture states (Deuteronomy 22:29): “The man who lay her shall pay the girl’s father fifty [shekels of] silver,[and she shall be his wife].” The pleasure of lying is separate, the fifty [shekels of] silver is outside of the shame and the damage and the pain.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:1)


**כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ מֶכֶר.** לָאָב בְּבִתּוֹ, דְּהַיְנוּ בְּקַטְנוּתָהּ, אֵין לוֹ בָהּ קְנָס, דְּאֵין קְנָס לִקְטַנָּה. וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּהָא מַתְנִיתִין, דְּהָא מַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי מֵאִיר הִיא דְּאָמַר קְטַנָּה אֵין לָהּ קְנָס. אֲבָל חֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים קְטַנָּה יֶשׁ לָהּ קְנָס, וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וּקְטַנָּה לְעִנְיַן זֶה הִיא מִבַּת שָׁלֹשׁ עַד שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד וְתָבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת:


כל מקום שיש מכר – to the father regarding his daughter, that is, while she is in her youth, he has no fine in regard to her, for there is no fine for a minor. But the Halakha is not like this Mishnah, for this Mishnah is according to Rabbi Meir who states that a minor girl has no fine, but the Sages state that a minor girl has a fine. And a minor girl in this regard is someone from age three until age twelve years and one day who brings forth two [pubic] hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:2)


**כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ קְנָס.** מִשֶּׁהִיא נַעֲרָה. וְהִיא כְּשֶׁתִּהְיֶה בַת שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד וְהֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, שֶׁזּוֹ יֶשׁ לָהּ קְנָס:


וכל מקום שיש קנס – when she is a young woman and this is when she is from twelve years and one day old and she brought forth two [pubic] hairs – like this she has a fine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:3)


**אֵין מָכֶר.** שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מוֹכֵר אֶת בִּתּוֹ כְּשֶׁהִיא נַעֲרָה:


אין מכר – for a man does not sell her daughter when she is a young woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:8:4)


**הַבּוֹגֶרֶת.** מִבַּת שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְשִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים וְיוֹם אֶחָד וְהֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת הִיא הַנִּקְרֵאת בּוֹגֶרֶת, וְאֵין אָבִיהָ יָכוֹל לְמָכְרָהּ, וְאֵין לָהּ קְנָס:


הבוגרת – from twelve years of age and six months and one day, and she has brought forth two [pubic] hairs, she is what is called a mature woman and her father cannot sell her and she does not have a fine.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר פִּתִּיתִי אֶת בִּתּוֹ שֶׁל פְּלוֹנִי.** לֹא מִבַּעְיָא הָאוֹמֵר אָנַסְתִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ פּוֹגֵם אוֹתָהּ כָּל כָּךְ, שֶׁאֵין לַעַז שֶׁל אֲנוּסָה גָּדוֹל כָּל כָּךְ, דִּפְשִׁיטָא דִּמְשַׁלֵּם בֹּשֶׁת וּפְגָם עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ הָאוֹמֵר פִּתִּיתִי, דְּפָגִים לָהּ טְפֵי, שֶׁלַּעַז שֶׁל מְפֻתָּה גָּדוֹל וְסָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמִינָא דְּלֹא מְהֵימְנִינַן לֵיהּ לְהַחֲזִיק הַלַּעַז, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּנִיחָא לָהּ לְדִידָהּ לְהַחֲזִיק הַלַּעַז כְּדֵי לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר הַמָּמוֹן:


האומר פתיתי את בתו של פלוני – needless to say the person who states I raped [her], that he does not damage her so much for there is no such great evil talk of an outraged woman (i.e., who had been raped) for it is self-evident that he pays shame and damages by his own testimony [but rather] even a person who says, “I seduced [her], causes her greater damage for the evil talk of a seduced woman is greater, and you might think I would say that we don’t believe him to attach merit to the evil report, it comes to teach us that it is beneficial to her for herself to attach merit to the evil report in order to profit from the money


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם קְנָס.** דְּמוֹדֶה בִקְנָס פָּטוּר, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (שמות כב) אֲשֶׁר יַרְשִׁיעוּן אֱלֹהִים, פְּרָט לַמַּרְשִׁיעַ אֶת עַצְמוֹ:


ואינו משלם קנס – for he admits that with regard to a fine, he is exempt, as Scripture states (Exodus 22:8): “He whom God declares guilty [shall pay double to the other],” except for one who accuses himself (see Talmud Bava Kamma 64b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:3)


**הֵמִית שׁוֹרִי אֶת פְּלוֹנִי.** וַהֲרֵינִי חַיָּב בַּכֹּפֶר, מְשַׁלֵּם עַל פִּי עַצְמוֹ, דְּקָסָבַר כֻּפְרָא מָמוֹנָא:


המית שורי את פלוני – and behold I am liable for indemnity through his own [testimony], for he holds that the fine is an indemnification.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 3:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/3:9:4)


**עַבְדּוֹ שֶׁל פְּלוֹנִי.** וַהֲרֵינִי חַיָּב שְׁלֹשִׁים סֶלַע, פָּטוּר, דִּקְנָס נִינְהוּ, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ אֵינוֹ שָׁוֶה סֶלַע נוֹתֵן שְׁלֹשִׁים:


עבדו של פלוני – and behold I am liable thirty Selah, he is exempt, for they are a fine and even if it is not worth a Selah, he gives thirty [Selaim].



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:1)


**נַעֲרָה.** וְהַצַּעַר בַּתְּפוּסָה. וְהַצַּעַר נַמִּי לְאָבִיהָ בַּאֲנוּסָה:


נערה: והצער בתפוסה – and the “pain” also goes to her father with someone who was raped.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:2)


**מֵת הָאָב.** מִשֶּׁעָמְדָה בַדִּין בְּנַעֲרוּתָהּ, בֵּין בָּגְרָה קֹדֶם מִיתָה בֵּין לֹא בָגְרָה, הֲרֵי הֵן שֶׁל אַחִין, דְּכֵיוָן דְּעָמְדָה בַדִּין זָכָה בָהֶן אָב:


מת האב – from when she stood in court during her being a young woman, whether she became an adult prior to [her father’s] death or whether she had not become a young man, they belong to the brothers, for since he stood before the court, the father took possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:3)


**לֹא הִסְפִּיקָה לַעֲמֹד בַּדִּין וְכוּ'.** כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא עָמַד בַּדִּין לָאו מָמוֹן הוּא לְהוֹרִישׁוֹ לְבָנָיו:


לא הספיק לעמוד בדין וכו' – since he did not stand before the court, it is not his money to bequeath to his sons.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָמְדָה בַּדִּין לֹא הֲוֵי מָמוֹן דְּאָב לְהוֹרִישׁוֹ לְבָנָיו עַד דְּמָטֵי לִידֵיהּ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) וְנָתַן הָאִישׁ הַשּׁוֹכֵב עִמָּהּ לַאֲבִי הַנַּעֲרָה חֲמִשִּׁים כָּסֶף, לֹא זִכְּתָה תּוֹרָה לָאָב אֶלָּא מִשְּׁעַת נְתִינָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר – even though he stood in court, it is not the money of the father to bequeath to his sons until it reaches his hand, as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:29): The man who lay with her shall pay the girls father fifty [shekels of] silver.” The Torah did not cause the father to take possession until the time of the giving [of the money], but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:5)


**מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ.** שֶׁעָשְׂתָה בְּחַיֵּי אָבִיהָ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא גָבְתָה, כְּגוֹן שְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּה:


מעשה ידיה – that she did during the life of her father, even though she had no collected, [such as, for example], the salary/reward for work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:1:6)


**וּמְצִיאָתָהּ.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, מְצִיאָתָהּ מִמַּאן גַּבְיָא. וּמְשָׁנֵי, הָכִי קָאָמַר, מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ כִּמְצִיאָתָהּ, מַה מְּצִיאָתָהּ בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב לָאָב אַחַר מִיתַת הָאָב לְעַצְמָהּ, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁאֵין הָאַחִים זוֹכִים בְּמַה שֶּׁמָּצְאָה הַבַּת לְאַחַר מִיתַת הָאָב, כָּךְ מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ בְּחַיֵּי הָאָב לָאָב, וְזָכוּ בָהֶן הָאַחִים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא גָבְתָה שְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּתָהּ. אֲבָל מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ שֶׁלְּאַחַר מִיתַת הָאָב לְעַצְמָהּ, וְאֵין לָאַחִין זְכוּת בְּמַעֲשֵׂי יְדֵי הַבַּת שֶׁעוֹשָׂה לְאַחַר מִיתַת הָאָב:


ומציאתה – In the Gemara (Tractate Ketubot 43a), it raises the question: “from whom is she to collect anything she finds?” And it responds as such: this is what he said: her handiwork is like anything she finds. Just as whatever she finds during the lifetime of her father belongs to her father, and after the death of her father is hers, meaning to say, that the brothers do not take possession of what the daughter has found after the death of the father, so too her handiwork [earned] during the lifetime of her father, belongs to the father and the brothers took possession of it [after their father’s death], even though she did not collect the earnings of her labor, but her handiwork that comes after the death of the father is hers, and the brothers do not have possession of the handiwork of the daughter who performs it after the death of the father.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:1)


**כְּתֻבָּתָהּ שֶׁל אָב.** כְּתֻבָּתָהּ שֶׁהִיא גוֹבָה מִשְּׁנֵי אֵרוּסִין הַלָּלוּ. וְקָסָבַר יֵשׁ כְּתֻבָּה לַאֲרוּסָה. וּבִימֵי נַעֲרוּת וְקַטְנוּת קָאָמַר:


כתובתה של אב – Her Ketubah which she collects from these years of betrothal, and he (i.e., the anonymous Tanna/teacher of the Mishnah) holds that there is a Ketubah for a betrothed woman, and in the days of her being a young woman (i.e., ages 12-12 ½) and a minor girl is stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:2)


**כְּתֻבָּתָהּ שֶׁלָּהּ.** דְּמִשֶּׁהִשִּׂיאָהּ פָּקַע רְשׁוּתוֹ, וּבָתַר גּוֹבַיְנָא אָזְלִינַן, וְגוֹבַיְנָא בָּתַר הָכִי הוּא. וְלֹא אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר כְּתִיבָה לוֹמַר הוֹאִיל וְהָרִאשׁוֹנָה נִכְתְּבָה בְּעוֹדָהּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָאָב תֶּהֱוֵי דְּאָב:


כתובתה שלה – from when he (i.e., the father) married her off, his control has no legal effect and we follow after the collection and collection after this is what [occurs] and we don’t follow after the writing, to state that since, the first was written while she was under the control of her father, it should be for the father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:2:3)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל אָב.** דְּקָסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בָּתַר כְּתִיבָה אָזְלִינַן, וְהוֹאִיל וּכְתֻבָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה נִכְתְּבָה לִפְנֵי הַנִּשּׂוּאִין בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַבַּת בִּרְשׁוּת הָאָב, הַוְיָא דְּאָב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


הראשונה של אב – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that we follow after the writing, and since the first Ketubah was written before the marriages while the daughter was under the control of the father, it belongs to the father, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:1)


**וְזִנְּתָה.** מִן הָאֵרוּסִין וְהִיא נַעֲרָה:


וזנתה – [she fornicated] from the betrothal and she is a young woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:2)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ בְּחֶנֶק.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נִתְגַּיְּרָה פְּחוּתָה מִבַּת שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים דִּבְחֶזְקַת בְּתוּלָה הִיא. דְּכִי כְּתִיבָא סְקִילָה בְּנַעֲרָה מְאֹרָסָה בְּבַת יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּתִיב, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כב) כִּי עָשְׂתָה נְבָלָה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


הרי זו בחנק – and even she converted younger than the age of three years, for she is in the status of a virgin, for when it is written [in the Torah] regarding [the penalty of] stoning, it is written with regard to an engaged young woman, with the daughter of an Israelite, as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:21): “for she did a shameful thing in Israel, [committing fornication while under her father’s authority].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:3)


**וְלֹא מֵאָה סָלַע.** אִם נִמְצָא הַבַּעַל שַׁקְרָן. דְּכֻלָּהּ פָּרָשָׁה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּתִיבָא:


ולא מאה סלע – for if the husband was found to be lying, for the entire portion is written with regard to an Israelite (and not a convert).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:4)


**וְלֵדָתָהּ בִּקְדֻשָּׁה הֲרֵי זוֹ בִסְקִילָה.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא (שם) וּסְקָלוּהָ בָּאֲבָנִים וָמֵתָה, שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר וָמֵתָה אֶלָּא לְרַבּוֹת הוֹרָתָהּ שֶׁלֹּא בִקְדֻשָּׁה. וּמִיהוּ לִקְנָס לֹא מְרַבִּינַן לָהּ, דִּלְמִיתָה נִתְרַבֵּית וְלֹא לִקְנָס:


ולידתה בקדושה ה"ז סקילה – as Scripture states (Deuteronomy 22:21): “[and the men of the town] shall stone her to death,” for it does not need to say, “that she dies,” but rather, to include her conception that was not in a state of sanctity; but however, for a fine, we don’t include her, since for death she is included, but not for a fine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:5)


**יֶשׁ לָהּ אָב.** בְּיִשְׂרְאֵלִית קָמַיְרֵי:


יש לה אב – we are speaking about an Israelite woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:3:6)


**וְאֵין לָהּ פֶּתַח בֵּית אָב.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאֵין לָאָב בַּיִת:


ואין לה פתח בית אביה – as for example, when the father does not have a house.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:1)


**הָאָב זַכַּאי בְבִתּוֹ.** בְּקַטְנוּתָהּ וּבְנַעֲרוּתָהּ:


האב זכאי בבתו – when she is a minor and when she is a young woman (i.e., from age twelve and one-day to twelve and one-half years of age).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:2)


**בְּקִדּוּשֶׁיהָ בַּכֶּסֶף.** שֶׁכֶּסֶף קִדּוּשֶׁיהָ שֶׁלּוֹ, דִּכְתִיב בָּאָמָה הָעִבְרִיָּה (שמות כא) וְיָצְאָה חִנָּם אֵין כָּסֶף, וְדָרְשִׁינַן, אֵין כֶּסֶף לְאָדוֹן זֶה, כְּלוֹמַר לָאָדוֹן שֶׁקְּנָאָהּ שֶׁיָּצְאָה מֵאִתּוֹ חִנָּם בְּסִימָנֵי נַעֲרוּת, אֲבָל יֵשׁ כֶּסֶף לְאָדוֹן אַחֵר, וּמַנּוּ, אָב, שֶׁכֶּסֶף קִדּוּשֶׁיהָ שֶׁלּוֹ אֲפִלּוּ כְּשֶׁהִיא נַעֲרָה עַד שֶׁתִּבָּגֵר:


בקידושיה בכסף – for the money of her betrothal is his (i.e., the father’s) as it is written concerning the Hebrew maidservant (Exodus 21:11): “she shall go free, without payment,” and we expound, that there is no money to this master, meaning to say to the master who purchased her that she left him gratuitously/for no reason with the signs of young womanhood, but if another master has money and the father appointed him, the money of her betrothal/sanctification is his, eve when she is a young woman until she becomes an adult woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:3)


**בַּשְּׁטָר וּבַבִּיאָה.** שֶׁמְּקַבֵּל שְׁטַר קִדּוּשִׁין עָלֶיהָ וּמוֹסְרָהּ לְבִיאָה לְשֵׁם קִדּוּשִׁין לְמִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, דִּכְתִיב וְהָיְתָה, אִתַּקּוּשׁ הֲוָיוֹת לַהֲדָדֵי, מַה כֶּסֶף שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מִן הַהֲוָיוֹת, בִּרְשׁוּת אָבִיהָ, אַף קִדּוּשֵׁי שְׁטָר וּבִיאָה בִּרְשׁוּת אָבִיהָ:


בשטר ובביאה – that he receives the betrothal document on her behalf and hands her over for sexual intercourse for the sake of betrothal to whomever he desires, as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:2): “[she leaves his household] and becomes the wife of another man” (Tosafot Yom Tov omits the first part of the Biblical verse); there is an analogy of the word הויות/becomes – to each other just as “money” is one of the הויות/becomes – in the domain of her father, so also the “document” and “sexual intercourse” while she is in the domain of her father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:4)


**בִּמְצִיאָתָהּ.** מִשּׁוּם אֵיבָה:


במציאתה – because of enmity/aversion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:5)


**וּבְמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וְכִי יִמְכֹּר אִישׁ אֶת בִּתּוֹ לְאָמָה, מַה אָמָה מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ לְרַבָּהּ, אַף בַּת מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ לְאָבִיהָ:


ובמעשה ידיה – as it is written (Exodus 21:7): “When a man sells his daughter as a slave, [she shall not be feed as male slaves are].” Just as a maid servant, her handiwork belongs to her master, also the daughter, her handiwork belongs to her father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:6)


**וּבַהֲפָרַת נְדָרֶיהָ.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ל) בִּנְעוּרֶיהָ בֵּית אָבִיהָ:


ובהפרת נדריה – as it is written (Numbers 30:17): “[and his daughter] while in her father’s household by reason of her youth.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:7)


**וּמְקַבֵּל גִּטָּהּ.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְיָצְאָה וְהָיְתָה, אִיתַּקַּשׁ יְצִיאָה לַהֲוָיָה, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהָאָב מְקַבֵּל קִדּוּשֶׁיהָ בְּקַטְנוּתָהּ וְנַעֲרוּתָהּ, כָּךְ מְקַבֵּל גִּטָּהּ:


ומקבל גיטה – as it is written (Deuteronomy 24;2) “she leaves [his household] and becomes [the wife of another man.” An analogy is made between [“leaving” to “becoming]; just as the father receives her betrothal while she is a minor and during her period as a young woman, so too he receives her bill of divorcement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:8)


**וְאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת בְּחַיֶּיהָ.** אִם נָפְלוּ לָהּ קַרְקָעוֹת מִבֵּית אֲבִי אִמָּהּ, אֵין אָבִיהָ אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹתֵיהֶן בְּחַיֶּיהָ, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מֵתָה וְהוּא יוֹרְשָׁהּ:


ואינו אוכל פירות בחייה – if land fell to her from the house of the father of her mother, her father cannot consume their fruits during her lifetime other than if she dies and he inherits her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:9)


**יָתֵר עָלָיו הַבַּעַל.** שֶׁהוּא זוֹכֶה בְּכָל הַשְּׁנוּיִים לְמַעְלָה שֶׁהָאָב זַכַּאי בְּבִתּוֹ, וְאוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ בִּירֻשָּׁה מִשֶּׁנִּשֵּׂאת לוֹ:


יתר עליו הבעל – for he takes possession of all of what is taught above that the father retains control, and he consumes the fruits of the property that fell to her in inheritance once she married him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:10)


**וּבְפִרְקוֹנָהּ.** אִם נִשְׁבֵּית חַיָּב לִפְדּוֹתָהּ:


ובפרקונה – if she is taken captive, he is liable to redeem her.
