###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:11)


**בִּקְבוּרָתָהּ.** שֶׁחֲכָמִים תִּקְּנוּ קְבוּרָתָהּ תַּחַת יְרוּשָׁתָהּ שֶׁהוּא יוֹרְשָׁהּ אִם מֵתָה:


בקבורתה – The Sages established her burial in place of her inheritance for he inherits her if she dies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:4:12)


**מִשְּׁנֵי חֲלִילִים.** לְהֶסְפֵּד:


משני חלילים – for a eulogy.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:1)


**לְעוֹלָם הִיא בִרְשׁוּת הָאָב.** אִם בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל מְאֹרֶסֶת לְכֹהֵן הִיא אֵינָהּ אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הִגִּיעַ הַזְּמַן שֶׁקָּבְעוּ לַנִּשּׂוּאִין וְלֹא נִשֵּׂאת שֶׁהַבַּעַל חַיָּב בִּמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ אֵינָהּ אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה:


לעולם היא ברשות האב – if an Israelite woman is betrothed to a Kohen she does not eat Terumah/priest’s due, and even if the time arrived that they established for marriage and she did not get married, as the husband is liable for her sustenance she cannot eat Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:2)


**עַד שֶׁתִּכָּנֵס לַחֻפָּה.** לְשֵׁם נִשּׂוּאִין, שֶׁתְּהֵא מְסוּרָה לִרְשׁוּת הַבַּעַל:


עד שתכנס לחופה – for the sake of marriage that she will be handed over to the domain of the husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:5:3)


**מָסְרוּ שְׁלוּחֵי הָאָב.** שֶׁפָּגְעוּ שְׁלוּחֵי הָאָב בִּשְׁלוּחֵי הַבַּעַל וּמְסָרוּהָ לָהֶם:


שלוחי האב – that the agents of the father ra into the agents of the husband and handed her over to re su



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:1)


**הָאָב אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בִּמְזוֹנוֹת בִּתּוֹ.** בְּחַיָּיו. דְּאִלּוּ לְאַחַר מִיתָה נִזּוֹנוֹת הַבָּנוֹת מִנְּכָסָיו מִתְּנַאי כְּתֻבָּה. וּבִתּוֹ לָאו דַּוְקָא, דְּהוּא הַדִּין בְּנוֹ, שֶׁאֵין הָאָב חַיָּב לָזוּן לֹא בָנָיו וְלֹא בְנוֹתָיו כְּשֶׁהֵם קְטַנִּים, אֶלָּא אִם הוּא גַבְרָא דְאָמִיד, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהוּא אִישׁ עָשִׁיר, בֵּית דִּין נוֹטְלִים מִמֶּנּוּ בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁכּוֹפִין כָּל שְׁאָר בְּנֵי הָעִיר עַל הַצְּדָקָה וּמְפַרְנְסִים בָּהּ בָּנָיו וּבְנוֹתָיו הַקְּטַנִּים, וְאִם אֵינוֹ גַבְרָא דְאָמִיד בֵּית דִּין מְחָרְפִים אוֹתוֹ וּמוֹדִיעִים אוֹתוֹ שֶׁאַכְזָרִיּוּת הִיא לוֹ אִם לֹא יְפַרְנֵס אֶת בָּנָיו, וְשֶׁהוּא פָּחוּת מִן הַחַיּוֹת הַמִּדְבָּרִיּוֹת שֶׁהֵם מְרַחֲמִין עַל בְּנֵיהֶם, אֲבָל אֵין יְכוֹלִין לְכוֹפוֹ עַל זֶה, בֵּין שֶׁהַבָּנִים זְכָרִים בֵּין נְקֵבוֹת. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁהַבָּנִים קְטַנִּים, אֲבָל קְטַנֵּי קְטַנִּים וְהֵם הַפְּחוּתִים מִבְּנֵי שֵׁשׁ, בֵּית דִּין כּוֹפִין אֶת הָאָב וּמוֹצִיאִים מִיָּדוֹ בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ כְּדֵי לְפַרְנֵס בָּנָיו שֶׁהֵם קְטַנֵּי קְטַנִּים, אֶחָד זְכָרִים וְאֶחָד נְקֵבוֹת:


האב אינו חייב במזונות בתו – during his lifetime [the father is not liable for her sustenance], whereas after [his] death, the daughters are supported from his property from the stipulations of the Ketubah (i.e., Jewish marriage contract) [and his daughter] not specifically, for the same law applies to his son, for the father is not liable to support/feed neither his sons nor his daughters when they are minors, but rather, if he is a man believed to be wealthy, meaning to say, that he is a rich man, the Jewish court takes from against his will in the manner that they force all the rest of the people of the city regarding tzedakah/charity and through it they support his minor age sons and daughters, but if he is not believed to be wealthy, the Jewish court shames/reviles him and announces to him that it is cruelty to him if he does not support his children, and that he is worse than the desert creatures who have compassion upon the offspring, ut they cannot compel him on this, whether his children are male or female and especially when the sons are young, but the youngest of the young and they are the least of the children of Shesh, the Jewish court forces the father and removes [funds] from his hand, against his will to support his children who are he smallest of the small, both the boys and the girls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:2)


**בַּכֶּרֶם.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹשְׁבִים שׁוּרוֹת שׁוּרוֹת כְּכֶרֶם:


בכרם – on account that they would sit row by row like a vineyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:3)


**הַבָּנִים יִירְשׁוּ וְהַבָּנוֹת יִזּוֹנוּ.** מִתְּנַאי כְּתֻבָּה, שֶׁהַבָּנִים זְכָרִים יִירְשׁוּ כְּתֻבַּת אִמָּן וְהַבָּנוֹת נְקֵבוֹת יִזּוֹנוּ מִנְּכָסָיו:


הבנים ירשו והבנות יזונו (see Tractate Bava Batra, Chapter 9, Mishnah 1 and Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 13, Mishnah 3, where this phrase appears as well) – from the provisions of the Ketubah that the male children will inherit the Ketubah of their mother and the female children will be supported from his (i.e., the father’s) property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:4)


**מָה הַבָּנִים.** זְכָרִים:


מת הבנים – the male [children] die,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:5)


**אֵין יוֹרְשִׁין.** כְּתֻבַּת אִמָּן אֶלָּא לְאַחַר מִיתַת אֲבִיהֶן:


[the daughters] do not inherit the Ketubah of their mother, other than after the death of their father;


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:6:6)


**כָּךְ הַבָּנוֹת אֵינָן נִזּוֹנוֹת.** מִנִּכְסֵי הָאָב מִכֹּחַ תְּנַאי כְּתֻבָּה אֶלָּא לְאַחַר מִיתַת אֲבִיהֶן:


similarly, the daughters are not supported from the property of the father from the strength of the provisions of the Ketubah other than until after the death of their father.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:7:1)


**וְלֹא כָתַב לָהּ כָּל נִכְסִים כוּ'‏ חַיָּב.** לִהְיוֹת כָּל נְכָסָיו אַחֲרָאִין לָהּ. וְלֹא יָכוֹל לְמֵימַר אֵין לִיךְ אֶלָּא שָׂדֶה הַכְּתוּבָה לִיךְ בִּכְתֻבְּתִיךְ:


ולא כתב לה כל נכסים כו' חייב – to make all of his property is a surety to her, and he is not able to say, “you don’t have other than the field that is written to you in your Ketubah.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:8:1)


**וּבְכֹהֶנֶת.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְקַיְּמָהּ מִשֶּׁנִּשְׁבֵּית, שֶׁאֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן אֲסוּרָה לְבַעְלָהּ מִשֶּׁנֶּאֶנְסָה, כּוֹתֵב לָהּ אֶפְרְקִנָּךְ וַאֲהַדְרִנָּךְ לִמְדִינְתָּךְ. וְחַיָּב לָתֵת לָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ צָרִיךְ לְגָרְשָׁהּ:


ובכהנת – for he cannot uphold her [as his wife] once she is taken captive, for the wife of a Kohen is prohibited to her husband once she is raped, he writes to her, “I will redeem you and return you to your country,” and he is obligated to give her the Ketubah settlement and even though that against his will, he needs to divorce her.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:1)


**אֵינוֹ רַשָּׁאי.** שֶׁכְּבָר נִתְחַיֵּב בְּפִרְקוֹנָהּ מִשֶּׁנִּשְׁבֵּית:


אינו רשאי – for he is already obligated to redeem her when she is taken captive.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:2)


**חַיָּב לְרַפֹּאתָהּ.** שֶׁהָרְפוּאָה כִּמְזוֹנוֹת:


חייב לרפאותה – for healing is like food/support.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:9:3)


**רַשָּׁאי.** שֶׁאֵין חַיָּב לָזוּן גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ:


רשאי – for he is not obligated to support/feed his divorced wife.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:10:1)


**יָתֵר עַל חוּלְקֵיהוֹן דְּעִם אֲחוּהוֹן.** אִם תָּמוּתִי בְּחַיַּי וְאִירָשֵׁךְ, יִטְּלוּ בָנַיִךְ כְּתֻבָּתֵךְ אַחֲרֵי מוֹתִי כְּשֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לַחֲלֹק בִּנְכָסַי עִם בָּנִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ לִי מֵאִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת. וְנַפְקָא מִנָּהּ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁכְּתֻבָּה שֶׁל זוֹ מְרֻבָּה מִשֶּׁל אַחֶרֶת אוֹ שֶׁבְּנֵי הָאִשָּׁה אַחֶרֶת מְרֻבִּים מִבָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל זֹאת, וְטוֹב לָאֵלּוּ שֶׁיִּטְּלוּ כְּתֻבַּת אִמָּן וַאֲפִלּוּ הַכְּתֻבּוֹת שָׁווֹת. וְאֵין כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין נִגְבֵּית אֲפִלּוּ בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה אֶלָּא מִמְּקַרְקְעֵי, אֲבָל לֹא מִמְּטַלְטְלֵי. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהִנִּיחַ הָאָב שִׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי הַכְּתֻבּוֹת וְדִינָר יוֹתֵר, אָז יוֹרְשִׁין הַבָּנִים כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין, הוֹאִיל וְנִשְׁאָר מָקוֹם לִירֻשָּׁה שֶׁל תּוֹרָה, שֶׁאַחַר שֶׁיִּטֹּל כָּל אֶחָד מֵהַבָּנִים כְּתֻבַּת אִמּוֹ נִשְׁאָר דִּינָר לַחֲלֹק וְלִירָשׁ בְּנִכְסֵי אֲבִיהֶן, אֲבָל אִם לֹא הִנִּיחַ הָאָב אֶלָּא כְשִׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי הַכְּתֻבּוֹת אוֹ פָּחוֹת מִזֶּה חוֹלְקִים הַנְּכָסִים בְּשָׁוֶה וְאֵין כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין נוֹהֶגֶת בָּהֶם:


יתר על חולקיהום דעם אחוהון – if you die during my life and I betroth you, your children will take your Ketubah after my death when they will come to divide my possessions with the children that I will have from another wife, and the practical difference, for example, is that the Ketubah of this one is greater than that of the other wife, or the children of the other wife are more numerous from the children of this one and it is good for these that they will take the Ketubah of their mother and even if the Ketubot are equivalent for there is not written that the male issue collect, other than from the landed properties, but not from movable properties, and especially where the father left a measure of two of the Ketubot and one Dinar more, then the children inherit the Ketubah of male issue since there remains a place for the inheritance of the Torah for after each one of the children will take the Ketubah of his mother, there remains one Dinar to divide and to inherit the property of their father, but if the father did not leave other than according to the measure of two Ketubot or less than this, they divide the property equally and the Ketubah of male issue is not practiced by them.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:11:1)


**עַד דְּתִנַּסְּבָן.** עַד שֶׁיִּתְאָרְסוּ. וְאִם בָּגְרוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְאָרְסוּ אֵין לָהֶם מְזוֹנוֹת, דְּקַיְמָא לָן בּוֹגֶרֶת אֵין לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת:


עד דתתנסבן – until they will become betrothed, and if they became adult women, even though they had not become betrothed, they have no support /food, for we hold that an adult woman has no support.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:12:1)


**מִגַּד אַלְמְנוּתִיךְ.** מֶשֶׁךְ אַלְמְנוּתִיךְ:


מיגד אלמנותיך – during your widowhood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/4:12:2)


**אַנְשֵׁי יְהוּדָה כוֹתְבִין כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַנְשֵׁי יְהוּדָה, אֶלָּא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִשֵּׂאת וְשֶׁלֹּא תָבְעָה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ בְּבֵית דִּין נִזּוֹנִית מִשֶּׁל בַּעְלָהּ וְדָרָה בַּבַּיִת שֶׁהָיְתָה דָרָה בְּחַיֵּי בַּעְלָהּ וּמִשְׁתַּמֶּשֶׁת בְּכָל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁהָיְתָה מִשְׁתַּמֶּשֶׁת בָּהֶן בְּחַיֵּי בַּעְלָהּ:


אנשי יהודה כותבין כו' – but the Halakha is not like the men of Judea but rather, all the while that she is not married and she did not claim her Ketubah in the Jewish court, she is supported from her husband and lives in the house that she lived in during the life of her husband, and uses all the utensils that she would use during the life of her husband.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:1)


**אַף עַל פִּי.** אִם רָצָה לְהוֹסִיף אֲפִלּוּ מֵאָה מָנֶה יוֹסִיף. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁאֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא לְבַיֵּשׁ אֶת מִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ:


אף על פי. אם רצה להוסיף אפי' מאה אמה יוסיף (this fragment quotes Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 4, Mishnah 7) – and we don’t say that he is not permitted to in order as to not embarrass whomever that lacks it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא כָתַב לָהּ.** תּוֹסֶפֶת מִדַּעְתּוֹ, אֶלָּא עַל מְנָת לְכָנְסָהּ:


שלא כתב לה – the supplement from his knowledge/desire, other than on the condition of marrying her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:3)


**וְהִיא כוֹתֶבֶת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַבְּלָה מוֹחֶלֶת וְכוֹתֶבֶת שֶׁנִּתְקַבְּלָה:


והיא כותבת – even though she did not receive [everything], she pardons and writes that she received it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:1:4)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר בִּגְזֵרוֹתָיו:


רמ"א כו' – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir in his decrees (i.e., that a virgin receives two hundred Zuz and a widow receives 100 Maneh or else it is fornication).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:1)


**נוֹתְנִין לִבְתוּלָה.** זְמַן לְכָנְסָהּ לַחֻפָּה מִיּוֹם שֶׁתְּבָעָהּ הַבַּעַל לְאַחַר שֶׁקִּדְּשָׁהּ, לְהַזְהִירָהּ עַל עִסְקֵי חֻפָּה לְהָכִין תַּכְשִׁיטֶיהָ:


נותנין לבתולה – the time to bring her into the canopy from the day that the husband demanded her after he betrothed her to warn her on the mattes the Huppah to prepare her ornaments/possessions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:2)


**לְפַרְנֵס עַצְמָהּ.** בְּתַכְשִׁיטֶיהָ שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (בראשית כד) תֵּשֵׁב הַנַּעֲרָה אִתָּנוּ יָמִים, וּמַאי יָמִים, שָׁנָה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כד) יָמִים תִּהְיֶה גְּאֻלָּתוֹ:


לפרנס עצמה – with her ornaments/possessions for twelve months, as it is written (Genesis 24:55): “Let the maiden remain with us some days;” And what are “days” – a year, as it is written (Leviticus 25:29): “[If a man sells a dwelling house in a walled city, I may be redeemed until a year has elapsed since its sale;] the redemption period shall be a year.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:3)


**לְפַרְנֵס עַצְמוֹ.** בְּצָרְכֵי סְעֻדָּה וְחֻפָּה:


לפרנס עצמו – the needs of the meal and the Huppah/marriage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:4)


**וּלְאַלְמָנָה שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ טוֹרַחַת כָּל כָּךְ בְּתַכְשִׁיטֶיהָ, שֶׁכְּבָר יֵשׁ בְּיָדָהּ:


ולאלמנה שלשים יום – who does not trouble [herself] so much with her ornaments because they are already in her hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:5)


**וְלֹא נִשָּׂאוּ.** שֶׁהַבְּעָלִים מְעַכְּבִים. וְאַיְדֵי דְתָנָא רֵישָׁא בְּדִידְהִי תָּנָא סֵיפָא נַמִּי בְּדִידְהִי:


ולא נשאו – for the owners delay and since the Tanna/teacher in the first segment taught about her, the end of the Mishnah also teaches about her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:6)


**וְאוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה.** וְאִם כֹּהֵן הוּא וְהִיא יִשְׂרְאֵלִית, שֶׁמִּשָּׁעָה שֶׁקִּדְּשָׁהּ אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה, דִּכְתִיב (שם כב) וְכֹהֵן כִּי יִקְנֶה נֶפֶשׁ קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ, וְהַאי קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ הוּא, אֶלָּא דְּרַבָּנָן גָּזוּר עַל בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל הַמְאֹרֶסֶת לְכֹהֵן שֶׁלֹּא תֹאכַל בַּתְּרוּמָה, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְזְגוּ לָהּ כּוֹס בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ וְתַשְׁקֶה לְאַחֶיהָ וּלְאַחְיוֹתֶיהָ, וּכְשֶׁהִגִּיעַ הַזְּמַן וְלֹא נִשֵּׂאת, שֶׁאוֹכֶלֶת מִשֶּׁלּוֹ, אֵינָהּ אוֹכֶלֶת בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ, אֶלָּא בַּעְלָהּ כֹּהֵן מְיַחֵד לָהּ מָקוֹם וּמַאֲכִילָהּ שָׁם, וְלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזָר תּוּ שֶׁמָּא תַשְׁקֶה מִכּוֹס שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה לְאַחֶיהָ וּלְאַחְיוֹתֶיהָ:


ואוכלת בתרומה – if he is a Kohen, and she is an Israelite, that from the time that he betrothed her, she consumes the Terumah/Priest’s due from Torah law, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): “But a person who is a priest’s property by purchase may eat of them.” And this person who is [the priest’s] property by purchase , but the Rabbis decreed upon an Israelite woman who is betrothed to a Kohen should not consume Terumah as a decree lest he offer her a cup to drink in her father’s house and she will give her some to her brothers and her sisters, and when the time arrives and she hasn’t married, she eats from his food, and does not eat in her father’s house, other than if her husband who is a Kohen designates a place and feeds her there, but there is no further decree lest she provide drink from a cup of Terumah to her brothers or to her sisters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:7)


**נוֹתְנִים לָהּ הַכֹּל תְּרוּמָה.** אִם יִרְצֶה. וּכְשֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ יְמֵי טֻמְאָתָהּ תִּמְכְּרֶנָּה וְתִקַּח חֻלִּין:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:2:8)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר מֶחֱצָה חֻלִּין.** לֶאֱכֹל בִּימֵי טֻמְאָתָהּ:


ר' עקיבא אומר מחצה חולין – to eat during her impurity.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:1)


**הַיָּבָם אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** בְּעוֹדָהּ שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וְכֹהֵן כִּי יִקְנֶה נֶפֶשׁ קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ, וְהַאי קִנְיַן אָחִיו הוּא:


היבם אינו מאכיל בתרומה – while she is a widow waiting for her brother-in-law to marry or reject her, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): “But a person who is a priest’s property by purchase [may eat of them],” but this is the purchase of his brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:2)


**עָשְׂתָה שִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים בִּפְנֵי הַבַּעַל.** מֵהָנָךְ שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ הַקְּבוּעִים לָהּ מִשֶּׁתְּבָעָהּ הַבַּעַל:


עשתה ששה חדשים בפני הבעל – from these twelve months that are fixed for her from when the husband makes a claim upon her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:3)


**אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ כֻלָּן בִּפְנֵי הַבַּעַל וְאֶחָד בִּפְנֵי הַיָּבָם.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּרֻבָּא בִּפְנֵי הַבַּעַל, סוֹף סוֹף לֹא נִתְחַיֵּב בִּמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ בְּחַיָּיו. וְהוּא הַדִּין נַמִּי אִם נִתְחַיֵּב בְּחַיָּיו לֹא אָכְלָה מִשֶּׁמֵּת, דְּקִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ פָּקַע, אֶלָּא דְּאִי כֻלָּם בִּפְנֵי הַבַּעַל הֲוָה אָכְלָה מִיהָא בְּחַיָּיו:


או אפי' כולן בפני הבעל ואחד בפני ביבם – even though that most is in the presence of the husband, inevitably, he is not liable for her support during his life, and the same law applies also if he had been liable during his lifetime, she did not consume once e died, for the property by purchase bursts open, but rather, if all of it was in the presence of the husband, she would consume anyway during his lifetime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:4)


**אוֹ כֻלָּן בִּפְנֵי הַיָּבָם.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר אִם הָיוּ כֻלָּן בִּפְנֵי הַיָּבָם:


או כולן בפני היבם – and one does not have to say if all of them were in the presence of the levir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:5)


**זוֹ מִשְׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה.** דְּמִשֶּׁהִגִּיעַ זְמַן אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה:


זו נשנה ראשונה – that when the time arrives, she eats priest’s due/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:3:6)


**אֵין הָאִשָּׁה אוֹכֶלֶת.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִמְצָא בָהּ מוּם וְנִמְצֵאת זָרָה לְמַפְרֵעַ, שֶׁהָיָה מִקְחוֹ מֶקַּח טָעוּת. וּלְמִשְׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְצָא בָהּ מוּם לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ נַמִּי שֶׁמָּא תַשְׁקֶה לְאַחֶיהָ וּלְאַחְיוֹתֶיהָ, דְּהָא מְיַחֵד לָהּ דּוּכְתָּא, הִלְכָּךְ שָׁרֵי לָהּ לֶאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה מִשֶּׁהִגִּיעַ זְמָן:


אין האשה אוכלת – for we suspect lest there is found in her a defect and she is found to be a foreigner retroactively that his business transaction had been in error. And regarding the “First Mishnah,” lest they find in her a defect and we don’t take into consideration for there is no reason to fear that she would have her brothers and sisters drink it for he sets aside for a place for her; therefore, they permit her to eat Terumah/priest’s due when the time arrives.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:1)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ עוֹשָׂה וְאוֹכֶלֶת.** שֶׁתִּקְּנוּ מְזוֹנוֹת תַּחַת מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ, לְפִיכָךְ עוֹשָׂה וְאוֹכֶלֶת לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל:


הרי זו עושה ואוכלת – for they degreed support in place of the work of her hands/her labor (i.e., her wages), therefore she works and consumes according to the words of everyone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:2)


**הַמּוֹתָר.** הִקְדִּישׁ אֶת מוֹתַר מַעֲשֵׂה יְדֵי אִשְׁתּוֹ מַה שֶּׁהִיא עוֹשָׂה יוֹתֵר עַל הָרָאוּי לִמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ, וְלֹא הִקְדִּישׁ מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ עַצְמָן:


המותר – he dedicated to the Temple the excess of his wife’s wages, what she does more than what is appropriate for her support and did not dedicate to the Temple her wages themselves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:4:3)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר הֶקְדֵּשׁ.** דְּסָבַר אָדָם מַקְדִּישׁ דָּבָר שֶׁלֹּא בָא לָעוֹלָם. וְאֵין כֵּן הֲלָכָה, אֶלָּא הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן הַסַּנְדְלָר דְּאָמַר חֻלִּין, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מַקְדִּישׁ דָּבָר שֶׁלֹּא בָא לָעוֹלָם:


ר' מאיר אומר הקדש – for he holds that a person who dedicates something [to the Temple] that did not ever exist, and this is not the Halakha, but rather, the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yohanan ben HaSandlar who stated that it is non-holy/unconsecrated, for a person does not dedicate/sanctify something that never existed.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:1)


**טוֹחֶנֶת.** בְּרֵחַיִם שֶׁל יָד שֶׁהֵם קְטַנִּים. וְאִם רֵחַיִם גְּדוֹלִים הֵם, מְכִינָה כָּל צָרְכֵי הַטְּחִינָה, כְּגוֹן נוֹתַנְתּוֹ בָּאֲפַרְכֶּסֶת וְקוֹלֶטֶת הַקֶּמַח:


טוחנת – with hand millstones which are small and if they are large millstones, she prepares all the needs of the grinding as for example, she places it in the grain-receiver/hopper and receives the grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:2)


**הִכְנִיסָה לוֹ שִׁפְחָה אַחַת.** מָעוֹת אוֹ נְכָסִים כְּדֵי לִקְנוֹת בָּהֶם שִׁפְחָה אַחַת:


הכניסה לו שפחה אחת – money or property in order to purchase one maidservant.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:3)


**יוֹשֶׁבֶת בַּקַּתֶּדְרָא.** עַל כִּסֵּא מְנוּחָה וְאֵינָהּ הוֹלֶכֶת בִּשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ לְכָאן וּלְכָאן, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן מוֹזֶגֶת לוֹ אֶת הַכּוֹס וּמַצַּעַת לוֹ אֶת הַמִּטָּה וְרוֹחֶצֶת לוֹ פָּנָיו יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו, שֶׁמְּלָאכוֹת אֵלּוּ אֵין מִשְׁתַּמְּשִׁים בָּהֶם בְּאִשָּׁה אֶלָּא בְּאִשְׁתּוֹ בִּלְבָד:


יושבת בקתדרא – on a resting/lounge chair and she does not go on his errands to here or there and even though she pours for him the cup [for drinks] and makes the bed for him and washes his face, hands and feet, for these labors, they are not performed by a woman other than his wife alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:5:4)


**שִׁעֲמוּם.** בֶּהָלָה. תַּרְגּוּם וּבְתִמְהוֹן לֵבָב (דברים כח) וּבְשַׁעֲמִימוּת לִבָּא. וּפְלוּגְתַּיְהוּ דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל הֲוֵי בְּאִשָּׁה שֶׁאֵינָה בְּטֵלָה אֶלָּא מְשַׂחֶקֶת בְּמִינֵי הַצְּחוֹק, דְּלִידֵי זִמָּה אִיכָּא, לִידֵי שִׁעְמוּם לֵיכָּא, שֶׁאֵין שִׁעְמוּם אֶלָּא בְּיוֹשֵׁב וְתוֹהֶה וּבָטֵל לְגַמְרֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


שיעמום – shock/sudden calamity, which is the Aramaic translation of (Deuteronomy 28:28): “and dismay” is and sudden calamity of the heart, and the dispute of Rabbi Eliezer and Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel regards a woman who is not idle, but rather engages in all sorts of merriment and there is lewdness involved. But there isn’t boredom/shock for there is no boredom but rather with one who sits and gazes/is astonished and is completely idle, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:1)


**הַמַּדִּיר אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר יֵאָסֵר הֲנָאַת תַּשְׁמִישֵׁךְ עָלַי. אֲבָל הֲנָאַת תַּשְׁמִישִׁי אָסוּר עָלַיִךְ, לֹא מִתַּסְרָא, דְּהָא מְשֻׁעְבָּד לָהּ, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וְעוֹנָתָהּ לֹא יִגְרָע:


המדיר את אשתו מתשמיש המטה – aa for example, that he said: the benefit of your sexual intercourse will be forbidden upon me, but the benefit of my sexual intercourse will be forbidden to you; he is not forbidden for he is subjugated to her, as it is written (Exodus 21:10): “he must not withhold her conjugal rights.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת.** אִם הִדִּירָהּ שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת תַּמְתִּין, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּיוֹלֶדֶת נְקֵבָה שֶׁטְּמֵאָה שְׁבוּעַיִם:


ב"ש אומרים שתי שבתות – if he took a vow for two weeks, she should wait, for such as we found with a woman who gives birth to a female who is ritually impure for two weeks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים שַׁבָּת אֶחָת.** שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בַּנִּדָּה שֶׁטְּמֵאָה שִׁבְעָה. וְגָמְרִינַן מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ, הַכַּעַס שֶׁכּוֹעֵס אָדָם עַל אִשְׁתּוֹ וּמַדִּירָהּ דְּהוּא מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ, מִנִּדָּה שֶׁהוּא מִידֵי דִשְׁכִיחַ, לַאֲפוֹקֵי לֵדָה דְּלֹא שְׁכִיחַ כֻּלֵּי הַאי. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי גָּמְרִינַן מִידֵי דְּהוּא גָּרַם לָהּ, נֶדֶר הָאִישׁ שֶׁהוּא גוֹרֵם לָהּ לִשְׁהוֹת, מִלֵּדָה שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ בָאָה לָהּ. לַאֲפוֹקֵי נִדָּה דְּמִמֵּילָא קָאָתֵי לָהּ. וְיוֹתֵר עַל שַׁבָּת אַחַת לְבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹ שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה גַּמָּל שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ לִשְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם אוֹ סַפָּן שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ לְשִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים:


וב"ה אומרים שבת אחת – for such we have found with a menstruant woman who is ritually impure for seven [days] and we learn something that is frequent. The anger [that a person expresses] over his wife and takes a vow concerning her is something that is frequent, [which we learn] from the menstruant woman something that is frequent, to exclude giving birth which is not all that frequent. But the School of Shammai holds that we learn something that he caused to her. The vow of a man which he causes her to wait/delay from giving birth, which is upon his hand comes upon her, to exclude the menstruant woman that of its own comes to her. And more than one week according to the School of Hillel and two weeks according to the School of Shammai, he should divorce [her] and give [her] the Ketubah settlement, and even if he was a camel driver whose period is thirty days or a sailor whose period is sixth months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:4)


**הַתַּלְמִידִים וְכוּ'‏ שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר הִיא. וּפְלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ וְאוֹמְרִים אֲפִלּוּ שְׁתַּיִם וְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


התלמידים וכו' שלשים יום – and our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Eliezer and the Rabbis dispute it and state even two or three years, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:5)


**הַטַּיָּלִים.** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם מְלָאכָה וְלֹא סְחוֹרָה:


הטיילים – who have no work nor business.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:6)


**הַחַמָּרִים.** שֶׁיּוֹצְאִים לַכְּפָרִים לְהָבִיא תְּבוּאָה:


החמרים – who go out to the villages to bring grain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:7)


**הַגַּמָּלִים.** מְבִיאִין חֲבִילוֹת עַל הַגְּמַלִּים מִמָּקוֹם רָחוֹק:


הגמלים – who bring packages on the camels from a place far away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:6:8)


**הַסַּפָּנִים.** הַמְפָרְשִׁים לַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל. וּמִי שֶׁהָיָה בִתְחִלָּתוֹ בַּעַל אֻמָּנוּת שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ קְרוֹבָה וּבִקֵּשׁ לֵעָשׂוֹת בַּעַל אֻמָּנוּת שֶׁעוֹנָתוֹ רְחוֹקָה, אִשְׁתּוֹ מְעַכֶּבֶת עָלָיו, חוּץ מִתַּלְמוּד תּוֹרָה, שֶׁאֵין הָאִשָּׁה יְכוֹלָה לְעַכֵּב עַל בַּעְלָהּ שֶׁהָיָה טַיָּל אוֹ פּוֹעֵל שֶׁלֹּא יֵעָשֶׂה תַּלְמִיד חָכָם:


הספנים – who go on a voyage to the Great Sea (Mediterranean). He who his beginning was the master of a craft whose period [permitted before he must have sexual relations with his wife] is close and he requested to become the master of a craft whose period is further off, his wife waits for him, except for that of the study of Torah where the woman is unable to wait for her husband who was at leisure ora worker who does not become a Sage.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:1)


**הַמּוֹרֶדֶת.** מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ. אֲבָל מִמְּלָאכָה כּוֹפִין אוֹתָהּ לַעֲשׂוֹת מְלָאכָה וְלֹא דַיְנִינַן לָהּ כְּמוֹרֶדֶת:


המורדת – from providing sexual relations, but from work, we force her to do work and we don’t judge her as rebellious.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:2)


**טַרְפְּעִיקִין.** כָּל טַרְפְּעִיק הוּא מִשְׁקַל אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנֶה שְׂעוֹרוֹת שֶׁל כֶּסֶף צָרוּף, כְּמִשְׁקַל שְׁלֹשָׁה דַרְכְּמוֹנִים שֶׁקּוֹרִין בַּעֲרָבִי דראה"ם, שֶׁכָּל דַּרְכְּמוֹן שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שְׂעוֹרוֹת בֵּינוֹנִיּוֹת שֶׁל כֶּסֶף:


טרפעיקין – Every half a denar (Quinarius – see Talmud Ketubot 64a) is a weight of forty-eight barley-corns of refined silver according to the weight of three Darkmonim which is called DRAHAS in Arabic, as each Darkmon is sixteen intermediate barley-corns of silver.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:3)


**עַד כְּנֶגֶד כְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹתֵן לָהּ גֵּט וְיוֹצְאָה בְּלֹא כְתֻבָּה. אֲבָל אֵינוֹ מַשְׁהֶה אוֹתָהּ כְּדֵי לִפְחוֹת עַל הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ מִבֵּית אָבִיהָ וּלְהַפְסִידָם מִמֶּנָּה:


עד כנגד כתובתה – and afterwards he gives her a Jewish bill of divorce and she leaves without the Ketubah settlement but he doesn’t restrain her in order to lessen on the property that fell to her from her father’s house and to cause her to lose out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:7:4)


**מוֹסִיפִין עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה דִינָרִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּצַעֲרָא דְגַבְרָא קָשֶׁה כְּשֶׁהוּא נִמְנָע מִן הַתַּשְׁמִישׁ יוֹתֵר מִצַּעֲרָא דְאִתְּתָא, לְפִיכָךְ כְּשֶׁהִיא מוֹרֶדֶת וּמְצַעַרְתּוֹ פּוֹחֲתִין לָהּ שִׁבְעָה דִינָרִין, וּכְשֶׁהוּא מוֹרֵד וּמְצַעֲרָהּ אֵין מוֹסִיפִין לָהּ אֶלָּא שְׁלֹשָׁה דִינָרִים. וְהֵיכָא דְמוֹרֶדֶת אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא חָזְרוּ וְנִמְנוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מַכְרִיזִין עָלֶיהָ אַרְבַּע שַׁבָּתוֹת זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ בְּבָתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת וּבָתֵּי מִדְרָשׁוֹת וְאוֹמֵר פְּלוֹנִית מָרְדָה בְּבַעְלָהּ, וְשׁוֹלְחִין לָהּ מִבֵּית דִּין הֱוֵי יוֹדַעַת שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ כְּתֻבָּתֵךְ מֵאָה מָנֶה הִפְסַדְתְּ אֶת כֻּלָּן, אַחַת אֲרוּסָה וְאַחַת נְשׂוּאָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ נִדָּה וַאֲפִלּוּ חוֹלָה וַאֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם. וְקֹדֶם הַכְרָזָה מוֹדִיעִים לָהּ שֶׁרוֹצִים לְהַכְרִיז עָלֶיהָ, וְאַחַר הַכְרָזָה מוֹדִיעִין אוֹתָהּ שֶׁכְּבָר שָׁלְמָה הַהַכְרָזָה, וְאִם עָמְדָה בְמִרְדָּהּ תֵּצֵא בְּלֹא כְתֻבָּה:


מוסיפין על כתובתה שלשה דינרים – because of the pain that a difficult man that he is prevented from sexual intercourse more than the pain of his wife. Therefore, when she rebels and causes him pain, we decrease her [Ketubah] by seven Denarim, but when he rebels and causes her pain, we don’t increase [the Ketubah] to her other than by three Denarim, and where she rebels, we state in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 63b) took a second note and an announcement regarding her shall be made on four consecutive Sabbaths and that then the court shall send her [the following warning]: ‘Be it known to you that even if your Ketubah is for one hundred Maneh, you have forfeited it.’ The same [law is applicable] to a betrothed or married woman and even to a menstruant [and even to a sick woman] and even to one awaiting the decision of the levir. But prior to the announcement, they inform her that they want to make an announcement about her and after the announcement, they inform her that she has already fulfilled the announcement, and if she stood in her rebellion, she would be divorced without her Ketubah settlement.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:1)


**הַמַּשְׁרֶה אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ עַל יְדֵי שָׁלִישׁ.** שֶׁנּוֹתֵן מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ עַל יְדֵי אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס וְאֵינָהּ אוֹכֶלֶת עִמּוֹ. תַּרְגּוּם וַיִּכְרֶה לָהֶם כֵּרָה גְדוֹלָה (מלכים ב ו), שֵׁירוּתָא:


המשרה את אשתו על יד שליש – he gives her support through an administrator and does not eat with him (II Kings 6:23): “And he prepared a lavish feast for them [and, after they had eaten and drunk, he let them go, and they returned to their master. And the Aramean bands stopped invading the Land of Israel];” service.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:2)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת לָהּ.** בְּשָׁבוּעַ:


לא יפחות לה – in a week.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:3)


**לֹא פָסַק לָהּ שְׂעוֹרִים.** כִּפְלַיִם מִן הַחִטִּים: אֶלָּא רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל שֶׁהָיָה סָמוּךְ לֶאֱדוֹם שֶׁשָּׁם הַשְּׂעוֹרִים רָעוֹת בְּיוֹתֵר:


לא פסק לה שעורים – double from the wheat, but Rabbi Yishmael, who was near Edom where the barley was the worst.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:4)


**דְּבֵלָה.** תְּאֵנִים דְּרוּסוֹת בְּעִגּוּל וְנִמְכָּרוֹת בְּמִשְׁקָל וְלֹא בְמִדָּה:


דבילה – figs that were tread in a circule and sold by weight and not by measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:5)


**מַפָּץ.** רַךְ מִמַּחֲצֶלֶת:


מפץ – softer than a matting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:6)


**כִּפָּה.** צָעִיף אֶחָד:


כפה – one veil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:7)


**וּמִנְעָלִים מִמּוֹעֵד לְמוֹעֵד.** מִנְעָלִים חֲדָשִׁים לְכָל שָׁלֹשׁ רְגָלִים:


ומנעלים ממועד למועד – new shoes for each of the Three Pilgrimage Festivals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:8)


**חֲדָשִׁים.** קָשִׁים לָהּ בִּימוֹת הַחַמָּה לְפִי שֶׁהֵם חַמִּים, וְיָפִים לָהּ בִּימוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים:


חדשים – hard for her during the months of sunlight (i.e. heat) because they are hot, and nice for her in the rainy days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:8:9)


**וְהַשְּׁחָקִים שֶׁלָּהּ.** אַף כְּשֶׁיִּקְנֶה לָהּ חֲדָשִׁים, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמִּתְכַּסָּה בָהֶן בִּימֵי נִדָּתָהּ:


והשחקים שלה – even when he purchases her new ones because she covers herself with them during the days of her menstruation.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:1)


**נוֹתֵן לָהּ מָעָה כֶסֶף.** בְּכָל שַׁבָּת לְצֹרֶךְ דְּבָרִים קְטַנִּים:


נותן לה מעה כסף – in each week for the needs of small things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:2)


**וְאוֹכֶלֶת עִמּוֹ לֵילֵי שַׁבָּת.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְכָל שְׁאָר הַיָּמִים יָכוֹל לָתֵת מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ עַל יְדֵי שָׁלִישׁ אִם יִרְצֶה, בְּלֵיל שַׁבָּת שֶׁהוּא לֵיל עוֹנָה חַיָּב לֶאֱכֹל עִמָּהּ:


ואוכלת עמו ליל שבת – even though that on all the rest of the days [of the week], he can give her support through a third party if he wants, on Friday night, which is the night of conjugal relations, he is obliged to eat with her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:3)


**מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ.** מוֹתַר מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ, כְּלוֹמַר מַה שֶּׁעוֹשָׂה יוֹתֵר עַל מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ:


מעשה ידיה – athe excess of her handiwork, meaning to say, what she does greater than her support/food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:4)


**שְׁתִי.** קָשֶׁה לִטְווֹת כִּפְלַיִם כְּשֶׁל עֵרֶב. וּמִשְׁקָל שֶׁל יְהוּדָה כִּפְלַיִם כְּשֶׁל גָּלִיל:


שתי – it is hard to wave double of woof and the weight of Judea is double that of the Galilee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/5:9:5)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי כְבוֹדוֹ.** וּכְפִי מִנְהַג הַמְּדִינָה נַמִּי:


הכל לפי כבודו – and also according to the custom of the country/province.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:1)


**מְצִיאַת הָאִשָּׁה.** וִירֻשָּׁתָהּ. אִם נָפְלָה לָהּ יְרֻשָּׁה:


מציאת האשה. וירושתה – if an inheritance fell to her,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:2)


**הוּא אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת בְּחַיֶּיהָ.** וְהַקֶּרֶן שֶׁלָּהּ:


he (i.e., her husband) enjoys the usufruct during her lifetime and the principal is hers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:3)


**וּפְגָמָהּ.** חַבָּלָה שֶׁיֶּשׁ בָּהּ פְּחַת דָּמִים שֶׁשָּׁמִין אוֹתָהּ כַּמָּה הָיְתָה יָפָה לִמָּכֵר בַּשּׁוּק וְכַמָּה הִיא שָׁוָה עַכְשָׁיו:


ופגמה – damages for injury that she has [sustained], the diminution of monies that they assess concerning her – how beautiful she was to sell in the marketplace and how much she is worth currently.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:4)


**וּבִזְמַן שֶׁבַּגָּלוּי.** שֶׁהַבֹּשֶׁת שֶׁלּוֹ. וְעוֹד, שֶׁנִּמְאֶסֶת עָלָיו וְהוּא סוֹבְלָהּ:


ובזמן שבגלוי – for the shame is his and furthermore, she is repulsive to him and he suffers for her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:1:5)


**יִלָּקַח בָּהֶן קַרְקַע וְהוּא אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת.** בְּחַיֶּיהָ, וְהַקֶּרֶן שֶׁלָּהּ לִכְשֶׁיָּמוּת אוֹ יְגָרְשֶׁנָּה. וְאִם תָּמוּת הִיא, יוֹרְשָׁהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ילקח בהן קרקע והוא אוכל פירות – during her lifetime and he principal is her, when he dies or divorces her but if she dies, she inheris it, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:2:1)


**וּמֵת חֲתָנוֹ.** וְנָפְלָה לִפְנֵי יָבָם:


ומת חתנו – and she fell before the levir [for him to consummate the marriage].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:2:2)


**יָכוֹל הוּא שֶׁיֹּאמַר.** אוֹ חֲלֹץ אוֹ יַבֵּם. וְאִלּוּ לְאָחִיו, אוֹ יִתֵּן מַה שֶּׁפָּסַק אוֹ תֵשֵׁב עַד שֶׁתַּלְבִּין רֹאשָׁהּ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאָחִיו עַם הָאָרֶץ וְהוּא תַּלְמִיד חָכָם, יָכוֹל הוּא לוֹמַר לְאָחִיךָ הָיִיתִי רוֹצֶה לִתֵּן וּלְךָ אֵינִי רוֹצֶה לִתֵּן:


יכול הוא שיאמר – either remove the shoe or fulfill the role of the levir (i.e., marry your sister-in-law), or give what had been decided or let her sit until her head becomes white (i.e., she becomes old), and even though his brother was an ignoramus and he (who had died) was a Sage, he (i.e., the father-in-law) can say – “to your brother, I would want to give [her], but to you, I would not want to give [her].”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:1)


**וְהוּא פוֹסֵק כְּנֶגְדָּן חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר מָנֶה.** אֶלֶף דִּינָר הֵן עֲשָׂרָה מָנֶה, וּכְשֶׁבָּא הֶחָתָן לְקַבֵּל עָלָיו וְלִכְתֹּב בַּכְּתֻבָּה, כּוֹתֵב שְׁלִישׁ יוֹתֵר, שֶׁהֵן חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה מָנֶה, אִם הִכְנִיסָה לוֹ הַכַּלָּה דִּינָרִין בְּעַיִן, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מִשְׂתַּכֵּר בָּהֶן:


והוא פוסק כנגדן ט"ו מנה – they are one thousand Denars [that the woman brings into the marriage], which are ten Maneh, and when the groom comes to accept upon himself and to write in the Ketubah , he writes one third more, which are fifteen Maneh, and if the bride brought in to him discernible Denarim because he profits through them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:2)


**וּכְנֶגֶד הַשּׁוּם.** וְאִם הִכְנִיסָה לוֹ בְּגָדִים וְתַכְשִׁיטִים שֶׁצְּרִיכִין שׁוּמָא, הוּא פוֹסֵק פָּחוֹת חֹמֶשׁ, שֶׁאִם הִכְנִיסָה שׁוּם שֶׁל אֶלֶף זוּז אֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל עָלָיו אֶלָּא שְׁמֹנֶה מֵאוֹת, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַבָּאִים לָשׁוּם הַבְּגָדִים וּנְכָסִים שֶׁל הַכַּלָּה לְשׁוּמָם יוֹתֵר מִשָּׁוְיָם לִכְבוֹד הַכַּלָּה וּלְחַבְּבָהּ עַל בַּעְלָהּ:


וכנגד השום – if she brought in [to the marriage] clothing and ornaments that require estimation, he agrees to restore one-fifth less, so if he brought in an estimated value of one-thousand Zuz, he doesn’t accept upon himself other than eight hundred, for it the manner of those who come to estimate clothing and the property of the bride to estimate them as greater than their worth in honor of the bride and to make her more beloved to her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:3)


**שׁוּם בְּמָנֶה וְשָׁוֶה מָנֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם לֹא שָׁמוּ נִכְסֵי הַכַּלָּה וְתַכְשִׁיטֶיהָ אֶלָּא כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵם שָׁוִים בַּשּׁוּק:


שום במנה ושוה במנה – meaning to say, if they did not estimate the property of the bride and her adornments other than like they are worth in the marketplace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:4)


**אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא מָנֶה.** לֹא יִכְתְּבוּ בִּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה אֶלָּא מָנֶה כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁמוּ:


אין לי אלא מנה – they should not write into the document of the Ketubah other than a Man as they have estimated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:5)


**שׁוּם בְּמָנֶה הִיא נוֹתֶנֶת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאֶחָד סֶלַע וְדִינָר.** שׁוּם שֶׁהוּא מְקַבְּלָהּ בְּמָנֶה, שֶׁאָמְרוּ לוֹ כְּתֹב מָנֶה בַּכְּתֻבָּה וְהִיא תַכְנִיס לְךָ שׁוּם שֶׁל מָנֶה, צָרִיךְ שֶׁיָּשׁוּמוּ אוֹתוֹ בְּבֵית הַחֲתוּנָה שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאֶחָד סֶלַע וְדִינָר, שֶׁהוּא מָנֶה וְחֻמְשׁוֹ:


שום במנה היא נותנת שלשים ואחד סלע ודינר – the estimation that he accepts her for a Maneh that they told him, write “Maneh” in the Ketubah and she shall bring in an estimation of a Maneh. They must place it in the house of the wedding ceremony thirty one Selah and a Sinar which is a Maneh and a fifth larger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:6)


**וּבְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת.** שׁוּם, כְּשֶׁיְּקַבֵּל עָלָיו הֶחָתָן לִכְתֹּב אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת. הִיא נוֹתֶנֶת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת לְפִי שׁוּמַת הַנּוֹעָדִים:


ובארבע מאות – the estimated value that the groom accepts upon himself to write four hundred [in the Ketubah], she gives five hundred according to the valuation of those designated/appointed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:3:7)


**וּכְשֶׁחָתָן פּוֹסֵק וְכוּ'.** וְאִם שָׁמוּ הֵם תְּחִלָּה וְהִכְנִיסָה לוֹ, בֵּין שׁוּם קָטָן בֵּין שׁוּם גָּדוֹל, הוּא כּוֹתֵב בַּשְּׁטָר פָּחוֹת חֹמֶשׁ:


וכשחתן פוסק וכו' – and if they estimated first and she brought in to him whether a small or a large valuation, he writes into the document one-fifth less.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:1)


**פָּסְקָה לְהַכְנִיס לוֹ כְסָפִים.** דִּינָרִים בְּעַיִן, שֶׁמּוּכָנִין לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר בָּהֶן מִיָּד:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:2)


**סֶלַע.** שֶׁהוּא אַרְבָּעָה דִינָרִין נַעֲשֶׂה לִכָּתֵב בִּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה בְּשִׁשָּׁה דִינָרִין, בְּתוֹסֶפֶת שְׁלִישׁ. וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן תַּנָּא דִּבְדִינָרִין בְּעַיִן, בֵּין שֶׁהֵן מְרֻבִּים כְּגוֹן אֶלֶף דִּינָר דְּקָתָנֵי בְּרֵישָׁא, בֵּין שֶׁהֵם מֻעָטִים כְּגוֹן סֶלַע דְּקָתָנֵי בְּסֵיפָא, לְעוֹלָם נִכְתָּבִים בִּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה בִּשְׁלִישׁ יוֹתֵר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁעוֹמְדִין לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר בָּהֶן מִיָּד. וּבִדְבָרִים שֶׁצְּרִיכִין שׁוּמָא כְּגוֹן בְּגָדִים וְתַכְשִׁיטִים, בֵּין שֶׁהֵם מְרֻבִּים כְּגוֹן וּכְנֶגֶד הַשּׁוּם דְּקָתָנֵי דְקָאֵי אַאֶלֶף זוּז דְּאָמַר בְּרֵישָׁא, בֵּין שֶׁהֵם מֻעָטִים כְּגוֹן הָא דְקָתָנֵי שׁוּם בְּמָנֶה, הִיא נוֹתֶנֶת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאֶחָד סֶלַע וְדִינָר, לְעוֹלָם פּוֹחֲתִין חֹמֶשׁ בִּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה מִמַּה שֶּׁשָּׁמוּ אוֹתָם בְּבֵית הַחֲתוּנָה, לֹא שְׁנָא אִם שָׁמוּ אוֹתָם תְּחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ בָּאִין לִכְתֹּב הַכְּתֻבָּה, שֶׁאָז צָרִיךְ לִפְחוֹת בִּשְׁטַר הַכְּתֻבָּה הַחֹמֶשׁ מִן הַשּׁוּמָא שֶׁשָּׁמוּ בְּבֵית הַכַּלָּה כִּדְקָתָנֵי וּכְשֶׁהֶחָתָן פּוֹסֵק וְכוּ', וְלֹא שְׁנָא אִם הוּא כָּתַב תְּחִלָּה הַכְּתֻבָּה, שֶׁאָז צָרִיךְ שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הַשּׁוּמָא בְּתוֹסֶפֶת חֹמֶשׁ מִמַּה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּכְּתֻבָּה כִּדְקָתָנֵי וּבְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת שׁוּם הִיא נוֹתֶנֶת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:4:3)


**לַקֻּפָּה.** שֶׁיִּתֵּן לָהּ עֲשָׂרָה זוּז עַל כָּל מָנֶה וּמָנֶה שֶׁהִיא מְבִיאָה לוֹ, כְּדֵי לִקְנוֹת לָהּ מֵהֶם בְּשָׂמִים וְתַמְרוּקֵי הַנָּשִׁים. וְלֹא אִתְפָּרֵשׁ בְּמַתְנִיתִין אִם לְכָל שָׁבוּעַ אוֹ לְכָל חֹדֶשׁ אוֹ לְכָל שָׁנָה:



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:5:1)


**וְכֵן הַמַּשִּׂיא אֶת הַיְתוֹמָה.** גַּבַּאי צְדָקָה:


המשיא את היתומה – collectors/managers of charity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:5:2)


**אִם יֵשׁ בַּכִּיס.** שֶׁל צְדָקָה:


אם יש בכיס – of charity.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:1)


**מַה שֶּׁרָאוּי לִתֵּן לָהּ.** עִשּׂוּר נְכָסִים:


מה שראוי ליתן לה – one tenth of the property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:2)


**אִם הִשִּׂיא.** הָאָב בְּחַיָּיו:


אם השיא – the father, during his lifetime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:6:3)


**אֶת הַבַּת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה יִנָּתֵן וְכוּ'.** בֵּין שֶׁהוּא פָּחוּת מֵעִשּׂוּר בֵּין שֶׁהוּא יוֹתֵר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָזְלִינַן בָּתַר אֻמְדָן דַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּאָב. וְאִם אִי אֶפְשָׁר לַעֲמֹד עַל אֻמְדָן דַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל אָב, יָהֲבִינַן לָהּ עִשּׂוּר נְכָסִים דְּמִשְׁתַּכְּחֵי הַשְׁתָּא בִּשְׁעַת נִשּׂוּאֶיהָ מִמְּקַרְקְעֵי. אֲבָל מִמְּטַלְטְלֵי אֵין לָהּ עִשּׂוּר. וְאִיכָּא לְמַאן דְּאָמַר דְּבַזְּמַן הַזֶּה מִמְּטַלְטְלֵי נַמִּי אִית לָהּ עִשּׂוּר. וְאִם נִשֵּׂאת וְלֹא תָבְעָה מִן הַיּוֹרְשִׁים, תּוֹבַעַת לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן אַחוֹלֵי אַחֲלָה גַּבַּיְהוּ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהָיְתָה נִזּוֹנִית מִנִּכְסֵי אָבִיהָ, אֲבָל אִם פָּסְקוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים מִלָּתֵת לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת [אַחוֹלֵי אַחֲלָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מָחֲתָה]. וְאִם הָיְתָה בוֹגֶרֶת שֶׁאֵין לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת וְעָמְדָה וְנִשֵּׂאת וְלֹא תָבְעָה מַה שֶּׁרָאוּי לָהּ לִנְדֻנְיָתָהּ מִנִּכְסֵי אָבִיהָ, אַחוֹלֵי אַחֲלָה גַּבֵּי יוֹרְשִׁים וְאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לִתְבֹּעַ עוֹד, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיְתָה נִזּוֹנֶת מִנִּכְסֵיהֶם:


[married off the first daughter], he should give, etc. (i.e., to the second what the first received), whether it is less than one tenth or whether it is more. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda, for we go after the approximate mental assessment of the father. But if it is impossible to stand by the approximate mental assessment of the father, we give her one-tenth of his possessions as they are found now at the time of her wedding from his immovable (i.e. landed) possessions, but from movable possessions, she does not receive one-tenth. And there are those according to the one who states that in this time, from movable [possessions] she also has one-tenth. But if she married and she did not claim from the heirs, she claims after she got married and we don’t say that she resigned her claim regarding them. And especially, when she was supported from the property of her father but if the heirs stopped from giving her support, [she resigned her claim unless she derived benefit] but if she was an adult woman, she has no support from the property of her father and she resigned her claim regarding the heirs and she is not able to claim further, and even if she was supported from their property.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:1)


**הַמַּשְׁלִישׁ מָעוֹת לְבִתּוֹ.** מָסַר מָעוֹת בְּיַד שָׁלִישׁ לְצֹרֶךְ בִּתּוֹ לִקְנוֹת לָהּ שָׂדֶה אוֹ נְדוּנְיָא לִכְשֶׁתִּנָּשֵׂא:


המשליש מעות לבתו – He [i.e., the father] transferred money in the hands of a third-party for the needs of his daughter to purchase for her a field or a dowry when she gets married.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:2)


**נֶאֱמָן בַּעְלִי עָלָי.** וּתְנֵם לוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:3)


**יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלִישׁ כוּ'.** יִקְנֶה הַשָּׂדֶה. וְאֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לָהּ, דְּמִצְוָה לְקַיֵּם דִּבְרֵי הַמֵּת:


יעשה שליש כו' – he should purchase/acquire the field and we don’t listen to her for it is a Mitzvah to fulfill the words of the dead person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/6:7:4)


**וְכִי אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא שָׂדֶה.** כְּלוֹמַר אֲפִלּוּ כְּבָר נִקְנָה הַשָּׂדֶה וְהִיא רוֹצָה לְמָכְרָהּ יְכוֹלָה לִמְכֹּר, הִלְכָּךְ שׁוֹמְעִין לָהּ. וּפְלֻגְתָּא דְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר דַּוְקָא בִּגְדוֹלָה מִן הָאֵרוּסִין, דְּאִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת דִּבְרֵי הַכֹּל שׁוֹמְעִין לָהּ. וּבִקְטַנָּה נַמִּי מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאֵין בְּמַעֲשֵׂה קְטַנָּה כְּלוּם וְאֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


וכי אינה אלא שדה – meaning to say, even if the field was already acquired and she wants to sell, she can sell it; therefore we listen to her. And the dispute of Rabbi Yosi and Rabbi Meir is specifically with regard to an adult woman from betrothal. For is she had already gotten married, everyone states that we listen to her, and with a minor also, Rabbi Yosi admits that there is nothing in the actions a minor at all, and we don’t listen to her. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:1)


**הַמַּדִּיר אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִלֵּהָנוֹת לוֹ.** עַל הֲנָאַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ אֵין הַנֵּדֶר חָל, מִשּׁוּם דִּמְשֻׁעְבָּד לָהּ וְלָאו כָּל כְּמִינֵיהּ לְהַפְקִיעַ שִׁעְבּוּדוֹ. וְעַל הֲנָאַת מְזוֹנוֹת אַף עַל גַּב דִּמְשֻׁעְבָּד לָהּ אַשְׁכְּחָן דְּחָל הַנֵּדֶר, כְּגוֹן שֶׁמַּסְפִּיקִין מַעֲשֵׂה יָדֶיהָ לִמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ אִם כֵּן לָמָּה יַעֲמִיד פַּרְנָס, וּמְשָׁנֵי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁאֵין מַסְפִּיקִין לִדְבָרִים שֶׁהָיְתָה רְגִילָה בָהֶם בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ, וְעַל זֶה בִּלְבָד יַעֲמִיד פַּרְנָס שֶׁיְּפַרְנְסֶנָּה. וְלֹא שֶׁיַּעֲמִיד שָׁלִיחַ לְפַרְנְסָהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי שְׁלוּחוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם כְּמוֹתוֹ, אֶלָּא שֶׁאוֹמֵר כָּל הַמְפַרְנֵס אֵינוֹ מַפְסִיד:


המדיר את אשתו מליהנות לו – on the benefit of sexual relations, this vow does not take effect because he is subjugated to her and he doesn’t have the power to cancel his obligation of his subjugation and on the benefit of food, even though he is subjugated to her, we find that the vow exists such as we supply her handiwork for her food. And in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 70b), it raises a question: if so, why should he appoint an administrator? And it answers – such as the case that we don’t supply the things that she was accustomed to in her father’s house and for this alone, he should appoint an administrator who will provide for her and not that he sill appoint an agent to support her, for the agent of a person is like that person, but rather, he says, all who support does not cause loss.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:2)


**יָתֵר מִכֵּן יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה.** דְּעַד שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם לֹא שָׁמְעֵי אֱינָשֵׁי וְלֵיכָּא זִילוּתָא. טְפֵי מִשְּׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם שָׁמְעֵי אֱינָשֵׁי וְאִיכָּא זִילוּתָא:


יתר מכן יוציא ויתן כתובה – for up to thirty days, people don’t listen and there is no disgrace. More than thirty days, people here and there is disgrace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:3)


**בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל.** אִם יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַחֲזִיר אֶת גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ:


בישראל – if he is an Israelite (as opposed to being a Kohen), he can restore his divorced wife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:1:4)


**וּבְכֹהֶנֶת.** שֶׁאִם יְגָרְשֶׁנָּה לֹא יוּכַל לְהַחֲזִירָהּ, יָהֲבוּ לֵיהּ רַבָּנָן זִימְנָא טְפֵי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ובכהנת – for if he disvorces her, he is not able to restore her [as his wife], the Rabbis hive him more time, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:2:1)


**אַחַד מִכָּל הַפֵּרוֹת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמְרָה הִיא קוֹנָם פְּרִי פְּלוֹנִי עָלַי, וְהוּא קִיֵּם לָהּ:


אחד מכל הפירות – such as for example, she said, “I take a vow against consuming this produce,” and it is fulfilled for her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:2:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


ר' יהודה אומר כו' – and the Halakha is not according to him.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:1)


**שֶׁלֹּא תִתְקַשֵּׁט בְּאַחַד מִכָּל הַמִּינִים.** [שֶׁאָמְרָה הִיא] קוֹנָם בֹּשֶׂם פְּלוֹנִי עָלַי, וְהוּא קִיֵּם לָהּ:


שלא תתקשט באחד מכל המינים – [for she said]: “I take a vow that this shame be upon me and he fulfilled it for her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:2)


**בַּעֲנִיּוֹת שֶׁלֹּא נָתַן קִצְבָּה.** לַדָּבָר, עַד מָתַי אֲסָרוֹ עָלֶיהָ, הוּא דְיוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה. אֲבָל אִם נָתַן קִצְבָּה, תַּמְתִּין עַד אוֹתוֹ זְמָן. וְכַמָּה קִצְבָּה, שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ:


בעניות שלא נתן קצבה לדבר – until when/how long was it prohibited to her, he should divorce her and give her the Ketubah settlement, but if he set a limit, she should wait until that time, and what is that limit? Twelve months.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:3:3)


**וּבַעֲשִׁירוֹת שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** שֶׁכֵּן אִשָּׁה חֲשׁוּבָה נֶהֱנֵית מֵרֵיחַ קִשּׁוּטֶיהָ שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, וְזֹאת תֵּהָנֶה מֵרֵיחַ הַקִּשּׁוּטִים שֶׁנִּתְקַשְּׁטָה לִפְנֵי הַנֵּדֶר שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ובעשירות שלשים יום – for it is the case that an important woman benefits from the scent of her adornments for thirty days and this one will benefit from the scent of the adornments that she was adorned with prior to the vow thirty days, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:4:1)


**רֶגֶל אֶחָד יְקַיֵּם, שְׁלֹשָׁה יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה.** הָא מַתְנִיתִין מְפָרְשִׁים לָהּ בַּגְּמָרָא הָכִי, רֶגֶל אֶחָד יְקַיֵּם, בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים, בְּאֵשֶׁת יִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲבָל בְּכֹהֶנֶת, שְׁנַיִם יְקַיֵּם, שְׁלֹשָׁה יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי יְהוּדָה הִיא דִמְפַלֵּיג בֵּין יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֶנֶת, וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


רגל אחד יקיים שלשה יוציא ויתן כתובה – This Mishnah, we explain it in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 71b) as such: [one] Festival he should uphold the marriage. When is this said? With an Israelite woman but with the daughter of a Kohen, two months he should uphold the marriage and three [months], he should divorce her and grant her the Ketubah settlement but our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Yehuda who disputes [regarding the marriage] of an Israelite to a daughter of a Kohen, but the Halakha is not according to him.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:1)


**שֶׁנּוֹעֵל בְּפָנֶיהָ.** דֶּלֶת שֶׁל שִׂמְחָה וַהֲסָרַת יָגוֹן. וּלְבֵית הָאֵבֶל נוֹעֵל בְּפָנֶיהָ, שֶׁלְּמָחָר הִיא מֵתָה וְאֵין אָדָם סוֹפְדָהּ:


שנועל בפניה – the door of joy and the removal of sorrow, and to the house of mourning, he locks it (i.e., the door) in her face, for tomorrow she may die, and no one will eulogize her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:2)


**מֵחֲמַת דָּבָר אַחֵר.** כְּגוֹן דְּאִתְחַזַּק שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם פְּרוּצִים מְצוּיִין שָׁם:


דבר אחר מחמת – as for example, that under the presumption that lawless human beings are found there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:3)


**שֶׁתֹּאמְרִי לִפְלוֹנִי וְכוּ'.** דְּבָרִים שֶׁל קַלּוּת:


שתאמרי לפלוני וכו' – things of degradation/disgrace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:5:4)


**שֶׁתְּהֵא מְמַלְּאָה וּמְעָרָה לָאַשְׁפָּה.** אִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי לְאַחַר שֶׁתְּשַׁמֵּשׁ וִימַלֵּא רַחְמָהּ שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע תְּנַפְּצֶנּוּ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִקְלֹט הַזֶּרַע וְתִתְעַבֵּר. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי שֶׁתְּמַלֵּא עֲשָׂרָה כַדֵּי מַיִם וּתְעָרֶה לָאַשְׁפָּה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּרְאֵית כְּשׁוֹטָה:


שתהא ממלאה ומערה לאשפה – there are those who interpret that after she has sexual intercourse and her womb is filled with semen, it would shake it out in order that the seed won’t be absorbed and she will become pregnant. And there are those who interpret that she will fill ten pitches of water and she will pour them out to the ground, because she appears as a women suspected of infidelity by her husband.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:1)


**מַאֲכִילָתוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְעֻשָּׂר.** וְלֹא נוֹדַע לוֹ אֶלָּא אַחַר שֶׁאֲכָלוֹ, כְּגוֹן דְּאָמְרָה לֵיהּ פְּלוֹנִי כֹּהֵן תִּקֵּן לִי אֶת הַכְּרִי, וְאָזַל שַׁיְילֵיהּ וְאִשְׁתַּכַּח שִׁקְרָא:


מאכילתו שאינו מעושר – and it was not known to him until after she fed him, such as for example, tat she said to him: So-and-so, a Kohen prepared for me the heap [of grain] and left; he asked him and it was found to be a lie.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:2)


**וּמְשַׁמַּשְׁתּוֹ נִדָּה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁהֻחְזְקָה נִדָּה בִּשְׁכֵנוֹתֶיהָ שֶׁרָאוּהָ לוֹבֶשֶׁת בִּגְדֵי נִדּוּת, וּלְבַעְלָהּ אָמְרָה טְהוֹרָה אֲנִי:


ומשמשתו נדה – as for example, when she was presumed to be a menstruant woman/Niddah in his neighborhood when they saw her dress in the clothing of menstruant woman and to her husband she said, “I am ritually pure.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:3)


**וְלֹא קוֹצָה לָהּ חַלָּה.** דְּאָמְרָה לֵיהּ פְּלוֹנִי תִּקֵּן לִי אֶת הָעִסָּה, וְאָזַל שַׁיְילֵיהּ וְאִשְׁתַּכַּח שִׁקְרָא:


ולא קוצה לה חלה – that she said to him: So-and-so prepared for me the dough and they asked him and it was found that to be a lie.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:4)


**וְטוֹוָה בַשּׁוּק.** וּמַרְאָה זְרוֹעוֹתֶיהָ לִבְנֵי אָדָם:


וטווה בשור – and she shows her arms to people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:5)


**וּמְדַבֶּרֶת עִם בְּנֵי אָדָם.** מְשַׂחֶקֶת עִם הַבַּחוּרִים:


ומדברת עם בני אדם – playing with the boys.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:6)


**וּמְקַלֶּלֶת יוֹלְדָיו בְּפָנָיו.** מְקַלֶּלֶת וּמְחָרֶפֶת אֲבִי בַעְלָהּ בִּפְנֵי בַעְלָהּ:


ומקללת יולדיו בפניו – she curses and blasphemes the father of her husband in the presence of her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:6:7)


**הַקּוֹלָנִית.** תּוֹבַעַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ בְּקוֹל רָם עַד שֶׁשְּׁכֵנוֹתֶיהָ שׁוֹמְעוֹת אוֹתָהּ. וְכָל הַנֵּי צְרִיכוֹת עֵדִים וְהַתְרָאָה לְהַפְסִידָן כְּתֻבָּתָן. וְאֵין לָהֶן לֹא כְּתֻבָּה וְלֹא תּוֹסֶפֶת, וְאֵינָן נוֹטְלוֹת אֶלָּא בְּלָאוֹתֵיהֶן הַקַּיָּמִים בִּלְבָד:


הקולנית – she claims sexual intercourse in a loud voice until her neighbors hear her, and all of these require witnesses and warning to cause them to lose their Ketubah settlements, and they don’t have neither the Ketubah nor the supplement and they don’t take anything other than their outworn garments that exist alone.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:7:1)


**שֶׁאֵין עָלֶיהָ נְדָרִים וְנִמְצְאוּ עָלֶיהָ נְדָרִים.** בְּאֵילוּ נְדָרִים אָמְרוּ, שֶׁלֹּא תֹאכַל בָּשָׂר וְלֹא תִשְׁתֶּה יַיִן וְלֹא תִתְקַשֵּׁט בְּבִגְדֵי צִבְעוֹנִים. אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר נְדָרִים, מְקֻדֶּשֶׁת:


שאין עליה נדרים ונמצאו עליה נדרים – on which vows did they say that she should not eat meat and not drink wine and not adorn herself in colorful clothing, but in the rest of the vows, she is betrothed/sanctified.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:7:2)


**כָּל הַמּוּמִין הַפּוֹסְלִין בַּכֹּהֲנִים.** בִּבְכוֹרוֹת קָא מָנֵי לְהוּ. וְהוֹסִיפוּ עֲלֵיהֶן בַּנָּשִׁים זֵעָה. וְרֵיחַ הַפֶּה אוֹ רֵיחַ רַע בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר בְּגוּפָהּ. וְשׁוּמָא שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ שֵׂעָר, בֵּין קְטַנָּה בֵּין גְּדוֹלָה כְּשֶׁהִיא כְּנֶגֶד פָּנֶיהָ, וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהִיא תַּחַת כִּפָּה שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ, דְּזִמְנִין מִתְחַזְיָא וְזִמְנִין דְּלֹא מִתְחַזְיָא, שֶׁאִם הִיא בְּמָקוֹם הַמְגֻלֶּה תָּמִיד הֲרֵי רָאָה וְנִתְפַּיֵּס, וְאִם הַשּׁוּמָא אֵין בָּהּ שֵׂעָר אֵינָהּ מוּם עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא גְדוֹלָה כְּאִיסָר. וְקוֹלָהּ עָבֶה וּמְשֻׁנֶּה מִקּוֹל שְׁאָר הַנָּשִׁים. וּנְשָׁכָהּ כֶּלֶב וְנַעֲשָׂה בִּמְקוֹם הַנְּשִׁיכָה צַלֶּקֶת, כְּלוֹמַר כִּוּוּץ כְּמוֹ מִכְוַת אֵשׁ. וְדַדֶּיהָ גַּסִּים מֵחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ טֶפַח. וְשֶׁדַּדֶּיהָ רְחוֹקִים זֶה מִזֶּה עַד שֶׁיֵּשׁ טֶפַח בֵּין דַּד לְדַד. אֵלּוּ מוּמִין בַּנָּשִׁים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָם מוּמִין בַּכֹּהֲנִים:


כל המומין הפוסלין בכהנים – with regard to first-born he counts them, and they added to them with regard to women: sweat, and bad breath or bad smell in another place in body. And warts that have in them hair, whether small or large when it is corresponding to her face or in the case where it is underneath the covering that on her head, for sometimes it is seen and sometimes it is not seen for if she is in a revealed place at all times, he sees it and is appeased, but if the wart has no hair, it is not a blemish until it as a large as an Issar. And her voice is thick and different from the voice of other women, and the dog bit her and made in the place of the bite became scarred (see Talmud Ketubot 75a), meaning to say, causing it to shrink like wounds by fire. And her breasts are larger than her neighbors by a handbreadth and just as the breasts are distant from one another until there is a and-breadth between each breast. These are blemishes in women and even though they are not [viewed as] blemishes in Kohanim.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:1)


**הָאָב צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה.** אִם בָּא לִתְבֹּעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִן הָאֵרוּסִין מִזֶּה שֶׁמְּמָאֵן לְקַחְתָּהּ, צָרִיךְ שֶׁיָּבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁלְּאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְאָרְסָה הָיוּ בָהּ מוּמִין הַלָּלוּ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָאִשָּׁה חֲזָקָה דְגוּפָהּ, הוֹאִיל וְנִמְצְאוּ הַמּוּמִין בִּרְשׁוּת הָאָב וְאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר כָּאן הָיוּ קֹדֶם אֵרוּסִין, וּלְפִיכָךְ אִם לֹא הֵבִיא רְאָיָה, בַּעַל מְהֵימָן:


האב צריך להביא ראיה – if he comes to claim her Ketubah from the betrothal, from this that he (i.e., the husband) refuses to take her, he (i.e., the father) needs to bring proof that after she became betrothed, she had blemishes. And even though there is for a woman a presumption regarding her body, for since, these blemishes were found in the domain of her father. And one can say here that they were prior to betrothal, and therefore, if he didn’t bring proof, the husband is believed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:2)


**נִכְנְסָה לִרְשׁוּת הַבַּעַל.** נִשְּׂאָה, וְעַתָּה בָּא לְהוֹצִיאָהּ בְּלֹא כְּתֻבָּה עַל מוּמִין שֶׁבָּהּ, עָלָיו לְהָבִיא רְאָיָה שֶׁקֹּדֶם שֶׁנִּתְאָרְסָה הָיוּ בָהּ מוּמִין הַלָּלוּ. דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא נִמְצְאוּ מוּמִין הַלָּלוּ אֶלָּא בִרְשׁוּתוֹ, אוֹמְרִים כָּאן נִמְצְאוּ כָּאן הָיוּ וּלְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת בָּאוּ לָהּ וְנִסְתַּחֲפָה שָׂדֵהוּ:


נכנסה לרשות הבעל – she married and now comes to divorce her without a Ketubah on account of the blemishes in her. It is upon him to bring proof that prior to her becoming betrothed she had these blemishes, for since these blemishes were not found other than in his domain (i.e., of the husband), we say that here they were found, here they were. But after she got married, they came to her and his field was flooded (see Mishnah Ketubot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:8:3)


**אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִטְעֹן.** דְּיָדַע וְנִתְפַּיֵּס:


אינו יכול לטון – for he knew and was appeased.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:9:1)


**שֶׁנּוֹלְדוּ בוֹ מוּמִין.** מִשֶּׁנְּשָׂאָהּ:


שנולדו בו מומין – from when he married her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:9:2)


**מוּמִין גְּדוֹלִים.** נִסְמֵית עֵינוֹ נִקְטְעָה יָדוֹ נִשְׁבְּרָה רַגְלוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמוּמִין גְּדוֹלִים אֵין כּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ לְהוֹצִיא:


מומין גדולים – his eye became blinded, his hand was mutilated, his leg was broken, but the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel and even with large blemishes, we don’t force him to divorce [her].



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:1)


**מֻכֵּה שְׁחִין.** מְצֹרָע:


מוכה שחין – a leper/Metzora.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:2)


**בַּעַל פּוֹלִיפּוֹס.** רֵיחַ הַחֹטֶם:


בעל פוליפוס – the smell of the nose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:3)


**וְהַמְקַמֵּץ.** מְקַבֵּץ צוֹאַת כְּלָבִים:


והמקמץ – who collects the feces of dogs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:4)
