**וְהַמְצָרֵף נְחֹשֶׁת.** הַמְחַתֵּךְ נְחֹשֶׁת מִמְּקוֹם מוֹצָאוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ, וּמַסְרִיחַ הוּא:


והמצרף נחשת – cuts off the copper from the place where he finds it from the land and it smells badly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:5)


**וְהַבֻּרְסִי.** מְעַבֵּד עוֹרוֹת:


והבורסי – dresses hides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 7:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/7:10:6)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמְּמִקָּתוֹ.** מְמַסְמַסְתּוֹ. לְשׁוֹן הָמֵק בְּשָׂרוֹ (זכריה יד). וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מפני שממיקתו – makes it loathsome. It is the language of (Zechariah 14:12): “Their flesh shall rot away,” and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה.** עַד שֶׁלֹּא תִתְאָרֵס. וְנִתְאָרְסָה:


האשה. עד שלא תתארס – and she became betrothed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים תִּמְכֹּר.** בְּעוֹדָהּ אֲרוּסָה, אֲבָל מִשֶּׁנִּשֵּׂאת לֹא:


ב"ש אומרים תמכור – while she is betrothed, but not from when she gets married.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:3)


**הוֹאִיל וְזָכָה בָאִשָּׁה.** שֶׁהִיא אֲרוּסָתוֹ:


הואיל וזכה באשה – that she is his betrothed,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:4)


**לֹא יִזְכֶּה בַנְּכָסִים.** בִּתְמִיהָּ:


should he not take possession of her property, in astonishment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:1:5)


**עַל הַחֲדָשִׁים.** שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ מִשֶּׁנִּשֵּׂאת אָנוּ בוֹשִׁין מָה רָאוּ חֲכָמִים לוֹמַר אִם מָכְרָה וְנָתְנָה הַבַּעַל מוֹצִיא מִיַּד הַלָּקוֹחוֹת, אֶלָּא שֶׁאַתֶּם מְגַלְגְּלִים עָלֵינוּ נְכָסִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ בְּעוֹדָהּ אֲרוּסָה לוֹמַר שֶׁאִם מָכְרָה מִכְרָהּ בָּטֵל לְפִי שֶׁזָּכָה בָהֶם הַבַּעַל:


על החדשים – [inheritance] that fell/came to her from when she got married, we are confounded: What did the Sages see to state that if she sold them or gave them away, the husband removes [them] from the hand of those who bought the property, but rather, that you burden us with property that fell/came to her while she was still betrothed meaning to say, that if she sold it, her sale is void, because the husband took possession of them.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:2:1)


**נְכָסִים יְדוּעִים לַבַּעַל וּנְכָסִים שֶׁאֵין יְדוּעִים.** אִית דִּמְפָרְשִׁים נְכָסִים יְדוּעִים, מְקַרְקְעֵי. וְשֶׁאֵין יְדוּעִים, מִטַּלְטְלֵי. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי, אֵלּוּ וָאֵלּוּ יְדוּעִים הֵם. וְאֵלּוּ הֵם שֶׁאֵינָם יְדוּעִים, כָּל שֶׁהִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת כָּאן וְנָפְלוּ לָהּ נְכָסִים בִּמְדִינַת הַיָּם. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה, בֵּין נְכָסִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ עַד שֶׁלֹּא תִתְאָרֵס וּבֵין שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ מִשֶּׁנִּתְאָרְסָה, אִם מָכְרָה אוֹתָם אַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת, הַבַּעַל מוֹצִיא מִיַּד הַלָּקוֹחוֹת פֵּרוֹת בְּחַיֶּיהָ, וְגוּף הַקַּרְקַע לְאַחַר מִיתָתָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שֶׁחוֹלֵק בֵּין נְכָסִים הַיְדוּעִים לַבַּעַל לִנְכָסִים שֶׁאֵינָם יְדוּעִים:


נכסים ידועים לבעל ונכסים שאינן ידועים – there are those who interpret “known property” from land, and that which is “not known [property]” as movable property and there are those who state that both (i.e., landed property and movable possessions) are known and these are the ones that are not known: all [the while] that she sits/dwells here and property fell/came to her abroad. And the Halakhic decision is that whether [we are speaking of] property that fell/came to her until she had become betrothed, or whether they fell/came to her from after she was betrothed, if she sold them after she got married, the husband removes [them] from the hand of the purchaser – the usufruct during her lifetime and the body of the land after her death. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon who divides between property that is known to the husband to possessions which are not known.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:3:1)


**שָׁמִין אוֹתָן.** דְּקָסָבַר מַה שֶּׁגָּדֵל בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ הֲווּ פֵּרוֹת וּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא גָדֵל בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ הֲוֵי קֶרֶן. וּלְפִיכָךְ מַה שֶּׁדְּמֵי הַקַּרְקַע יְקָרִים עַכְשָׁיו בִּשְׁבִיל תְּבוּאָה זוֹ, הֲוֵי קֶרֶן, וְצָרִיךְ לִתֵּן לָהּ דָּמִים וְיִלָּקַח בָּהֶן קַרְקַע וְהוּא אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹתֶיהָ:


שמין אותן – for he (i.e., Rabbi Meir) holds that what grew in his domain, they are usufruct, and what didn’t grow in his domain, they are the principal; therefore, what that the monetary [value] of the land is expensive now for this produce, it is principal, and he needs to give her the money and purchase with them land and he eats its usufruct.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:3:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** אַף אֵלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא גָדְלוּ בִרְשׁוּתוֹ תּוֹרַת פֵּרוֹת נָתְנוּ בָהֶם, הוֹאִיל וְהַקַּרְקַע קַיְמָא לָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים – even those that did not grow in his domain, the status of usufruct has been given to them for since that the land belongs to her, and Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:4:1)


**בִּיצִיאָתָהּ.** אִם בָּא לְגָרְשָׁהּ:


ביציאתה – if he comes to divorce her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:4:2)


**פֵּרוֹת הַמְחֻבָּרִים לַקַּרְקַע, בִּכְנִיסָתָהּ שֶׁלּוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר בִּשְׁעַת נְפִילָתָן שֶׁלּוֹ, כְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים. וְאִיכָּא בֵּין חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פֵּרוֹת הַמְחֻבָּרִין בִּשְׁעַת יְצִיאָה, דְּלֹא אַיְרֵי בְּהוּ חֲכָמִים, וְלֵית לְהוּ הָא דְאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בִּיצִיאָתָהּ שֶׁלָּהּ, דְּקָסָבְרֵי מַה שֶּׁגָּדֵל בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


פירות המחוברים לקרקע בכניסתה שלו – meaning to say, at the time of their falling/coming [into inheritance] they are his (i.e., the husband’s) according to the words of the Sages, but there is a difference between the Sages and Rabbi Shimon regarding produce that was attached [to the ground] at the time of her going forth [from the marriage; i.e., divorce}, but the Sages do not speak of them and they don’t hold [this way] regarding that that Rabbi Shimon stated, that when she went forth [from the marriage because of divorce], whatever grew in his domain is his, but the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:1)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר לֹא תִמְכֹּר.** יְכוֹלָה הִיא לְעַכֵּב. וְהִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


רבן שמעון בן גמליאל אומר לא תמכור – she can prevent/detain it and the Halakha is according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר לֹא תִמְכֹּר.** וְהִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


רבי יהודה אומר לא תמכור – and the Halakha is according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:3)


**קִמְעָא.** דָּבָר מֻעָט וַאֲפִלּוּ כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


קימעא – a small amount and even just a little bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:5:4)


**יִשָּׁבַע כַּמָּה הוֹצִיא וְיִטֹּל.** וּשְׁבוּעָה זוֹ כְּעֵין שֶׁל תּוֹרָה הִיא, וְהַנֵּי מִילֵי, שֶׁהָיָה הַשֶּׁבַח כְּנֶגֶד הַהוֹצָאָה אוֹ יוֹתֵר, אֲבָל אִם הָיְתָה הַהוֹצָאָה יְתֵרָה עַל הַשֶּׁבַח יִשָּׁבַע שְׁבוּעַת הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁהוֹצִיא כְּנֶגֶד הַשֶּׁבַח, וְיִטֹּל כְּנֶגֶד הַשֶּׁבַח בִּלְבָד וְהַשְּׁאָר יַפְסִיד. וְכָל זֶה כְּשֶׁגֵּרְשָׁהּ הַבַּעַל, אֲבָל אִם מָרְדָה הִיא, בֵּין אָכַל הַבַּעַל בֵּין לֹא אָכַל, יִשָּׁבַע כַּמָּה הוֹצִיא וְיִטֹּל כְּנֶגֶד הַשֶּׁבַח. וּבְנִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל, כָּל הַשֶּׁבַח שֶׁל בַּעַל, דְּאָמְרִינַן אִם הוֹסִיפוּ הוֹסִיפוּ לוֹ:


ישבע במה הוציא ויטול – and this oath is similar to the Torah and these words [regard] that there was improvement/gain corresponding to the [financial] outlay or more, but if the outlay was greater than the improvement/gain, he should take the oath of the Torah that he made an outlay corresponding to the improvement/gain, and he should take corresponding to the improvement/gain and the rest he loses. And all of this [occurs] when the husband divorced her. But if she rebelled, whether the husband consumed or whether he did not consume, he should take an oath how much he spent and take corresponding to the improvement/gain and in property of mort-main, all the improvement/gain belongs to the husband, for we say, that if he increased [his outlay], he increased for himself.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:1)


**שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהּ נְכָסִים.** מִבֵּית אָבִיהָ, בְּעוֹדָהּ שׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם:


שנפלו לה נכסים – from her father’s house/estate while she is still a woman awaiting levirate marriage with her dead husband’s brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:2)


**מַה יַּעֲשׂוּ בִכְתֻבָּתָהּ.** מָנֶה וּמָאתַיִם וְתוֹסֶפֶת וּנְדוּנְיָא שֶׁהִכְנִיסָה לוֹ וְקִבֵּל עָלָיו אַחֲרָיוּת:


מה יעשו בכתובתה – [whether it is] a Maneh, or two-hundred and the supplement and the dowry that she brought in to him, go in with her [when she gets married], and when she goes out from her husband (i.e., gets divorced), go out with her and he has accepted upon himself surety (which may be resorted to in case of non-payment).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:3)


**וּבַנְּכָסִים הַנִּכְנָסִין וְהַיּוֹצְאִין.** נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג, שֶׁכְּשֶׁהִיא נִכְנֶסֶת נִכְנָסִים עִמָּהּ וּכְשֶׁיּוֹצֵאת מִבַּעְלָהּ יוֹצְאִים עִמָּהּ:


ובנכסים הנכנסין והיוצאין – usufruct that when she enters [into marriage], they enter with her and when she leaves from her husband, they leave with her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:4)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים יַחֲלֹקוּ יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל עִם יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאָב.** דְּסָפֵק נְשׂוּאָה לַיָּבָם הִיא וְזוֹכֶה בַּחֲצִי יְרֻשָּׁתָהּ מִסָּפֵק:


בית שמאי אומרים יחלוקו יורשי הבעל עם יורשי האב – for she was doubtfully married to the levir and he takes possession of one half of her inheritance from doubt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:6:5)


**נְכָסִים בְּחֶזְקָתָן.** נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל בְּחֶזְקָתָן. וְלֹא אִתְפָּרֵשׁ בְּחֶזְקַת מִי, אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל הוֹאִיל וְאַחֲרָיוּתָן עָלָיו, אִי בְּחֶזְקַת יוֹרְשֵׁי הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהָיוּ שֶׁלָּהּ. וּבְפֶרֶק ד'‏ מִיבָמוֹת תִּמְצָא הַמִּשְׁנָה הַזֹּאת:


נכסים בחזקתן – the mort-main property are in their legal status/presumption and it is not explained in the possession of whom - if it is in the possession of the heirs of the husband, for since surety/property that may be resorted to in the event of non-payment is upon him or if [it is in the possession of] the heirs of the wife, which were hers, and in the fourth chapter of [Tractate] Yevamot (Mishnah 3), you will find this Mishnah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:1)


**יִלָּקַח בָּהֶן קַרְקַע.** לְפִי שֶׁכְּתֻבָּתָהּ עַל נִכְסֵי בַעְלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן, לְפִיכָךְ נִכְסֵי הַמֵּת אַחֲרָאִין לִכְתֻבָּתָהּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַיָּבָם אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת אִם מְיַבֵּם אוֹתָהּ, וְסָבַר מִטַּלְטְלֵי מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדֵי לַכְּתֻבָּה:


ילקח בהן קרקע – because her Ketubah is based upon the property of her first husband; therefore, the property of the dead is surety for her Ketubah but the levir eats from the usufruct and if he performs levirate marriage with her, and [and holds] that movable possessions are mortgaged to the Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:2)


**שָׁמִין אוֹתָם.** דְּכָל מַה שֶּׁגָּדֵל בִּרְשׁוּת הַמֵּת אַחֲרָאִין לַכְּתֻבָּה:


שמין אותם – all that grew in the domain of the dead [brother] is surety to the Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים פֵּרוֹת הַמְחֻבָּרִים לַקַּרְקַע שֶׁלּוֹ.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, וְהָא כָּל נְכָסָיו אַחֲרָאִין וְעַרְבָאִין לַכְּתֻבָּה, וּמְשָׁנֵי, תָּנֵי שֶׁלָּהּ:


וחכמים אומרים פירות המחוברים לקרקע שלו – In the Gemara (Tractate Ketubot 82a) it raises the question: but aren’t all his landed property a surety and a pledge for her Ketubah? And it answers (in the words of Resh Lakish): Read, “belongs to her” (the Sages’ dispute is limited to detached produce and money which, they maintain, as movables and not pledged to the Ketubah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:4)


**כָּל הַקּוֹדֵם זָכָה.** קָסָבְרֵי מִטַּלְטְלֵי לַכְּתֻבָּה לֹא מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדֵי אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן תָּפְסָה. וּמֵחַיִּים דְּבַעַל בָּעִינַן תְּפִיסָה. וְהוּא הַדִּין נַמִּי דִּפְלִיגֵי אַכְּסָפִים, דְּמַאי שְׁנָא כְּסָפִים מִפֵּרוֹת תְּלוּשִׁין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


כל הקודם זכה – for they hold that movables are not mortgaged to the Ketubah other than if she took hold of them and we require from the lifetime of the husband is the taking hold/possession and the same law applies regarding money, for is the difference of money from detached produce, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:7:5)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כְאִשְׁתּוֹ.** שֶׁמְּגָרְשָׁהּ בְּגֵט, וּמַחֲזִירָהּ. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן וְיִבְּמָהּ אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא וַעֲדַיִן יִבּוּמִין רִאשׁוֹנִים עָלֶיהָ וְלֹא תִסְגֵּי לָהּ בְּגֵט. וְהָכִי נַמִּי כְּשֶׁגֵּרְשָׁהּ הֲוָה אֲמִינָא מִצְוָה דְּרַמְיָא רַחֲמָנָא עָלֶיהָ הָא קָעָבְדָה, וְתֵיקוּ עֲלֵיהּ בְּאִסּוּר אֵשֶׁת אָח וְלֹא מָצֵי לְהַחֲזִירָהּ, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאָמַר רַחֲמָנָא (דברים כה) וּלְקָחָהּ לוֹ לְאִשָּׁה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלְּקָחָהּ הֲרֵי הִיא כְאִשְׁתּוֹ:


הרי היא כאשתו – for when he divorces her with a Jewish bill of divorce and restores her [as his wife], and we don’t speak about (Deuteronomy 25:5): “and perform the levir’s duty,” the All-Merciful said, and still the first levirate marriages are upon her and a Jewish bill of divorce is not sufficient for her, and that is so, that she was divorced, we would say that it a Mitzvah that the All-Merciful cast upon her that when she performs it and established upon her the prohibition of the wife of a brother , and he cannot bring her back, this comes to teach us that the All-Merciful stated (Deuteronomy 25:5): “he shall take her as his wife and perform the levir’s duty.” Since he took her, she is like his wife.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:1)


**כָּל נְכָסָיו.** שֶׁיָּרַשׁ מֵאָחִיו, אַחֲרָאִין לִכְתֻבָּתָהּ:


כל נכסיו – that he inherited from his brothers is surety for her Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:2)


**גֵּרְשָׁהּ אֵין לָהּ אֶלָּא כְּתֻבָּתָה.** אֲבָל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא גֵרֵשׁ הָיוּ כָל הַנְּכָסִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לָהּ:


גירשה אין לה אלא כתובה – but all the while that he did not divorce [her], all of his proerpty is mortgaged to her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/8:8:3)


**הֲרֵי הִיא כְּכָל הַנָּשִׁים.** שֶׁהַמְגָרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ וּמַחֲזִירָהּ, עַל מְנָת כְּתֻבָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה הֶחֱזִירָהּ. וְאִצְטְרִיךְ לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן הָא בִּיבָמָה, דְּלֹא תֵימָא אִשְׁתּוֹ הִיא דְּאִיהוּ כָּתַב לָהּ כְּתֻבָּה מִמָּמוֹנוֹ, אֲבָל יְבִמְתּוֹ דְּלָאו אִיהוּ כָּתַב לָהּ אֶלָּא נִכְסֵי בַעְלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן הוּא דַהֲווּ אַחֲרָאִין וְעַרְבָאִין, הֵיכָא דְגֵרְשָׁהּ וְאַהְדְּרָהּ. אֵימָא כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִנֵּיהּ, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


הרי היא ככל הנשים – for a person who divorces his wife and restored her [as his wife In remarriage] on the condition of her first Ketubah he has remarried her and it is necessary to teach us that regarding the widow of a brother who died without issue that she should not say that she is his wife, for he wrote for her a Ketubah from his money, but his widow of a brother who died without issue, who is not his, he wrote for her other than the property of her first husband, which were pledged and surety when he divorced her and restored her [as his wife]. I might sya this is her Ketubah from her. This comes to teach us that this is not the case.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:1)


**הַכּוֹתֵב לְאִשְׁתּוֹ דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי בִנְכָסַיִךְ.** בְּעוֹדָהּ אֲרוּסָה כּוֹתֵב לָהּ, לִכְשֶׁתִּנָּשְׂאִי לִי דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי בִנְכָסַיִךְ. אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא קָנוּ מִנֵּיהּ, מוֹכֶרֶת וְנוֹתֶנֶת וּמִכְרָהּ קַיָּם, שֶׁנַּחֲלָה הַבָּאָה לָאָדָם מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר, אָדָם מַתְנֶה עָלֶיהָ שֶׁלֹּא יִירָשֶׁנָּה. וְאִי קָנוּ מִנֵּיהּ, אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת מִכְרָהּ קַיָּם, אֲבָל פֵּרוֹת אוֹכֵל, וְאִם מֵתָה יוֹרְשָׁהּ, דְּהָכִי מַשְׁמַע, בִּנְכָסַיִךְ אֵין לִי דִין, אֲבָל בְּפֵרוֹתֵיהֶן יֵשׁ לִי דִין, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁהֵן נְכָסַיִךְ, שֶׁאַתְּ בַּחַיִּים, אֵין לִי דִין וּדְבָרִים בָּהֶם, הָא לְאַחַר מִיתָה יֵשׁ לִי בָהֶם דִּין וּדְבָרִים:


הכותב לאשתו דין ודברים אין לי בנכסיך – while she still is betrothed, he (i.e., the future husband) writes her: “When you marry me, I have [no] claims whatsoever on your property,” even though they were not acquired from him , she an sell them and give them and her sale is valid , for an inheritance that comes to a person of from another place, a man may make a condition upon her that she will not inherit it, and if they acquired I from her, even after she has married, her sale is valid, but he consumes the usufruct, and if she dies, he inherits her, for that is the implication of: “I have no claims on your property,” but their usufruct, I have a claim and as long as they are your property while you are alive, I have no claim on them – but after death, I have a claim on them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר לְעוֹלָם הוּא אוֹכֵל פֵּרֵי פֵרוֹת.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ אֵלּוּ הֵן פֵּרוֹת וְאֵלּוּ הֵן פֵּרֵי פֵרוֹת, הִכְנִיסָה לוֹ קַרְקַע וְעָשְׂתָה פֵרוֹת אֵלּוּ הֵן פֵּרוֹת, מָכַר אֵלּוּ הַפֵּרוֹת וְלָקַח בָּהֶן קַרְקַע וְעָשְׂתָה פֵרוֹת הֲרֵי הֵן פֵּרֵי פֵרוֹת. וּכְשֶׁאָמַר לָהּ דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי בִנְכָסַיִךְ וּבְפֵרוֹתֵיהֶן בִּלְבָד, אוֹכֵל פֵּרֵי פֵרוֹת לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, דְּהָא לֹא סְלִיק נַפְשֵׁיהּ אֶלָּא מִן הַפֵּרוֹת בִּלְבָד. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר לעולם הוא אוכל פירי פירות – In the Gemara (Tractate Ketubot 83b), it explains what is usufruct and what is usufruct of the usufruct (i.e., produce vs. yield of the produce)? If she brought in [to the marriage] land and it produced fruit/usufruct, these are usufruct. If he sold these fruits/usufruct and purchased with them fields and they produced fruit/usufruct, these are yield of the produce/usufruct of the usufruct. And when he said to her, I have no claim against your property and their usufruct alone, he can consume the usufruct of the usufruct , according to the words of Rabbi Yehuda, he did not remove himself other than from the usufruct/produce alone and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:1:3)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר אִם מֵתָה יִירָשֶׁנָּה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּתְנֶה עַל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כז) וְיָרַשׁ אוֹתָהּ, מִכָּאן שֶׁהַבַּעַל יוֹרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ. אֲבָל מַסְקָנָא דְמִלְּתָא, שֶׁיְּרֻשַּׁת הַבַּעַל אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ אֵינוֹ מִן הַתּוֹרָה אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, וּקְרָא אַסְמַכְתָּא בְּעַלְמָא. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל, לֹא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּתְנֶה עַל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁחֲכָמִים עָשׂוּ לָהּ חִזּוּק כְּשֶׁל תּוֹרָה:


רשב"ג אומר אם מתה יירשנה מפני שמתנה על מה שכתוב בתורה – as it is written (Numbers 27:11): “[If his father had no brothers, you shall assign his property to his nearest relative in his own clan,] and he shall inherit it.” From here [we learn] that the husband inherits his wife, but the final result of the matter is that the inheriting of wife by her husband is not from the Torah, but rather from the Rabbis, and the Biblical verse is a mere support, but nevertheless, the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel not because it makes a condition against what is written in the Torah, but rather because the Sages made it stringent measure for the protection of the law (i.e., the Written Torah).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:1)


**יִנָּתְנוּ לַכּוֹשֵׁל שֶׁבָּהֶן.** אִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי, לְמִי שֶׁשְּׁטָרוֹ מְאֻחָר, שֶׁהוּא כּוֹשֵׁל וְחַלָּשׁ שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִטְרֹף לָקוֹחוֹת הַקּוֹדְמִים לוֹ. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי, לִכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה, וְהִיא קְרוּיָה כּוֹשֵׁל שֶׁאֵין דַּרְכָּהּ לַחֲזֹר אַחַר נִכְסֵי הַמֵּת וּלְבַקֵּשׁ הֵיכָן יֶשׁ לוֹ קַרְקַע כְּמוֹ הָאִישׁ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי דְיַתְמֵי לֹא מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב וְלֹא לִכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה, הָכָא דְלָאו בִּרְשׁוּתַיְהוּ מַנְחֵי, סְבִירָא לְרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן דְּמוֹצִיאִין מִיַּד הַלֹּוֶה אוֹ מִיַּד שֶׁהַפִּקָּדוֹן אֶצְלוֹ וְנוֹתְנִין לְבַעַל חוֹב וְלִכְתֻבָּה:


ינתנו לכושל שבהן – there are those who interpret it as one whose promissory note was postdated (which is not disqualified necessarily by the Sages, provided that the lender was willing to accept it – as it presents no opportunity for defrauding other creditors) which is the weakest and with legal disadvantage of them all, for he cannot seize properties for a debt that precede it, and there are those who interpret that is for the Ketubah of a wife and it is called weak, and it is not her manner to return after the property of the dead and to request where he has land like the man. And even tough movables of orphans are not mortgaged to the creditor nor to the Ketubah of a woman, here, where they are not in their domain, he leaves them, Rabbi Tarfon holds that we remove them from the hand of the borrower or from the hand of the one holding the deposit and give them to creditor and for the Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:2)


**יִנָּתְנוּ לַיּוֹרְשִׁים.** וְלֹא מַהֲנְיָא תְּפִיסָה:


ינתנו ליורשים – possession does not take effect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:2:3)


**שֶׁכֻּלָּן צְרִיכִין שְׁבוּעָה.** שֶׁהַבָּא לִפָּרַע מִנִּכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים לֹא יִפָּרַע אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבוּעָה, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁבְּעוּ אֵין אָנוּ יוֹדְעִים אִם יֵשׁ לָהֶם עָלָיו כְּלוּם, הִלְכָּךְ מִשֶּׁמֵּת הַמֵּת זָכוּ בָהֶן הַיּוֹרְשִׁים וּבִרְשׁוּתָן הֵן:


שכולן צריכין שבועה – for a person who comes to collect from the property of orphans, he should not collect without an oath, and all the while that they did not take an oath, we don’t known if they have anything against him; therefore, when the dead person dies, he orphans took possession of them and they are in their domain.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:1)


**כָּל הַקּוֹדֵם בָּהּ זָכָה.** קָדְמוּ יוֹרְשִׁים, זָכוּ וְאֵין מוֹצִיאִין מִיָּדָם, דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי דְיַתְמֵי לֹא מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב וְלִכְתֻבָּה. קָדַם אֶחָד מֵהֶם הָאִשָּׁה אוֹ הַמַּלְוֶה, זָכָה הוּא, דְּרַבִּי טַרְפוֹן אִית לֵיהּ תְּפִיסָה דִּלְאַחַר מִיתָה מַהֲנְיָא:


כל הקודם בהם זכה – if the heirs came earlier, they take possession and we don’t remove it from their hands for orphans’ movables are not mortgaged to a creditor or for a Ketubah. If one of them – the wife or the lender he takes possession, [for Rabbi] Tarfon holds that taking possession after death has legal effect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:2)


**זָכְתָה אִשָּׁה יוֹתֵר עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** אִם קָדְמָה וְתָפְסָה וְיֵשׁ יוֹתֵר מִן הַכְּתֻבָּה, אוֹ קָדַם בַּעַל חוֹב וְתָפַס וְיֵשׁ יוֹתֵר עַל חוֹבוֹ:


זכתה אשה יותר על כתובתה – if she got there ealier and took hold of it and it has more than [the value of] the Ketubah or the creditor arrived first and seized it and there is more than the lien [due him].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:3:3)


**הַמּוֹתָר כוּ'‏ יִנָּתְנוּ לַכּוֹשֵׁל.** זֶה בַּעַל הַשְּׁטָר שֶׁיָּדוֹ עַל הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה. וְאִם יָבֹאוּ לְיַד הַיְתוֹמִים שׁוּב לֹא יוֹצִיאוּ מֵהֶם לֹא אִשָּׁה וְלֹא בַעַל חוֹב. וְהָאִידְנָא דִּנְהִיגֵי בְּכָל בָּתֵּי דִינִים שֶׁבְּיִשְׂרָאֵל דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי דְיַתְמֵי מִשַּׁעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב, מִי שֶׁמֵּת וְהִנִּיחַ מִטַּלְטְלִין וְהָיָה עָלָיו בַּעַל חוֹב וּכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה, כָּל הַקּוֹדֵם זָכָה, בֵּין בַּעַל חוֹב מֻקְדָּם בֵּין בַּעַל חוֹב מְאֻחָר, שֶׁאֵין דִּין קְדִימָה בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין. וְאִם לֹא קָדַם אֶחָד מֵהֶם, יְחַלְּקוּ הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין בֵּינֵיהֶם, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק מִי שֶׁהָיָה נָשׂוּי:


המותר – should be given to the weakest. This owner of the document whose had is at a disadvantage, if it were to come into the hand of the orphans, furthermore, he cannot remove it from them – neither the wife nor the creditor. But nowadays, we have the practice in all Jewish courts throughout Israel that possessions of the orphans are mortgaged to the creditor; a person who died and left movables, and he had on them a creditor and the Ketubah of a wife , all who comes first gained possession, whether the creditor was early or whether the creditor was later, for there is no antecedent with regard to movables. But if none of them came early, they divide the movables between themselves , as we state further on in Chapter [Ten, Mishnah 2) “He who was married.”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:1)


**חֶנְוָנִית.** לִמְכֹּר וְלִקְנוֹת בֶּחָנוּת:


חנונית – to sell and to buy in the store


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:2)


**אַפּוֹטְרוֹפָּא.** לְהִתְעַסֵּק בְּמָמוֹנוֹ וְלִשָּׂא וְלִתֵּן:


אפוטרופא – to deal with his money and engage in business.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:3)


**מַשְׁבִּיעָהּ כָּל זְמָן שֶׁיִּרְצֶה.** שְׁבוּעָה כְּעֵין שֶׁל תּוֹרָה עַל טַעֲנַת שֶׁמָּא, וְיָכוֹל לְגַלְגֵּל עָלֶיהָ שֶׁלֹּא עָשְׂתָה אוֹנָאָה עַל פִּלְכָּהּ וְעַל עִסָּתָהּ:


משביעה כל זמן שירצה – an oath like that of the Torah on a claim of perhaps, and he is able to assess her that she did not make a fraudulent representation/overcharging regarding her spindle and her dough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:4:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר.** אֲפִלּוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה שֶׁלֹּא עַל יְדֵי גִלְגּוּל יָכוֹל לְהַשְׁבִּיעָהּ עַל פִּלְכָּהּ וְעַל עִסָּתָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


... but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:1)


**מַשְׁבִּיעַ הוּא אֶת יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ.** אִם גֵּרְשָׁהּ וּמֵתָה וְיוֹרְשֶׁיהָ תּוֹבְעִין הֵימֶנּוּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, נִשְׁבָּעִים שְׁבוּעַת הַיּוֹרְשִׁים שֶׁלֹּא פְקַדְתָּנוּ בִּשְׁעַת מִיתָה וְלֹא אָמְרָה לָנוּ קֹדֶם לָכֵן וְלֹא מָצִינוּ בֵּין שְׁטָרוֹתֶיהָ שֶׁשְּׁטַר כְּתֻבָּתָהּ פָּרוּעַ:


משביע הוא את יורשיה – if he divorced her or she died and her heirs claim from him her Ketubah, they take the oath of heirs that she did not deposit anything with us at the time of death nor say to us anything before then and we did not find among her documents that the document of her Ketubah had been paid off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:2)


**וְאֶת הַבָּאִים בִּרְשׁוּתָהּ.** אִם מָכְרָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ לַאֲחֵרִים וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה וּמֵתָה וְהַלָּקוֹחוֹת תּוֹבְעִים כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, נִשְׁבָּעִים אַף הֵן שְׁבוּעַת הַיּוֹרְשִׁים:


ואת הבאים ברשותה – if she sold her Ketubah to others and she was divorced or died and the purchaser of mortgaged property claim her Ketubah, even they are bound to swear the oath of heirs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:3)


**אֲבָל יוֹרְשָׁיו מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ.** אִם נִתְאַלְמְנָה וְהִיא אוֹ יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ נִפְרָעִין מִן הַיְתוֹמִים, צְרִיכִים שְׁבוּעָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא פְטָרָן אֶלָּא מִמֶּנּוּ אִם תִּגְבֶּה כְתֻבָּתָהּ בְּחַיָּיו:


אבל יורשיו משביעין אותה – if she became a widow and he or her heirs collect money from the orphans, they require an oath, for they are not exempted other than from him, if she collects her Ketubah settlement during his lifetime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:5:4)


**לַבָּאִין בִּרְשׁוּתִי.** אִם אֶמְכֹּר נְכָסַי וְאַתְּ בָּאָה לִפָּרַע מִן הַלָּקוֹחוֹת:


לבאין ברשותי – if I sell my property and you come to collect from the bought properties.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:1)


**הָלְכָה מִקֶּבֶר בַּעְלָהּ.** זוֹ שֶׁפְּטָרָהּ בַּעְלָהּ מִן הַשְּׁבוּעָה:


והלכה מקבר מעלה – this one who exempted her husband from the oath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:2)


**לְבֵית אָבִיהָ.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְעַסְּקָה שׁוּב בַּנְּכָסִים:


לבית אביה – who did not engaged further in property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:3)


**אֵין הַיּוֹרְשִׁים מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ.** בְּמַה שֶּׁנִּתְעַסְּקָה בֵּין מִיתָה לִקְבוּרָה. שֶׁאִם הָיוּ יוֹרְשִׁים יְכוֹלִים לְהַשְׁבִּיעָהּ עַל כָּךְ, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהִיא צְרִיכָה לְהָבִיא עֵדִים בְּמַה שֶּׁהִיא מוֹכֶרֶת וְקוֹנָה לְצֹרֶךְ קְבוּרַת הַמֵּת, יִשְׁהֶה הַמֵּת וְיִתְנַוֵּל:


אין היורשים משביעין אותה – with what she had engaged in between death and the burial for if the heirs are able to cause her to take an oath on this while she needs to bring witnesses in what she is selling and buying for the needs of the burial of the dead, the dead body will be delayed [from being buried] and will be disgraced.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:4)


**עַל הֶעָתִיד לָבֹא.** שֶׁלֹּא עִכְּבָה בְיָדֶיהָ כְּלוּם מִן הָעֵסֶק שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית אַפּוֹטְרוֹפָּא עָלָיו לְאַחַר קְבוּרַת בַּעְלָהּ, דְּלֹא מַהֲנְיָא בֵיהּ פְּטוּר דִּידֵיהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁל יְתוֹמִים הֵם:


על העתיד לבוא – she did not delay in her hands at all from the business that was done by the guardian upon him after the burial of her husband and he did not benefit from his exemption, for the property belongs to the orphans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:6:5)


**עַל שֶׁעָבָר.** עַל עֵסֶק שֶׁבְּחַיֵּי בַעְלָהּ:


על שעבר – on the business that was during the lifetime of her husband.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:1)


**לֹא תִפָּרַע אֶלָּא בִשְׁבוּעָה.** הַנִּפְרָע מֵחֲבֵרוֹ אֵינוֹ מְדַקְדֵּק לָשׂוּם לֵב עַל כָּל מַה שֶּׁפְּרָעוֹ, וְזוֹ הוֹאִיל וְנִפְרְעָה בְּמִקְצָת אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁנִּפְרְעָה הַכֹּל, וְרָמוּ רַבָּנָן שְׁבוּעָה עֲלָהּ כִּי הֵיכִי דְתִידוֹק:


לא תפרע אלא בשבועה – a person who collects payment from his colleague is not exacting to pay attention of what he has collected and this since he was paid in part, it is possible that he was paid in full, and the Rabbis placed upon him an oath in order that he pay special attention.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:2)


**עֵד אֶחָד מֵעִיד שֶׁהִיא פְרוּעָה לֹא תִפָּרַע אֶלָּא בִשְׁבוּעָה.** תַּקָּנַת חֲכָמִים הִיא, כְּדֵי לְהָפִיס דַּעְתּוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל. וּשְׁבוּעוֹת הַלָּלוּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁתַּקָּנַת חֲכָמִים הֵן, אֵינָן אֶלָּא כְּעֵין שֶׁל תּוֹרָה בִּנְקִיטַת חֵפֶץ, שֶׁכָּל שְׁבוּעוֹת שֶׁתִּקְּנוּ בַּמִּשְׁנָה כְּעֵין שֶׁל תּוֹרָה הֵן:


עד אחד מעיד שהיא פרועה לא תפרע אלא בשבועה – it is the ordinance of the Sages in order to appease the mind of the owner and these oaths, even though they are the ordinance of the Sages, they are not other than like that of the Torah, in seizing an object for all of the oaths that the Sages ordained are like those of the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:7:3)


**מִנְּכָסִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאִי הֲוָה גָבֵי מִן הַלֹּוֶה גּוּפֵיהּ וַהֲוָה טָעֵין לֹוֶה אִשְׁתַּבַּע לִי דְּלֹא פְרַעְתִּיךָ, מַשְׁבְּעִינַן לֵיהּ, וַאֲנַן טָעֲנִינַן בִּשְׁבִיל לָקוֹחוֹת דִּלְמָא אִי הֲוָה גָבִית מִן הַלֹּוֶה הֲוָה טָעֵין לָךְ, אִשְׁתַּבַּע לִי דְּלֹא פְרַעְתִּיךָ, וּבָעֵית אִשְׁתַּבּוֹעֵי, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי אִשְׁתַּבַּע:


מנכסים משועבדים – because if he would collect from the borrower himself, the borrower would claim: “swear to me that I have not paid you,” we cause him to take an oath and we make a claim because of the mortgaged properties, for perhaps, if you had collected the borrower, he would claim against you: “swear to me that I have not paid you and you require that I should also take an oath now.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:8:1)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן.** קָאֵי לְעֵיל אַרַבָּנָן דְּאָמְרֵי הַמּוֹשִׁיב אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ חֶנְוָנִית אוֹ אַפּוֹטְרוֹפָּא מַשְׁבִּיעָהּ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, וְאִם כָּתַב לָהּ נֶדֶר וּשְׁבוּעָה אֵין לִי וְלֹא לְיוֹרְשַׁי עָלַיִךְ אֵין יוֹרְשִׁין מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ, וַאֲתָא הוּא לְאַפְלוֹגֵי וְאָמַר דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא תוֹבַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ יוֹרְשִׁין מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ וַאֲפִלּוּ כָּתַב לָהּ נֶדֶר וּשְׁבוּעָה אֵין לִי וְלֹא לְיוֹרְשַׁי עָלַיִךְ. וְאִם אֵינָהּ תּוֹבַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ אֵין יוֹרְשִׁין מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ עַל אַפּוֹטְרוֹפָּא שֶׁבְּחַיֵּי בַעְלָהּ, וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא פְטָרָהּ מִן הַשְּׁבוּעָה, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וּמַחֲלֻקְתּוֹ דְּאָמְרֵי לְעֵיל בְּמַתְנִיתִין מַשְׁבִּיעָהּ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁיִּרְצֶה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון – This [statement] refers to what is above (see Mishnah 4), referring to the Rabbis who stated that a person sets up his wife as a storekeeper or appointed her as a guardian, he may impose upon her an oath [that she has not misappropriated any of his property any time that he wants, and if he wrote her: “I don’t nor do my heirs have a vow or an oath against you,” the heirs cannot cause her to take an oath. And he comes to dispute and state that whenever she claims her Ketubah, the heirs force her to take an oath and even if he wrote her that neither he nor his heirs have on her a vow or an oath, if she does not claim her Ketubah, the heirs cannot force her to take an oath on being a guardian during the lifetime of her husband, and even if he did not exempt her from the oath, for Rabbi Eliezer and his dispute (even on her spindle and on her dough, they may force her to take an oath) that we stated above (see the end of Mishnah 4), that we force her to take an oath whenever he wants, and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:1)


**הוֹצִיאָה גֵט וְאֵין עִמּוֹ כְתֻבָּה.** בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁאֵין כּוֹתְבִים כְּתֻבָּה וְסוֹמְכִים עַל תְּנַאי בֵית דִּין:


הוציאה גט ואין עמו כתובה – in a place where they don’t write a Ketubah, and they rely upon the stipulation of the Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:2)


**גּוֹבָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ.** דִּתְנַאי כְתֻבָּה מַעֲשֵׂה בֵית דִּין הוּא, וְכָל מַעֲשֵׂה בֵית דִּין כְּמַאן דְּנָקֵיט שְׁטָרָא דָּמֵי. וְדַוְקָא מָנֶה מָאתַיִם הוּא דְגַבְיָא, אֲבָל תּוֹסֶפֶת אֵין לָהּ עַד שֶׁתּוֹצִיא שְׁטַר כְּתֻבָּה:


גובה כתובתה – for it is a stipulation of the Ketubah is an act of the Jewish court and all acts of the Jewish court are like one that holds a document. And specifically, one that is a Maneh or two hundred [Zuz] is collected, but the supplement has no testimony that she should remove the document of the Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:3)


**פְּרוֹזְבּוּל.** הִלֵּל תִּקֵּן פְּרוֹזְבּוּל כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תַשְׁמֵט שְׁבִיעִית, שֶׁמּוֹסֵר שְׁטָרוֹתָיו לְבֵית דִּין שֶׁיִּגְבֶּה מִן הַלֹּוֶה חוֹבוֹ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁיִּתְבְּעֶנּוּ, דְּהַשְׁתָּא לֹא קָרֵינָא בֵיהּ לֹא יִגֹּשׂ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ תוֹבְעוֹ כְלוּם אֶלָּא בֵּית דִּין תּוֹבְעִין:


פרוזבול – Hillel ordained Prozbul in order that the seventh year would not cancel [a debt] forhe would transfer his documents to the Jewish court which would collect from the borrower his obligation all the while that he would make a claim against him, for now we do not call him (Deuteronomy 15:2): “he shall not dun [his fellow or kinsman, for the remission proclaimed is of the LORD],” for he does not claim anything but rather, the Jewish court makes a claim against him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:4)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ לֹא יִפָּרֵעוּ.** חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא כְּבָר גָּבְתָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ, וְזֶה הִשְׁמִיטָתוֹ שְׁבִיעִית:


הרי אלו א יפרעו – we suspect lest her Ketubah [settlement] was already collected, and [regarding Prozbul] the seventh year caused the release from debt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/9:9:5)


**מִן הַסַּכָּנָה.** שֶׁגָּזְרוּ גּוֹיִם עַל הַמִּצְוֹת וְהָיוּ יְרֵאִים לִשְׁמֹר גִּטֵּיהֶן, וּמִשֶּׁקִּבְּלָתוֹ שׂוֹרַפְתּוֹ. וְכֵן פְּרוֹזְבּוּלֵיהֶן:


מן הסכנה – that the heathens decreed against [performance of] the commandments, and they were afraid to keep their Jewish bills of divorce and when she would receive it, she would burn it and similar with their Prozbul documents.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:1:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיָה.** יוֹרְשֵׁי הָרִאשׁוֹנָה קוֹדְמִים לְיוֹרְשֵׁי הַשְּׁנִיָּה. אִם מֵתוּ נָשָׁיו אַחֲרָיו עַד שֶׁלֹּא הִסְפִּיקוּ לִגְבּוֹת:


מי שהיה. יורשי הראשונה קודמם ליורשי השניה – if his wives died after him until they did not have sufficient time to collect [their Ketubah settlement].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:1:2)


**שְׁנִיָּה וְיוֹרְשֶׁיהָ קוֹדְמִים.** שֶׁהִיא בַּעֲלַת חוֹב, וְיוֹרְשֵׁי רִאשׁוֹנָה בָּאִים לִירָשׁ מֵאֲבִיהֶן כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין, דְּהָא אִינוּן יִרְתוּן תְּנַן, לְפִיכָךְ פּוֹרְעִין אֶת הַחוֹב תְּחִלָּה, וּמַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר יִירְשׁוּ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁבְּעָה הַשְּׁנִיָּה עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, אָז הִיא אוֹ יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ נוֹטְלִים כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. אֲבָל אִם מֵתָה הִיא לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּת בַּעְלָהּ וְלֹא נִשְׁבְּעָה עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, אֵין יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ נוֹטְלִים כְּלוּם מִכְּתֻבָּתָהּ, דְּקַיְמָא לָן אֵין אָדָם מוֹרִישׁ שְׁבוּעָה לְבָנָיו, כְּלוֹמַר מָמוֹן שֶׁאֵין אָדָם זוֹכֶה בוֹ אֶלָּא בִשְׁבוּעָה וּמֵת קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּע, אוֹתוֹ מָמוֹן אֵין אָדָם מוֹרִישׁוֹ לְבָנָיו:


שניה ויורשיה קודמים – that she is a creditor and the inheritors of the first wife come to inherit from their father, according to the Ketubah “male issue,” for we are inheritors, it is taught in the Mishnah. Therefore, they pay the obligation first and what remains they shall succeed to the dowry, therefore, we pay the obligation first and what remains, they inherit, and specifically when the second [wife] took an oath on her Ketubah, then she or her heirs take her Ketubah. But if she died after her husband passed away, and she did not take an oath on her Ketubah, her heirs do not take anything from her Ketubah for we hold that a person does not bequeath an oath to his children, meaning to say, money that a person doesn’t take possession of other than through an oath, and if he dies prior to his taking an oath, that money a man cannot bequeath to his children.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:1)


**וִיתוֹמִים מְבַקְשִׁים כְּתֻבַּת אִמָּן.** שֶׁכְּתֻבָּה שֶׁל אַחַת מְרֻבָּה מִשֶּׁל חֲבֶרְתָּהּ, וְאוֹמְרִים בָּנֶיהָ כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין נִטֹּל, וְכֵן אַתֶּם, וְהַשְּׁאָר נַחֲלֹק:


ויתומים מבקשים כתובת אמן – since the Ketubah [of] their mother was greater than that of the other, and her children say that the Ketubah of the male issue is taken, and similarly you (the children of the other wife) and the rest we will divide.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:2)


**חוֹלְקִים בְּשָׁוֶה.** כִּשְׁאָר כָּל הַיְרֻשּׁוֹת, וְאֵין נוֹטְלִים כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין:


חולקים בשוה – like the rest of all the inheritances and they don’t take the Ketubah of male issue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:3)


**אִם אָמְרוּ יְתוֹמִים.** בְּנֵי הַכְּתֻבָּה הַגְּדוֹלָה:


אם אמרו יתומים – the children of the larger Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:2:4)


**הֲרֵי אָנוּ מַעֲלִין עַל נִכְסֵי אָבִינוּ.** מַעֲלִין דְּמֵיהֶן לְקַבְּלָם עָלֵינוּ בְּיֹקֶר, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שָׁם מוֹתַר דִּינָר וְיִטְּלוּ כְתֻבַּת אִמָּן:


הרי אנו מעלין על נכסי אבינו – we reckon their funds upon us to receive them as of a higher price in order that there will an additional Denar and they take the Ketubah of their mother.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:1)


**הָיוּ שָׁם נְכָסִים בָּרָאוּי.** כְּגוֹן עִסְקָא שֶׁבְּיַד אֲחֵרִים אוֹ הַלְוָאָה:


היו שם נכסים בראוי – as for example, merchandise that is in the hands of others or a loan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:2)


**אֵינָן כְּבַמֻּחְזָק.** אֵינָן נֶחְשָׁבִים לִהְיוֹת כְּאִלּוּ הֵן מֻחְזָקִים בְּיָדוֹ וְיֵשׁ כָּאן מוֹתַר דִּינָר:


אינן כבמוחזק – they are not considered to be as if they are in possession in his hand and there is here and there is an excess of a Denar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:3)


**שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם אַחֲרָיוּת.** מִטַּלְטְלִים:


שאין להם אחריות – movable possessions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:3:4)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם אַחֲרָיוּת.** קַרְקָעוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן. וְאַף בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה דִּנְהִיגֵי דְּמִטַּלְטְלֵי דְיַתְמֵי מְשַׁעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב, אֵין כְּתֻבַּת בְּנִין דִּכְרִין נוֹהֶגֶת בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין אֶלָּא בְקַרְקָעוֹת:


שיש להם אחריות – land/property and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon and even at this time where we have the practice that movable property of the orphans is mortgaged to the creditor, the Ketubah of male issue is not practiced with movable possessions, but only with land/property.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:1)


**שֶׁל זוֹ מָנֶה וְשֶׁל זוֹ מָאתַיִם כוּ'.** וּשְׁלָשְׁתָּן נֶחְתְּמוּ בְּיוֹם אֶחָד. דְּאִי בִּתְלָתָא יוֹמֵי, הַקּוֹדֶמֶת בַּשְּׁטָר קוֹדֶמֶת בַּגִּבּוּי. אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא הִנִּיחַ אֶלָּא מִטַּלְטְלִין, שֶׁאֵין דִּין קְדִימָה בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין:


של זו מנה ושל זו מאתים כו' – and the [Ketubot of the] three of them were signed on one day, for if it were on three [separate] days, the earliest one with a document comes first in collection, or if he didn’t leave anything other than movable possessions, for there is no law of precedence in movable possessions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:2)


**חוֹלְקוֹת בְּשָׁוֶה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי כֹּחַ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן שָׁוֶה בְּשִׁעְבּוּד מָנֶה, דִּבְכֻלְּהוּ אִיכָּא מָנֶה:


חולקות בשוה – for the power of them three of them similar with the hypothecary obligation of the Maneh and for all of them there is a Maneh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:3)


**הָיוּ שָׁם מָאתַיִם.** אֵין לְבַעֲלַת מָנֶה שִׁעְבּוּד אֶלָּא בְּמָנֶה, אֲבָל בְּמָנֶה שֵׁנִי אֵין שִׁעְבּוּד לַשְּׁטָר שֶׁל בַּעֲלַת מָנֶה:


היו שם מאתים – there isn’t for the person who has hypothecary obligation for a Maneh other than a Maneh, but the second Maneh there is no hypothecary obligation for a document of the owner of the Maneh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:4)


**שֶׁל מָנֶה נוֹטֶלֶת חֲמִשִּׁים.** בַּגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, שְׁלִישִׁית מָנֶה הָיָה רָאוּי שֶׁתִּטֹּל, וְהֵיאַךְ נוֹטֶלֶת חֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁהוּא חֲצִי מָנֶה. וּמְשָׁנֵי, דְּמַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי דְּאָמְרָה לָהּ בַּעֲלַת מָאתַיִם לְבַעֲלַת מָנֶה, דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי עִמָּךְ בַּמָּנֶה הַמְשֻׁעְבָּד לָךְ וְלֹא יִתְמַעֵט חֶלְקֵךְ בִּשְׁבִילִי, הִלְכָּךְ הִיא וּבַעֲלַת שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת חוֹלְקוֹת אוֹתוֹ. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁלֹּא נָתְנָה בַּעֲלַת הַמָּאתַיִם לְבַעֲלַת הַמָּנֶה חֶלְקָהּ בְּמַתָּנָה אֶלָּא אָמְרָה לָהּ שֶׁלֹּא תָרִיב עִמָּהּ וּבִשְׁבִילָהּ לֹא יִתְמַעֵט חֶלְקָהּ, לְאַחַר שֶׁלָּקְחָה בַּעֲלַת הַמָּנֶה חֲמִשִּׁים נִשְׁאַר זְכוּת בַּעֲלַת הַמָּאתַיִם שָׁוֶה עִם זְכוּת בַּעֲלַת הַשְּׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת בַּמָּנֶה וָחֵצִי הַנִּשְׁאָר, וְכָל אַחַת מֵהֶן נוֹטֶלֶת שְׁלֹשָׁה דִינְרֵי זָהָב שֶׁהֵן שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה דִינָרִים שֶׁל כֶּסֶף, שֶׁכָּל דִּינָר זָהָב הוּא עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה דִינָרִים שֶׁל כֶּסֶף:


של מנה נוטלת חמשים – In the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 93a), the question is raised that one-third of a Maneh was appropriate to be taken, and how does she take “fifty” which is one-half of a Maneh and it answers that the our Mishnah is speaking about when the owner of the two hundred said to the owner of the Maneh: I have no claim and I don’t have anything to do with the Maneh that is subjugated to you, and your portion will not be reduced on my account; therefore, she and the owner of the three hundred divide it, and because the portion of owner of the two hundred was not given to the owner of the portion of the Maneh as a gift, but rather she said to her that she would not quarrel with her, and because of her, her portion would not be reduced after the owner of the Maneh took fifty, there remained the merit of the owner of the “two hundred” as equal with the merit of the owner of the “three hundred,” and each of them take three golden Denarim, which are seventy five silver Denarim as every golden Denare is twenty-five silver Denarim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:5)


**הָיוּ שָׁם שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת.** מָנֶה רִאשׁוֹן מְשֻׁעְבָּד לְכֻלָּם, וְהַשֵּׁנִי לְבַעֲלַת מָאתַיִם וּלְבַעֲלַת שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת, וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי לְבַעֲלַת שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת בִּלְבָד:


היו שם שלש מאות – the first Maneh is subjugated to everyone and the second is to the owner of the “two hundred” and to the owner of the “three hundred,” and the third to the owner of the “three hundred” alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:4:6)


**שֶׁל מָנֶה נוֹטֶלֶת חֲמִשִּׁים וְשֶׁל מָאתַיִם מָנֶה.** כְּגוֹן דְּאָמְרָה לְהוּ בַּעֲלַת שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת לְבַעֲלַת מָנֶה וּלְבַעֲלַת מָאתַיִם, דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי עִמָּכֶם בַּמָּנֶה, הִלְכָּךְ מָנֶה הָרִאשׁוֹן חוֹלְקוֹת בַּעֲלַת מָאתַיִם וּבַעֲלַת מָנֶה, נִמְצֵאת בַּעֲלַת מָנֶה נוֹטֶלֶת חֲמִשִּׁים, וּמָנֶה הַשֵּׁנִי חוֹלְקוֹת בַּעֲלַת מָאתַיִם וּבַעֲלַת שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת, נִמְצֵאת בַּעֲלַת מָאתַיִם נוֹטֶלֶת מָנֶה, חֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁחָלְקָה מֵהַמָּנֶה הָרִאשׁוֹן עִם בַּעֲלַת הַמָּנֶה, וַחֲמִשִּׁים מֵהַמָּנֶה הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁחָלְקָה עִם בַּעֲלַת הַשְּׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת. וְהַמָּנֶה הַשְּׁלִישִׁי נוֹטֶלֶת כֻּלּוֹ בַּעֲלַת הַשְּׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת, נִמְצֵאת נוֹטֶלֶת שִׁשָּׁה דִינְרֵי זָהָב שֶׁהֵם מָנֶה וּמֶחֱצָה, הַמָּנֶה הַשְּׁלִישִׁי כֻּלּוֹ שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר לָהּ וַחֲצִי הַמָּנֶה שֶׁחָלְקָה עִם בַּעֲלַת הַמָּאתַיִם. וּבַגְּמָרָא מַסִּיק דְּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי נָתָן הִיא וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה, דְּאָמַר רַבִּי אֵין אֲנִי רוֹאֶה דְבָרָיו שֶׁל רַבִּי נָתָן בָּזֶה, אֶלָּא חוֹלְקוֹת בְּשָׁוֶה, דְּהוֹאִיל וְכָל נְכָסָיו אַחֲרָאִין לַכְּתֻבָּה, כָּל שְׁלֹשֶׁת הַמָּנִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לְבַעֲלַת הַמָּנֶה כִּשְׁאָר חַבְרוֹתֶיהָ, עַד שֶׁתִּגְבֶּה כָּל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ חוֹלְקוֹת בְּשָׁוֶה, וְכָךְ נוֹטֶלֶת בַּעֲלַת הַמָּנֶה כְּמוֹ בַעֲלַת הַמָּאתַיִם וּשְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת. אֲבָל בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁהִטִּילוּ מָעוֹת לַכִּיס זֶה מָנֶה וְזֶה מָאתַיִם וְזֶה שְׁלֹשׁ מֵאוֹת, דְּהָתָם שֶׁבַח שֶׁהִשְׁבִּיחוּ מְעוֹתֵיהֶן קָא שָׁקְלֵי, דִּינָא הוּא שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד יִטֹּל לְפִי מְעוֹתָיו. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהִשְׁבִּיחוּ מֵחֲמַת הַמָּעוֹת עַצְמָן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנָּה הַמַּטְבֵּעַ אוֹ הוֹסִיפוּ עָלָיו אוֹ פִחֲתוּ, אָז חוֹלְקִים הַשֶּׁבַח וְהַהֶפְסֵד לְפִי הַמָּמוֹן. אֲבָל אִם קָנוּ סְחוֹרָה מִן הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁהִטִּילוּ לַכִּיס וְהִרְוִיחוּ בַסְּחוֹרָה אוֹ הִפְסִידוּ, אֵין מְחַלְּקִים הָרֶוַח וְהַהֶפְסֵד אֶלָּא לְפִי מִסְפַּר הַשֻּׁתָּפִין וְלֹא לְפִי הַמָּמוֹן, וְכָךְ נוֹטֵל בַּהֶפְסֵד וּבַשֶּׁבַח מִי שֶׁנּוֹתֵן בַּכִּיס מָעוֹת מֻעָטִים כְּמוֹ אוֹתוֹ שֶׁנָּתַן מָעוֹת מְרֻבִּים, אִם לֹא הִתְנוּ תְּחִלָּה שֶׁיְּחַלְּקוּ לְפִי מָמוֹן. וְכֵן דָּנִים בְּכָל בָּתֵּי דִינִים:


של מנה נוטלת חמשים ושל מאתים מנה – such as the example where the owner of three hundred said to the owner of the Maneh and to the owner of the “two hundred”: “I have no claim against you with the Maneh.” Therefore, the first Maneh the owner of the “two hundred” and the owner of the Maneh divide; it is found that the owner of the Maneh takes “fifty” and the second Maneh, the owners of the “two hundred” and the “three hundred”; it is found that the owner of “two hundred” takes a Maneh – fifty that was divided from the first Mnaeh with the owner of the Maneh and fifty from the second Maneh that she divided with the owner of the “three-hundred,” and third Maneh – the owner of the “three hundred” takes it all; it is found that she takes six golden Denars which a Maneh and one-half of the entire third Maneh that remains to her and one-half of the Maneh that she divided with the owner of the “two hundred.” And in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 93a) reaches the conclusion that our Mishnah is [according to] Rabbi Natan and is not the Halakha, for Rabbi [Yehuda HaNasi] sai: I do not approve (literally, “see”) of Rabbi Natan’s views in these cases for [the three wives] take equal shares, for since all of his landed property is pledged to the Ketubah, all three of the Manehs are subjugated to the owner of the Maneh like the rest of her colleagues until she collects all of the Ketubah settlement; therefore, they divide it equally, and as such, the owner of the Maneh takes like the owner of the “two hundred and the “three hundred.” But the three who put their money into a single purse, this one of Maneh, and that one of “two hundred” and the other of “three hundred,” the profit in their monies that was grew in value, they take a Denar – for each one takes according to his funds. And specifically, when they grew in value as a result of the monies themselves, such as the coinage changed or they added to it or subtracted from it, then they divide the prophet or the loss according to the money, but if they purchased goods from the monies that they placed into the purse and they earned in value through the goods or lost, they do not divide the gain or the loss other than according to the number of partners, not according to the money and as such they take in the loss and/or in the gain – whomever placed in a small amount of money in the purse is like that individual that placed a lot of money, if they not make a condition from the beginning that they would divide it according to thej money. And such is how they judge in all the Jewish courts.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:1)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה קוֹדֶמֶת לַשְּׁנִיָּה.** אוֹתָהּ שֶׁזְּמַן כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מֻקְדָּם קוֹדֶמֶת לַשְּׁנִיָּה שֶׁזְּמַנָּהּ מְאֻחָר. וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


הראשונה קודמת לשניה – hers whose time of her Ketubah precedes that of the second, whose time is later, and similarly for all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:2)


**הָרִאשׁוֹנָה נִשְׁבַּעַת לַשְּׁנִיָּה.** אִם שְׁנִיָּה טוֹעֶנֶת הִשָּׁבְעִי לִי שֶׁלֹּא גָבִית מִשֶּׁל בַּעְלִי כְּלוּם, דִּלְמָא לֹא יִשָּׁאֵר לִי מִמַּה שֶּׁאֶגְבֶּה כְתֻבָּתִי. וְאַף שְׁלִישִׁית תֹּאמַר כֵּן לַשְּׁנִיָּה, וּרְבִיעִית לַשְּׁלִישִׁית. אֲבָל הָרְבִיעִית נִפְרַעַת שֶׁלֹּא בִשְׁבוּעָה, וּכְגוֹן שֶׁאֵין שָׁם יוֹרֵשׁ אוֹ בַעַל חוֹב אַחֵר שֶׁיַּשְׁבִּיעָהּ:


הראשונה נשבעת לשניה – if the second [wife] claims: “swear to me that you have not collected from my husband anything, for perhaps there will not remain for me from what I will collect my Ketubah and even the third [wife] will claim the same thing to the second [wife] and the fourth [wife] to the third, but the fourth collects without an oath as for example if there is no heir or another creditor who will make her take an oath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:5:3)


**בֶּן נַנָּס אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** פְּלוּגְתַּיְהוּ דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא וּבֶן נַנָּס מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּמְצֵאת שָׂדֶה אַחַת מֵאֵלּוּ הַשָּׂדוֹת שֶׁגָּבוּ הַשָּׁלֹשׁ נָשִׁים הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת, שֶׁאֵינָהּ שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁנּוֹדַע שֶׁגְּזָלָהּ וְסוֹף שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ בְעָלֶיהָ וְיִטְּלוּהָ, וּכְשֶׁבָּאָה הָרְבִיעִית לִגְבּוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִשָּׂדֶה רְבִיעִית בָּאָה זֹאת וְאוֹמֶרֶת לָהּ לְמָחָר יָבֹא הַנִּגְזָל וְיִטֹּל שָׂדֵהוּ מִיָּדִי, רְצוֹנִי שֶׁתִּשָּׁבְעִי לִי שֶׁלֹּא גָבִית כְּתֻבְּתִיךְ בְּחַיֵּי הַבַּעַל. תַּנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר בַּעַל חוֹב מְאֻחָר שֶׁקָּדַם וְגָבָה, מַה שֶּׁגָּבָה לֹא גָבָה, הִלְכָּךְ לָמָּה תִּשָּׁבַע, אִם יָבֹא הַנִּגְזָל וְיִטְרֹף מִזּוֹ תַּחֲזֹר הִיא עַל הָרְבִיעִית וְתִטֹּל מִמֶּנָּה מַה שֶּׁגָּבְתָה, דְּהַוְיָא לָהּ רְבִיעִית בַּעַל חוֹב מְאֻחָר. וּבֶן נַנָּס סָבַר, בַּעַל חוֹב מְאֻחָר שֶׁקָּדַם וְגָבָה, מַה שֶּׁגָבָה גָּבָה, וְאִם תַּחֲזִיק זֹאת אֵין הַשְּׁלִישִׁית יְכוֹלָה לַחֲזֹר עָלֶיהָ, וּלְכָךְ תִּשָּׁבַע לָהּ שֶׁלֹּא גָבְתָה מִנִּכְסֵי בַעְלָהּ כְּלוּם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא. וּמִמֵּילָא שָׁמְעִינַן שֶׁאִם תָּפְסָה מִטַּלְטְלִין שֶׁצְּרִיכָה לִשָּׁבַע לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין דִּין קְדִימָה בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין וּמַה שֶּׁגָבָה גָּבָה:


בן ננס אומר כו' – the disagreement of the first Tanna and Ben Nanos is explained in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 94a) as in the case where there is found one field from these field that the first three wives have collected which is not his, and it was known that he stole it, and eventually that its owners will come and take it and when the fourth [wife] comes to collect her Ketubah settlement from the fourth field, this one comes and says to her: “tomorrow the person who had been robbed will come and take his field; it is my desire that you will take an oath that you have not collected your Ketubah during the lifetime of the husband”; the First Tanna/teacher holds that the creditor of a later date who came early and collected, what he had collected, he did not collect; therefore, why should she take an oath if the person who was robbed will come and snatch it from this one. Let her return to the fourth [wife] and take from her what she collected, and hence the fourth [wife] would be a creditor of a later date. And Ben Nanos holds that a creditor o a later date who came early and collected what he collected, he has collected, and if she (i.e. the wife who came early) takes possession of this, the third [wife] cannot return to her, and therefore, takes an oath to her that she did not collect anything from the property of her husband and the Halakha is according to the First Tanna/teacher. Of itself, we learn that if she did not take hold of movable property, that she must take an oath, according to everyone’s opinion since there is no precedence as regards to movable property and what she collected, she collected.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:1)


**וְכָתְבָה רִאשׁוֹנָה לַלּוֹקֵחַ וְכוּ'.** וּכְגוֹן שֶׁקָּנוּ מִיָּדָהּ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ, וְתֵימָא נַחַת רוּחַ עָשִׂיתִי לְבַעְלִי. וּמְשָׁנֵי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁמָּכַר הַבַּעַל שָׂדֶה זוֹ לְאִישׁ אַחֵר קֹדֶם לָזֶה וְלֹא רָצְתָה הָאִשָּׁה לַחְתֹּם לוֹ, וְלָזֶה חָתְמָה, דְּאִם אִיתָא דְנַחַת רוּחַ עוֹשָׂה לְבַעְלָהּ הָיְתָה חוֹתֶמֶת לָרִאשׁוֹן:


וכתבה ראשונה ללוקח וכו' – as, for example when they purchased it from her hand, and in the Gemara (Talmud Ketubot 95a) it raises the question: Could she not say: “I merely wished to oblige my husband (i.e., her purchase was not to be taken seriously)? And it responds, as for example, that the husband sold this field [of his wife] to another man prior to that one, and the wife did not want to endorse it for this one, but she endorsed it for the other, and if it is that it is acting [in a manner that is] obliging to her husband, she would have endorsed the first [sale].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:2)


**פְּשָׁרָה.** בִּצּוּעַ. לֹא כֻלּוֹ לָזֶה וְלֹא כֻלּוֹ לָזֶה. וּלְשׁוֹן פּוֹשְׁרִין הוּא, לֹא חַמִּים וְלֹא קָרִים:


פשרה – adjustment/compromise; not everything to this one, nor everything to that one, and the language of “tepid,” – not hot nor cold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:3)


**וְכֵן בַּעַל חוֹב.** וּשְׁנֵי לָקוֹחוֹת. רְאוּבֵן נוֹשֶׁה בְשִׁמְעוֹן מָנֶה וְלוֹ שְׁתֵּי שָׂדוֹת, וּמְכָרָן לִשְׁנַיִם זוֹ בַּחֲמִשִּׁים וְזוֹ בַּחֲמִשִּׁים, וְכָתַב בַּעַל חוֹב לְלוֹקֵחַ שֵׁנִי דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי עִמָּךְ, בַּעַל חוֹב נוֹטֵל מִיַּד הָרִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר לוֹ הִנַּחְתִּי לְךָ מָקוֹם שֶׁתִּגְבֶּה מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁחוֹבוֹ כְּנֶגֶד שְׁנֵיהֶם, וְלוֹקֵחַ רִאשׁוֹן מוֹצִיא מִיַּד הַשֵּׁנִי, וּבַעַל חוֹב חוֹזֵר וּמוֹצִיא אַף זוֹ מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְלוֹקֵחַ שֵׁנִי מִבַּעַל חוֹב, וְחוֹזְרִין חֲלִילָה עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשׂוּ פְשָׁרָה:


וכן ב"ח – and two mortgaged properties. Reuven has a claim against Shimon for a Maneh, and he has two fields and sold them to two people – to this one for fifty and to that one for fifty, and the creditor wrote to the second purchaser: “I have nothing against you.” The creditor takes from the hand of the first, for he is not able to say to say to him: “I left you a place that you can collect from him, for his debt corresponds to both, and the first purchaser removes from the hand of the second, and the creditor returns and removes even that from the first, and the second purchaser from the creditor and goes back in turn until they make a compromise.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/10:6:4)


**וְכֵן אִשָּׁה בַעֲלַת חוֹב.** שֶׁהָיָה לָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ עַל בַּעְלָהּ וּמָכַר שְׁתֵּי שְׂדוֹתָיו לִשְׁנַיִם וְאֵין בִּשְׁנֵיהֶם אֶלָּא כְּדֵי כְתֻבָּתָהּ, וְכָתְבָה לַשֵּׁנִי דִּין וּדְבָרִים אֵין לִי עִמָּךְ, הָאִשָּׁה מוֹצִיאָה מִיַּד לוֹקֵחַ רִאשׁוֹן, וְהוּא מִן הַשֵּׁנִי, וְהַשֵּׁנִי מִן הָאִשָּׁה [וְהָאִשָּׁה מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן] וְלוֹקֵחַ רִאשׁוֹן מִיַּד הַשֵּׁנִי, וְחוֹזְרִים חֲלִילָה:


וכן אשה בעלת חוב – she had her Ketubah on her husband, and he sold his two fields to two [different] people but neither of the two had other than up to her Ketubah, and she wrote to the second [purchaser]: “I have no claim against you and nothing to do with you.” The woman removes from the first purchaser and he removes it from the hand of the second [and the woman from the first] and the second from the woman and the first purchaser from the hand of the second and it goes back in turn [until they make a compromise].



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:1:1)


**אַלְמָנָה נִזּוֹנֶת מִנִּכְסֵי יְתוֹמִין.** בֵּין קַרְקָעוֹת בֵּין מִטַּלְטְלִין. דִּתְנַאי כְתֻבָּה הוּא, וְאַתְּ תְּהֵא יַתְבָא בְּבֵיתִי וּמִתַּזְּנָא מִנְּכָסַי:


אלה ניזונת מנכסי יתומין – whether land or movables, for it is the condition of the Jewish marriage contract: “And you will dwell in my house and be supported from my property.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:1:2)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִין בִּקְבוּרָתָהּ.** שֶׁהַבַּעַל חַיָּב בִּקְבוּרָתָהּ תַּחַת יְרֻשָּׁתָהּ, וְעַכְשָׁיו שֶׁיּוֹרְשֶׁיהָ גּוֹבִין כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִיּוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל, הֵם יִקְבְּרוּהָ. וּמִמֵּילָא שָׁמְעִינַן שֶׁאִם מֵתָה וְלֹא נִשְׁבְּעָה עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, שֶׁעַכְשָׁיו אֵין יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ גּוֹבִין כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, עַל יוֹרְשֵׁי הַבַּעַל מֻטָּל לְקָבְרָהּ:


ואין חייבין בקבורתה – for the husband is liable in her burial in place of her inheritance, and now those who inherit her collect her Ketubah settlement from those who inherit the husband – and they would bury her and of itself, we learn that if she died, and did not take an oath about her Ketubah, for now, her heirs do not collect her Ketubah, and it is placed upon the husband’s heirs to bury her.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:1)


**בֵּין מִן הָאֵרוּסִין.** שֶׁאֵין לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת וּמוֹכֶרֶת לִכְתֻבָּתָהּ:


בין מן האירוסין – for she has no support/food and sells [it] for her Ketubah settlement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:2)


**בֵּין מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִין.** שֶׁהִיא מוֹכֶרֶת לִמְזוֹנוֹת:


בין מן הנשואין – which she sells for support/food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:3)


**מוֹכֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא בְּבֵית דִּין.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא בְבֵית דִּין מֻמְחִין. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם צְרִיכָה לִמְכֹּר בִּפְנֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בְקִיאִין בְּשׁוּמַת קַרְקַע:


מוכרת שלא בבית דין – meaning to say, that is not in a Jewish court of specialists/experts, but nonetheless, she needs to sell in the presence of three who would be expert in estimation of the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:2:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִין.** שֶׁהִיא מוֹכֶרֶת לִמְזוֹנוֹת, מוֹכֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא בְבֵית דִּין, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לָהּ לִהְיוֹת יוֹשֶׁבֶת וּמִתְעַנָּה עַד שֶׁיִּזְדַּקְקוּ לָהּ בֵּית דִּין. אֲבָל מִן הָאֵרוּסִין שֶׁאֵין מְכִירָתָהּ אֶלָּא לִכְתֻבָּה, אֵינָהּ מוֹכֶרֶת אֶלָּא בְבֵית דִּין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר מן הנשואין – that she sells for food, selling not before a Jewish court for it is impossible to her to be sitting [and suffering] until she they would obligate for her a Jewish court. But from the betrothal, where here sale is not for anything other than the Ketubah, she does not sell it other than in a Jewish court, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:1)


**מָכְרָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ.** מָנֶה מָאתַיִם:


מכרה כתובתה – a Maneh [out of] two hundred,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:2)


**לֹא תִמְכֹּר אֶת הַשְּׁאָר.** הַתּוֹסֶפֶת, אֶלָּא בְבֵית דִּין. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן הִיא דְּאָמַר אֵין מוֹכֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא בְבֵית דִּין אֶלָּא לִמְזוֹנוֹת, וְזוֹ הוֹאִיל וְגָבְתָה מִקְצַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ אֵין לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת:


she cannot sell the rest. The supplement [is not sold] other than in a Jewish court, and our Mishnah is according to Rabbi Shimon who said: she does not sell when not in a Jewish court other than for food, and this, since she has collected part of her Ketubah settlement, she lacks food/support.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים מוֹכֶרֶת הִיא.** לִכְתֻבָּה, אֲפִלּוּ לִפְרָקִים. וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן מוֹכֶרֶת הִיא בֵּינְתַּיִם לִמְזוֹנוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא אִבְּדָה מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגָּבְתָה קְצַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


וחכמים אומרים מוכרת – for the Ketubah even for periods of time and even though this is the case, she sells it in the meanwhile for food/support that she has not lost her support/food and even though she collected part of her Ketubah settlement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:4)


**וְכוֹתֶבֶת.** בִּשְׁטַר הַמְּכִירָה, לִמְזוֹנוֹת מָכַרְתִּי:


וכותבת – in the document of sale that I sold them for food/support.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:3:5)


**וּגְרוּשָׁה לֹא תִמְכֹּר.** לִכְתֻבָּה, אֶלָּא בְבֵית דִּין. דְּטַעֲמָא מַאי אָמוּר רַבָּנָן אַלְמָנָה בֵּין מִן הָאֵרוּסִין מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִין מוֹכֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא בְבֵית דִּין, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין אָדָם רוֹצֶה שֶׁתִּתְבַּזֶּה אִשְׁתּוֹ בְּבֵית דִּין, וְזוֹ הוֹאִיל וּגְרוּשָׁה הִיא לֹא אִכְפַּת לֵיהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים שֶׁאִשָּׁה מוֹכֶרֶת בֵּין לִכְתֻבָּה בֵּין לִמְזוֹנוֹת שֶׁלֹּא בְבֵית דִּין מֻמְחִים, וַאֲפִלּוּ הִתְקַבְּלָה מִקְצַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מוֹכֶרֶת לִמְזוֹנוֹת עַד שֶׁתִּתְקַבֵּל כָּל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. וּכְשֶׁמּוֹכֶרֶת, בֵּין לִכְתֻבָּה בֵּין לִמְזוֹנוֹת, צְרִיכָה שְׁבוּעָה וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה הַכְרָזָה:


וגרושה לא תמכור – her Ketubah other than before a Jewish court. What is the reason? The Rabbis stated that a widow whether from betrothal or from marriage sells not in the presence of a Jewish court because no man wants that his wife should be despised in a Jewish court and this is the case since she is a divorcee and he doesn’t care, but the Halakha is according to the Sages that a woman sells whether for her Ketubah settlement or for food/support not in the presence of the Jewish court of specialists and even if she had received part of her Ketubah settlement, she sells for food/support until she receives all of her Ketubah settlement and when she sells, whether for her Ketubah or for food/support, she needs an oath and does not require announcement.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:1)


**שָׁוֶה מָאתַיִם בְּמָנֶה נִתְקַבְּלָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ.** דְּאָמְרִינַן לָהּ אַתְּ אַפְסַדְתְּ. וְשָׁוֶה מָנֶה בְּמָאתַיִם אַף עַל גַּב דְּהִיא הִרְוִיחָה לֹא מָצֵית לְמֵימַר אֲנָא אַרְוָחֵי, שֶׁהַשּׁוֹלֵחַ שְׁלוּחוֹ לַשּׁוּק לִסְחוֹרָה וְלָקַח בְּזוֹל הַכֹּל לְבַעַל הַמָּעוֹת. וְלֹא שָׁמְעִינַן מֵהָכָא אֶלָּא בְדָבָר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ קִצְבָה, כְּגוֹן קַרְקַע שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לְהִמָּכֵר בְּאֹמֶד זֶה בְּפָחוֹת וְזֶה בְּיָתֵר, אֲבָל דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ קִצְבָה וְקָנָה הַשָּׁלִיחַ בְּפָחוֹת, לֹא אִתְבְּרִירָא לָן דִּינָא מֵהָכָא. וְחָזִינָא לְרַבְּוָתָא דְּאִפְלִיגוּ בָהּ. וּמַסְקָנָא לְפוּם מַאי דְּחָזֵי לָן, שֶׁחוֹלְקִין הַשָּׁלִיחַ וְהַמְשַׁלֵּחַ:


שוה מאתים במנה נתקבלה כתובתה – for we tell her “you have suffered loss/ruin and that which is worth a Maneh [that was sold] for two hundred, even though it gained in value, one cannot say: “I will make a profit for a person who sends his agent to the market to do business and he purchased it cheaply, everything goes to the owner of the money and we don’t hear from here other than regarding a thing that has no limit such as the case of land whose manner is for it to be sold through mere sight/estimate – this one for less and that one for more, but a thing that has a limit and the agent purchased it for less, the law is not made clear to us from this and becomes fit for something remarkable for they argued about it and the conclusion is according to [what] appears to us that the agent and the person sending the agent are arguing about.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:2)


**מִכְרָהּ בָּטֵל.** שֶׁאוֹתוֹ דִינָר אֵין לָהּ רְשׁוּת לִמְכֹּר, נִמְצָא שֶׁכָּל הַמֶּכֶר טָעוּת, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּבַת אַחַת הָיָה:


מכרה בטל – that same Denar, she does not have permission to sell, it is found that the entire sale is in error, for it took place at one time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:3)


**לְעוֹלָם מִכְרָהּ קַיָּם.** וְהִיא תַחֲזִיר אֶת הַדִּינָר לַיּוֹרְשִׁין, דְּמַה הִפְסִידָתָן. עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בָּאוֹנָאָה כְּדֵי שֶׁאִלּוּ לֹא הָיְתָה הָאוֹנָאָה הָיָה מִשְׁתַּיֵּר שָׂדֶה בַּת תִּשְׁעָה קַבִּין, שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר שָׂדֶה:


לעולם מכרה קיים – and she should return the Denar to the inheritors for what loss did she cause them? Until it would be a fraudulent representation in order that if it were no overreaching, the field would remain worth nine Kabim which is the measure of the field,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:4:4)


**וּבְגִנָּה בַּת חֲצִי קַב.** שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר גִּנָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


and in the garden within one-half of Kab which is the measure of a garden. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:5:1)


**אִגֶּרֶת בִּקֹּרֶת.** הַכְרָזָה. שֶׁעַל יְדֵי הַהַכְרָזָה בְּנֵי אָדָם מְבַקְּרִים בָּהּ. וּבְהָא מוֹדֶה תַּנָּא קַמָּא לְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


אגרת בקורת – an announcement that through this announcement, people inspect her and in this, the First Tanna/teacher agrees with Rabban Gamaliel and the Halakha is according to the First Tanna.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:6:1
