[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:1)


**הַשְּׁנִיָּה.** שְׁנִיּוֹת לָעֲרָיוֹת, שֶׁהֵן מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים:


השנייה – incest of second degree which is from the enactments of the Soferim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:2)


**אֵין לָהֶם כְּתֻבָּה.** מָנֶה מָאתַיִם. מְמָאֶנֶת, מִשּׁוּם דְּמֵעַצְמָהּ יוֹצְאָה. שְׁנִיָּה, קְנָסוּהָ רַבָּנָן מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא מַרְגִּילָתוֹ לְנָשְׂאָהּ, שֶׁאֵינָהּ נִפְסֶדֶת כְּלוּם בַּנִּשּׂוּאִין שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָהּ נִפְסֶלֶת בָּהֶן וּוְלָדָהּ כָּשֵׁר. אַיְלוֹנִית, מִשּׁוּם דְּמֶקַּח טָעוּת הוּא:


אין להם כתובה – [worth] a Maneh or two hundred. She refuses because she leaves on her own. The second decree [incest] is fined by the Rabbis because she persuades him to marry her and she doesn’t lose anything in her marriage, for she is not made ritually invalid through them and her offspring is ritually valid. A sterile woman/incapable of conception [does not receive her Ketubah] because it is a business transaction in error.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:3)


**וְלֹא פֵרוֹת.** הַפֵּרוֹת שֶׁאָכַל הַבַּעַל אֵין מוֹצִיאִין מִמֶּנּוּ:


ולא פירות – the usufruct that the husband consumed we don’t remove from him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:4)


**וְלֹא מְזוֹנוֹת.** כְּגוֹן אִם לָוְתָה וְאָכְלָה בְּעוֹדָהּ תַּחְתָּיו וְאַחַר כָּךְ מֵאֲנָה, אֵין הַבַּעַל חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם. אֲבָל חַיָּב בִּמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ בְּעוֹדָהּ תַּחְתָּיו. וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה וְהָאַיְלוֹנִית אֵין לָהֶם מְזוֹנוֹת בְּעוֹדָן תַּחְתָּיו, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם לָווּ וְאָכְלוּ שֶׁאֵין הַבַּעַל חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם:


ולא מזונות – an example such as if she borrowed and consumed while still under him (i.e., his domain) and afterwards, refused [to be married to him], the husband is not liable for her support while she is under him (i.e., in his domain), and the second degree [incest] and the woman incapable of conception/sterile have no support while they are under him and all the more so, if they borrowed and consumed for the husband is not obligated to pay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 11:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/11:6:5)


**וְלֹא בְלָאוֹת.** שֶׁנֶּאֶבְדוּ וּבָלוּ לְגַמְרֵי, בֵּין מִנִּכְסֵי מְלוֹג בֵּין מִנִּכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל, אֵין הַמְמָאֶנֶת יְכוֹלָה לְהוֹצִיא מִיַּד בַּעְלָהּ. אֲבָל בְּלָאוֹתֶיהָ הַקַּיָּמִים נוֹטֶלֶת לְעוֹלָם, בֵּין הַמְמָאֶנֶת בֵּין הַשְּׁנִיָּה בֵּין הָאַיְלוֹנִית, וַאֲפִלּוּ זִנְּתָה לֹא הִפְסִידָה בְּלָאוֹתֶיהָ הַקַּיָּמִים. וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה אֵין לָהּ בְּלָאוֹת שֶׁל נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג, אֲבָל בְּלָאוֹת שֶׁל נִכְסֵי צֹאן בַּרְזֶל יֶשׁ לָהּ:


ולא בלאות – that were lost or worn out completely whether from the usufruct (the wife’s estate of which the husband has the fruition without responsibility for loss or deterioration) or from the wife’s estate held by her husband, which in case of her death or divorce, he must restore in specie, being responsible with all his landed property for loss or deterioration (i.e. mort-main), the woman who refuses to marry her husband cannot remove from them from the hand of her husband but the worn-out garments (or the woman’s right to claim compensation for the wear or ruin of the things which she brought along as her property, whether she refuses him in marriage or is second-degree of incest or is a sterile woman, and even if she ran about as a prostitute, she does not lose her worn-out garments/claim for compensation for the wear or ruin of things which she brought along as her property, but the woman charged with second-degree incest has no claim for compensation for the wear or ruin of things which she brought along as her property of the usufruct, but she does have the compensation for wear or ruin of things of the mort-main.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:1)


**הַנּוֹשֵׂא אֶת הָאִשָּׁה וּפָסְקָה עִמּוֹ.** בִּשְׁטָר וְקִנְיָן, אוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְעֵדִים אַתֶּם עֵדַי שֶׁאֲנִי מְחַיֵּב עַצְמִי לָזוּן אֶת בִּתָּהּ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לָהּ מֵאִישׁ אַחֵר:


הנושא את האשה ופסקה עמו – in the document and symbolic form of making an agreement binding, or he (i.e., the husband) aid to witnesses: “you are my witnesses that I obligate myself to support her (i.e., my wife-to-be’s) daughter that she has from another man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:2)


**לִכְשֶׁתָּבֹא אֶצְלִי.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם הָיִיתִי מְקַיֵּם אֶת אִמָּהּ הָיִיתִי זָנָהּ:


לכשתבא אצלי – meaning to say, if I would sustain her mother, I would feed her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:1:3)


**מוֹלִיךְ לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ לִמְקוֹם שֶׁאִמָּהּ שָׁם.** דְּקַיְמָא לָן בַּת אֵצֶל אִמָּהּ בֵּין גְּדוֹלָה בֵּין קְטַנָּה כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהָאֵם רוֹצָה, וְהַבֵּן עַד שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים אֵצֶל אִמּוֹ, וְחַיָּב הָאָב לְפַרְנְסוֹ וְהוּא אֵצֶל אִמּוֹ אִם הָאֵם רוֹצָה:


מוליך לה מזונותיה למקום שאמה שם – for we hold that a daughter is with her mother whether as an adult or as a child – all the time that the mother wants. And the son, until six years with his mother, and the father is obligated to provide support and he is with his mother, if the mother wants.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:1)


**בְּנוֹתֵיהֶן נִזּוֹנוֹת מִנְּכָסִים בְּנֵי חוֹרִין.** וְלֹא מִמְּשֻׁעְבָּדִים, שֶׁאֵין מוֹצִיאִים לִמְזוֹן הָאִשָּׁה וְהַבָּנוֹת מִנְּכָסִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים, מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁאֵין הַלָּקוֹחוֹת יוֹדְעִים כַּמָּה יַעֲלוּ הַמְּזוֹנוֹת כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלִיזָהֲרוּ לְהַנִּיחַ לָהֶם מָקוֹם לִגְבּוֹת:


בנותיהן ניזונות מנכסים בני חורין – but not mortgaged [properties] for we do not release for the support of the wife and the daughters from mortgaged properties because of the welfare of the world, for those who bought the property do not know how much food provisions will cost so that they would beware to leave them a place from which to collect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:2)


**וְהִיא נִזּוֹנֶת.** אוֹתָהּ הַבַּת:


והיא ניזונת – that same daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:3)


**שֶׁהִיא כְבַעֲלַת חוֹב.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ עֲלֵיהֶן שְׁטַר מְזוֹנוֹת:


שהיא כבעלת חוב – for she has upon them a document of support (i.e., food).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:2:4)


**כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאַתְּ עִמִּי.** וְלֹא אִם אָמוּת אוֹ תָמוּתִי אוֹ אֲגָרְשֵׁךְ:


כל זמן שאת עמי – and not if I die or if you die or I will divorce you.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:3:1)


**וְנוֹתְנִין לָהּ מָדוֹר.** וְכֵן כְּלֵי תַשְׁמִישׁ וַעֲבָדִים וּשְׁפָחוֹת שֶׁהָיְתָה מִשְׁתַּמֶּשֶׁת בָּהֶם בְּחַיֵּי בַעְלָהּ. וְאִם נָפַל הַבַּיִת אֵין חַיָּבִים לִבְנוֹת לָהּ בַּיִת אַחֵר, דְּאַתְּ תְּהֵא יַתְבָא בְּבֵיתִי תְּנַן. וַאֲפִלּוּ רָצְתָה לִבְנוֹתוֹ מִמָּמוֹנָהּ אֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לָהּ:


ונותנין לה מדור – and similarly, utensils, male servants and maid servants that she would use them during the lifetime of her husband and if the house fell, they don’t have to build her another house, for it is taught in the Mishnah: “you will dwell in my house,” and even if she wanted to build it from her from her own funds, we don’t listen do her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:3:2)


**אִם אַתְּ אֶצְלֵנוּ יֶשׁ לִיךְ מְזוֹנוֹת.** שֶׁבִּרְכַּת הַבַּיִת בְּרֻבָּהּ:


אם את אצלנו יש ליך מזונות – that the blessing of the house is plentiful.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:1)


**כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא בְבֵית אָבִיהָ.** וְהַיּוֹרְשִׁים נָתְנוּ לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ שָׁם:


כל זמן שהיא בבית אביה – and the heirs gave to support there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:2)


**גּוֹבָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ.** כְּשֶׁתִּרְצֶה:


גובה כתובתה – when she desires.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:3)


**שֶׁתַּעֲשֶׂה טוֹבָה.** מִנִּכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים, נוֹתֶנֶת לֶחֶם וּמֶלַח לִשְׁכֵנוֹתֶיהָ כְּדֵי כְתֻבָּתָהּ:


שתעשה טובה – from the property of orphans, she gives bread and salt to her neighbors up to [the value of] her Ketubah settlement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** לֹא הֻזְכְּרוּ חָמֵשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה לְהַפְסִידָהּ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ בִּשְׁבִיל הַטּוֹבָה שֶׁתַּעֲשֶׂה מִנִּכְסֵי הַיְתוֹמִים, אֶלָּא לְעִנְיַן הַמְּחִילָה הֻזְכְּרוּ, דְּהוֹאִיל וְשָׁתְקָה וְלֹא תָבְעָה כָּל הַשָּׁנִים הַלָּלוּ מָחֲלָה, הִלְכָּךְ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא בְבֵית בַּעְלָהּ אֵין שְׁתִיקָתָהּ מְחִילָה, שֶׁמִּפְּנֵי שֶׁמְּכַבְּדִים אוֹתָהּ הִיא בּוֹשָׁה לִתְבֹּעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, אֲבָל בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ מִשֶּׁשָּׁתְקָה עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים מְחִילָה הִיא:


וחכמים אומרים – they did not mention twenty five years to cause her to lose her Ketubah settlement because of the good that she will do from the property of the orphans, but rather, concerning the issue of renunciation, it was mentioned by them that since she was silent and had not claimed [her Ketubah settlement] all these years, she renounced [it], therefore, all the time that she is in the house of her husband, her silence is renunciation since because they honor her, she is ashamed to claim her Ketubah settlement; but in her father’s house, since she had been silent for twenty-five years, it is a renunciation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 12:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/12:4:5)


**מֵתָה, יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ מַזְכִּירִין כְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר צְרִיכִין לִתְבֹּעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ בְּתוֹךְ עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁנִּשְׁבְּעָה עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁמֵּתָה הוּא דְיוֹרְשֶׁיהָ יְכוֹלִים לִתְבֹּעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נִשְׁבְּעָה עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, אֵין יוֹרְשֶׁיהָ יוֹרְשִׁים מִכְּתֻבָּתָהּ כְּלוּם, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מוֹרִישׁ שְׁבוּעָה לְבָנָיו:


מתה יורשיה מזכירין כתובתה – meaning to say, they need to claim her Ketubah settlement within twenty-five years and especially when she took an oath on her Ketubah prior to her passing. This is the case where her heirs are able to claim her Ketubah settlement, but if she had not taken an oath regarding her Ketubah, her heirs do not inherit from her Ketubah anything, and no one bequeaths an oath to his children.



## Chapter 13


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:1)


**שְׁנֵי דַיָּנֵי גְזֵלוֹת.** שֶׁהָיוּ גוֹזְרִין גְּזֵרוֹת עַל הַגַּזְלָנִים וְקוֹנְסִים עֲלֵיהֶם קְנָסוֹת:


שני דייני גזילות (note that the opening words of this comment are different from what is written in the Mishnah, and seem to reflect a different manuscript in the hands of Rabbi Obadiah of Bertinoro) – they were making decrees regarding thieves and imposing fines upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:2)


**שְׁנֵי דְבָרִים.** שֶׁאֵין חֲכָמִים מוֹדִים לוֹ:


שני דברים – that the Sages do not agree with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:3)


**תִּשָּׁבַע בַּסּוֹף.** כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ בוֹ שֶׁמֵּת וְתָבֹא לִגְבּוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, תִּשָּׁבַע שֶׁלֹּא עִכְּבָה בְיָדָהּ מִשֶּׁל בַּעְלָהּ כְּלוּם:


תשבע בסוף – when they heard regarding him that he (i.e., the husband) had died, and she (i.e., the wife, now the widow) comes to collect her Ketubah, she takes an oath that she has not held back any property of her husband at all (after her husband’s death).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:4)


**וְלֹא תִשָּׁבַע בַּתְּחִלָּה.** בִּשְׁעַת גְּבִיַּת מְזוֹנוֹת. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, תִּשָּׁבַע בַּסּוֹף, כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא בַּעְלָהּ וְיַכְחִישֶׁנָּה וְיֹאמַר הִנַּחְתִּי לָךְ מְזוֹנוֹת, תִּשָּׁבַע שֶׁלֹּא הִנִּיחַ לָהּ כְּלוּם:


ולא תשבע בתחלה – at the time of collection of support. But Maimonides explained that she should take an oath at the end when her husband would come and contradict her and say, “I left you support,” she should take an oath claiming that he had not left her anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:1:5)


**רַבָּן יוֹחָנָן בֶּן זַכַּאי אוֹמֵר.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ. וְדַוְקָא אַחַר שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים לִיצִיאַת הַבַּעַל פּוֹסְקִין מְזוֹנוֹת לָאִשָּׁה אִם תָּבְעָה מְזוֹנוֹת, אֲבָל קֹדֶם זְמַן זֶה לֹא, שֶׁחֲזָקָה אֵין אָדָם מַנִּיחַ בֵּיתוֹ רֵיקָן וְיוֹצֵא:


רבי יוחנן בן זכאי אומר – and the Halakha is according to him, and especially after three months from the husband departing, they provide/assign support to the wife if she claimed support, but not prior to this time, for it is a legal presumption that a man does not leave his house empty and leaves.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:2:1)


**אִבֵּד אֶת מְעוֹתָיו.** דְּאָמַר, לֹא אָמַרְתִּי לְךָ הַלְוֵנִי וַאֲנִי אֶפְרַע. אֲבָל אִם הִלְוָה אֶת הָאִשָּׁה מָעוֹת לִמְזוֹנוֹתֶיהָ עַל מְנָת שֶׁתְּשַׁלֵּם לוֹ, הוּא תוֹבְעָהּ וְהִיא תוֹבַעַת אֶת הַבַּעַל וִישַׁלֵּם. וְאִם הַבַּעַל טוֹעֵן הִנַּחְתִּי לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת, וְהִיא אוֹמֶרֶת לֹא הִנִּיחַ וְרוֹצָה לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנּוּ, נִשְׁבַּע הַבַּעַל שְׁבוּעַת הֶסֵּת וְנִפְטָר, וְיִהְיוּ הַמָּעוֹת חוֹב עָלֶיהָ לִכְשֶׁתִּתְאַלְמֵן אוֹ תִתְגָּרֵשׁ. וּבְהָא מַתְנִיתִין נַמִּי הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן יוֹחָנָן בֶּן זַכַּאי:


אבד את מעותיו – that he said, “I did not say to you, lend me and I will pay it back,” but if he lent the woman money for her support/maintenance on the condition that she should pay him, he makes a claim [against her] and she makes a claim the husband and he pays. But if the husband claims, “I left her support,” and she states, “he did not leave me, and wants to remove [money] from him, the husband takes an oath of inducement (an oath instituted by the Sages where the defendant completely denies a claim, based upon the tradition of Rav Nahman – to clear himself of suspicion), and he is exempted, and the moneys would be liability upon her when she becomes a widow or a divorcee, and in this Mishnah also, the Halakha is according to Rabban Yohanan ben Zakkai.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:3:1)


**וּבִנְכָסִים מֻעָטִים.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם פַּרְנָסָה שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ לִזְכָרִים וְלִנְקֵבוֹת. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ, כָּל שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם לְפַרְנֵס הַבָּנִים וְהַבָּנוֹת עַד שֶׁיִּבָּגְרוּ הַבָּנוֹת, קָרוּי נְכָסִים מֻעָטִים:


בנכסים מועטים – that they lack support for twelve months for males and females. And Maimonides explained that everyone who lack the means to support the boys and girls until the girls become adults is called meager resources/a small amount of property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:3:2)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאֲנִי זָכָר הִפְסָדְתִּי.** כְּלוֹמַר, בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאֲנִי זָכָר וְרָאוּי לִירָשׁ בִּנְכָסִים מְרֻבִּים הִפְסַדְתִּי בִּנְכָסִים מֻעָטִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַדְמוֹן:


בשביל שאני זכר הפסדתי – meaning to say, with the fact that I am a male and designated/predestined to inherit with a great deal of property, I have lost out when there is a small amount of property/meager resources. But the Halakha is not according to Admon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:1)


**וְהוֹדָה בַקַּנְקַנִּים.** רֵיקִים בְּלֹא שֶׁמֶן. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁטְּעָנוֹ חֲבֵרוֹ עֲשָׂרָה כַדֵּי שֶׁמֶן יֵשׁ לִי אֶצְלְךָ:


והודה בקנקנים – empty [jars] without oil as for example where his fellow made a claim against him for ten pitchers of oil that I have that are with you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:2)


**אַדְמוֹן אוֹמֵר.** יֵשׁ בְּמַשְׁמָעוּת טַעֲנָה זוֹ שֶׁמֶן וְכַדִּים, וּכְשֶׁהוֹדָה לוֹ בְּכַדִּים רֵיקִים הֲוֵי הוֹדָאָה בְמִקְצָת וְחַיָּב שְׁבוּעָה:


Admon states that there is an implication in this claim [for] oil and pitchers, and when he admitted to him about the empty pitchers, that is a partial admission of guilt and he requires taking an oath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:4:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים.** אֵין בְּמַשְׁמָעוּת טַעֲנָה זוֹ אֶלָּא שֶׁמֶן בִּלְבָד שִׁעוּר עֲשָׂרָה כַדִּים, וּכְשֶׁהוֹדָה לוֹ בְּכַדִּים רֵיקִים, מַה שֶּׁטְּעָנוֹ לֹא הוֹדָה לוֹ וּמַה שֶּׁהוֹדָה לוֹ לֹא טְעָנוֹ, וְאֵין כָּאן הוֹדָאָה בְמִקְצָת מִמִּין הַטַּעֲנָה וְאֵינוֹ חַיָּב שְׁבוּעָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּאַדְמוֹן:


But the Sages state that there is no implication in this claim other than the oil alone which is the measure of ten pitchers, and when he admitted to him regarding the empty pitchers, what he claimed against him was not admitted to him, and what he admitted to him was not claimed against him, and there is no partial admission from the kind of a claim and he is not liable to take an oath, but the Halakha is according to Admon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:1)


**וּפָשַׁט לוֹ אֶת הָרֶגֶל.** לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן, כְּלוֹמַר טֹל טִיט וְאָבָק שֶׁתַּחַת רַגְלַי. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, תְּלֵה אוֹתִי בְרַגְלִי עַל הָעֵץ, שֶׁאֵין לִי מַה לִּתֵּן לְךָ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ וּפָשַׁט לוֹ אֶת הָרֶגֶל, שֶׁהָלַךְ בַּדֶּרֶךְ מֵרָחוֹק:


ופשט את הרגל – it is the language of contempt/disgrace, meaning to say, “take the plaster and dust that is under my feet. Another explanation: “Suspend me by my feet on the tree, for I lack what I can give to you” (i.e., he defaulted). And Maimonides explained, “and he shows him the foot”/ופשט את הרגל (i.e., refuses to give it to him), that he walked on the road from afar.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:2)


**עַד שֶׁתַּלְבִּין רֹאשָׁהּ.** עַד שֶׁתַּזְקִין. וְאֵין כּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ לֹא לִכְנֹס וְלֹא לִפְטֹר:


עד שתלבין ראשה – until she gets old and we don’t force him neither to marry [her] or to set her free.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:5:3)


**אַדְמוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּאַדְמוֹן:



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:1)


**הָעוֹרֵר עַל הַשָּׂדֶה וְכוּ'.** רְאוּבֵן מְעַרְעֵר עַל הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁבְּיַד שִׁמְעוֹן וְאוֹמֵר לוֹ, לֵוִי שֶׁמָּכַר לְךָ גְּזָלָהּ מִמֶּנִּי, וְהוּא חָתוּם עַל שְׁטַר הַמְּכִירָה שֶׁכָּתַב לֵוִי לְשִׁמְעוֹן שֶׁמָּכַר לוֹ:


העורר על השדה וכו' – Reuven protests concerning the field that is in the hand of Shimon, and says to him: “Levi who sold it to you stole it from me, and he is inscribed on the bill of sale that Levi wrote to Shimon who sold it to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:2)


**אַדְמוֹן אוֹמֵר יָכוֹל הוּא שֶׁיֹּאמַר.** מַה שֶּׁלֹּא עִרְעַרְתִּי בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁלָּקַחְתָּ שָׂדֶה זוֹ מִלֵּוִי וְחָתַמְתִּי עֵד בְּתוֹךְ הַשְּׁטָר, לְפִי שֶׁלֵּוִי אָדָם חָזָק וְקָשֶׁה לְהוֹצִיאָהּ מִיָּדוֹ וְנֹחַ לִי שֶׁתְּהֵא בְּיָדְךָ שֶׁאוֹצִיאֶנָּה מִמְּךָ בְּדִין:


אדמון אומר יכול הוא שיאמר – just as I did not protest at the time when you purchased this field from Levi and I signed/inscribed as a witness in the midst of the document because Levi is a strong man and difficult to remove something from his hand, it was pleasant for me that it should be in your hand than that I would [try] to remove it from you in court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:6:3)


**אִבֵּד אֶת זְכוּתוֹ.** דְּהוֹאִיל וְחָתַם הוֹדָה שֶׁאֵין לוֹ עֵסֶק בָּהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאַדְמוֹן. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁחָתַם בָּהּ בְּעֵד הוּא דִפְלִיגֵי אַדְמוֹן וַחֲכָמִים, אֲבָל אִם הוּא חָתוּם בְּדַיָּן לְקַיֵּם אֶת הַשְּׁטָר, הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁלֹּא אִבֵּד אֶת זְכוּתוֹ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיָּכוֹל לוֹמַר לֹא הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּשְּׁטָר, שֶׁהַדַּיָּן הַחוֹתֵם עַל הַהֶנְפֵּק לְקַיֵּם אֶת הַשְּׁטָר אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֵּדַע מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּשְּׁטָר אֶלָּא שֶׁיַּכִּיר חֲתִימַת הָעֵדִים בִּלְבָד:


אבד את זכותו – for since and he signed, he admitted that he had no business with this, but the Halakha is not like Admon, and especially when he signed it as a witness, this is what Admon and the Sages were arguing about, but if he is signed as a judge to uphold the document, everyone agrees that he did not lose his right because he would be able to say: “I did not know what was written in the document for the judge who signs as the legal endorsement of a note, stating that it has been produced in court and found valid – to uphold the document and it is not necessary that he knew what was written in the document other than that he should recognize the signatures alone.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:7:1)


**וְאָבְדָה דֶרֶךְ שָׂדֵהוּ.** שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ בָהּ בַּעֲלֵי הַשָּׂדוֹת שֶׁבִּמְצָרָיו:


ואבדה דרך שדהו – that the owners of the field who took possession of it that was in his narrow path marking the boundary between fields.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:7:2)


**יֵלֵךְ לוֹ בַּקְּצָרָה.** עַל כָּרְחָם יִטֹּל לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ לְשָׂדֵהוּ, אֲבָל יִבְרֹר לוֹ דֶּרֶךְ קְצָרָה שֶׁלֹּא יַרְבֶּה לִטֹּל. וּבִזְמַן שֶׁאַרְבָּעָה בְנֵי אָדָם מַקִּיפִים אוֹתוֹ מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת מוֹדֶה אַדְמוֹן לַחֲכָמִים שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד יָכוֹל לוֹמַר אַיְתֵי רְאָיָה דְּדַרְכָּךְ גַּבַּאי וּשְׁקוֹל. וְאִם אָדָם אֶחָד הִקִּיפָהוּ מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת מוֹדִים חֲכָמִים לְאַדְמוֹן דְּמִמָּה נַפְשָׁךְ אוֹרְחֵיהּ גַּבֵּיהּ הוּא. לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁהִקִּיפָהוּ אָדָם אֶחָד שֶׁבָּא מִכֹּחַ אַרְבָּעָה בְנֵי אָדָם. אַדְמוֹן אוֹמֵר, מָצֵי אָמַר לֵיהּ מִמָּה נַפְשָׁךְ אוֹרְחִי גַבָּךְ, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים מָצֵי אָמַר לֵיהּ הִיאַךְ, אִי שָׁתַּקְתָּ שָׁתַּקְתָּ, וְאִי לֹא, מְהַדַּרְנָא שְׁטָרָא לְמָרַיְהוּ וְלֹא מָצֵית לְאִשְׁתַּעוֹיֵי דִינָא בַּהֲדַיְהוּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ילך לו בקצרה – by force against their will, he should take for himself the path to his field, but he should choose for himself the shortest path so that he would not have to take a lot. And at the time when people surround it from the four directions Admon agrees with the Sages that each person can say, “I brought proof that your path is [where the money was] collected and taken. But if one person surrounded it from the four directions, the Sages agree that whatever side you take, his path is with him. They did not dispute other than when one person surrounded it when he came under the power of four people. Admon states that one can say to him, whatever side you take, my path is with you. But the Sages say that one can say to him: How? If you were silent, you were silent, but if not, return the document to his master, and you will not be able to talk about the law with/to sue him. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:8:1)


**וְהַלָּה הוֹצִיא שֶׁמָּכַר לוֹ אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה.** הַלֹּוֶה הוֹצִיא עָלָיו שְׁטַר מְכִירָה מְאֻחֶרֶת לִשְׁטַר הַהַלְוָאָה, וְאוֹמֵר שְׁטָרְךָ מְזֻיָּף אוֹ פָרוּעַ, שֶׁאִם הָיִיתִי חַיָּב לְךָ לֹא הָיִיתָ מוֹכֵר לִי אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה, שֶׁהָיָה לְךָ לִגְבּוֹת חוֹבְךָ:


והלה הוציא שמכר לו את השדה – the borrower brought out against him the latter bill of sale to the loan document and stats that your document is forged, or it is paid off , for if I had been liable to you, you would not have sold me the field that was yours to collect your lien.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:8:2)


**זֶה הָיָה פִקֵּחַ שֶׁמָּכַר לוֹ אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה זֶה מַבְרִיחַ מִטַּלְטְלָיו, וְלֹא הָיָה לוֹ מֵהֵיכָן יְמַשְׁכְּנֶנּוּ עַל חוֹבוֹ, וְעַכְשָׁיו יִטֹּל אֶת הַקַּרְקַע. וּבְאַתְרָא דְהַלּוֹקֵחַ יָהֵיב זוּזֵי וַהֲדַר כָּתְבֵי שְׁטַר מְכִירָה כֻּלֵּי עַלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי שֶׁהָיָה לַמּוֹכֵר לְעַכֵּב בְּחוֹבוֹ הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁקִּבֵּל וְלֹא יִכְתֹּב לוֹ הַשְּׁטָר, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁכְּתָבוֹ הוֹכִיחַ שֶׁאֵין לוֹ עָלָיו חוֹב. כִּי פְלִיגֵי בְּאַתְרָא דְכָתְבֵי שְׁטָרָא וַהֲדַר יָהֵיב הַלּוֹקֵחַ זוּזֵי. אַדְמוֹן סָבַר, אִיבָּעֵי לֵיהּ לְמִמְסָר מוֹדָעָה אֵינִי מוֹכֵר לוֹ אֶלָּא כְּדֵי שֶׁאוּכַל לְמַשְׁכְּנוֹ. וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים, הַאי דְּלֹא מָסַר מוֹדָעָא כִּי יָרֵא שֶׁמָּא יִשְׁמַע הַדָּבָר וְיִמָּנַע מִלִּקְנוֹת אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


זה היה פקח שמכר לו את השדה – because this one abandoned his movable property, and he did not have from where he could he could mortgage on his lien and now he takes the property. Bu in a place where the purchaser gives Zuzim/money and afterwards the writing of a bill/document of sale, everyone does not disagree that the seller should have tarried with his lien of the money that received and should not write for him the document and since he wrote it, it proves that he does not have a lien upon him, but they argue in a place where they wrote the document and afterwards the purchaser gives the Zuzim/money. Admon holds that he should have sent a declaration (especially a protest before witnesses against a forced or unduly influenced action): “I will not see you other than in order that I am able to mortgage it.” But the Sages state: the fact that he did not send a declaration because he was afraid lest the matter should become known and he would have been prevented from purchasing the field, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:9:1)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים זֶה גוֹבֶה שְׁטַר חוֹבוֹ.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א זה גובה שטר חובו – And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת לַנִּשּׂוּאִין.** שֶׁאִם נָשָׂא אִשָּׁה בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְכוֹפָהּ לָצֵאת אַחֲרָיו מֵאֶרֶץ אֶל אֶרֶץ:


שלש ארצות לנשואין – if a person married a woman in one of these [lands], he cannot force her to go out after him one country/land to another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:2)


**כְּרַךְ.** גָּדוֹל מֵעִיר. וְהוּא מְקוֹם שְׁוָקִים, וּמִכָּל סְבִיבָיו בָּאִין שָׁם לִסְחוֹרָה, וְכָל דָּבָר מָצוּי בוֹ:


כרך – larger than a city, and it is a place of markets, and from everywhere around it, [people] come there for business and for all things that are found in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא מֵעִיר לִכְרַךְ.** שֶׁיְּשִׁיבַת הַכְּרַכִּין קָשָׁה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַכֹּל מִתְיַשְּׁבִים שָׁם וְדוֹחֲקִים וּמְקָרְבִים הַבָּתִּים זוֹ לְזוֹ וְאֵין שָׁם אֲוִיר:


אבל לא מעיר לעיר – for dwelling in the cities is hard for everyone resides there and crowds and the houses are close to one another and there is no air.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:4)


**וְלֹא מִכְּרַךְ לְעִיר.** דְּבַכְּרַךְ שְׁכִיחֵי כָּל מִילֵי, בָּעִיר לֹא שְׁכִיחֵי כָּל מִילֵי:


ולא מכרך לעיר – since in the [big] city, is found all kinds of things; in the town, all sorts of things are not found.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:10:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַנָּוֶה הַיָּפָה בוֹדֵק.** אֶת הַגּוּף וּמַחֲלִיאוֹ, שֶׁשִּׁנּוּי וֶסֶת אֲפִלּוּ לְטוֹבָה תְּחִלַּת חֹלִי מֵעַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל. וְאִם אִישׁ מֵאֶרֶץ הַגָּלִיל נָשָׂא אִשָּׁה בְּאֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה אוֹ אִפְּכָא, כּוֹפִין אוֹתָהּ לָצֵאת עִמּוֹ, שֶׁעַל מְנָת כֵּן נְשָׂאָהּ. וּבְכָל מָקוֹם מוֹצִיאִים מֵעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים לְעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל, אֲבָל לֹא מֵעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים:


מפני שהנוה היפה בודק – the body and makes it sick. For the change in the period, even for [her] good is the beginning of stomach illness. But the Halakha is according to Rabban Gamaliel. But if a person from the land of the Galilee married a woman from the land of Judea or its opposite, we force her to go out with him, for it is on that account that he married her. And in every place, we remove [someone] from a city where most of it is idolatrous to a city where the majority is Israelite, but not from a city where its majority is Israelite to a city where its majority is idolatrous.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:1)


**הַכֹּל מַעֲלִין.** אֶת כְּל בְּנֵי בֵיתוֹ אָדָם כּוֹפֶה לַעֲלוֹת עִמּוֹ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, וַאֲפִלּוּ אִם קָנָה עֶבֶד עִבְרִי יֵלֵךְ הָעֶבֶד אַחֲרָיו עַל כָּרְחוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ מִנָּוֶה הַיָּפֶה לְנָוֶה הָרַע, וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעִיר שֶׁרֻבָּהּ עוֹבְדֵי כוֹכָבִים:


הכל מעלין – a person may force all the members of his household to make Aliyah with him to Jerusalem, and even if he purchased a Hebrew slave, that slave should go after him against his will, and even from a nice neighborhood to a bad neighborhood, and even from a city whose majority was Israelite to a city whose majority was idolatrous.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:2)


**וְאֵין הַכֹּל מוֹצִיאִין.** וְאֵין מוֹצִיאִין שׁוּם אָדָם:


ואין הכל מוציאין – and we do not remove any individual [from Jerusalem].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:3)


**אֶחָד הָאֲנָשִׁים וְאֶחָד הַנָּשִׁים.** אִם הוּא אוֹמֵר לַעֲלוֹת וְהִיא אוֹמֶרֶת שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲלוֹת, תֵּצֵא בְּלֹא כְתֻבָּה. וְאִם הִיא אוֹמֶרֶת לַעֲלוֹת וְהוּא אוֹמֵר שֶׁלֹּא לַעֲלוֹת, יוֹצִיא וְיִתֵּן כְּתֻבָּה:


אחד האנשים ואחד הנשים – If he (i.e., the husband) says to make Aliyah and she says that she doesn’t make Aliyah, she should be divorced without her Ketubah [monies], but if she states that [that she wants to make] Aliyah and he he says not to make Aliyah, he should divorce her and give her her Ketubah [monies].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Ketubot 13:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Ketubot/r/13:11:4)


**קַפּוֹטְקִיָּא.** הִיא כַּפְתּוֹר. וְקוֹרִין לָהּ בַּעֲרָבִי דמא"ת. וּמְעוֹתֶיהָ גְּדוֹלוֹת וְשׁוֹקְלוֹת יוֹתֵר מִשֶּׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וּמִשּׁוּם דִּכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, הֵקֵלּוּ בָהּ לִפְרֹעַ אוֹתָהּ בַּפָּחוּת שֶׁבַּמָּעוֹת. וְרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל סָבַר כְּתֻבָּה דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי אָזֵיל בָּהּ לְחֻמְרָא. וְלֵית הִלְכָתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


קפוטקיא – this is Caphtor, and we call it Dima’t in Arabic. Its monies are large and weigh more than tose of the land of Israel. But because the Ketubah/marriage contract of a woman is from the words of the Scribes, they were lenient concerning it to pay her off with the least [amount of] money. But Rabbi Shimon ben Gamaliel holds that the Ketubah is from the Torah, and because of this, he goes according to the stricter position in this. But, the Halakah is not according to him.
